+# Design Document
+
+## Overview
+The `admin-product-variants` feature implements administrative capabilities for defining multi-attribute option groups (e.g. Color, Storage) and managing the product variant matrix (price, inventory, active status per variant combination). It extends backend administrative product APIs (`POST /api/v1/admin/products`, `PUT /api/v1/admin/products/:id`, `GET /api/v1/admin/products/:id`) and enhances the admin frontend form (`ProductFormPage`) with an intuitive Option Group builder and Variant Matrix editor.
+
+Addresses requirements: **Req 1 (1.1-1.4), Req 2 (2.1-2.5), Req 3 (3.1-3.4), Req 4 (4.1-4.3), Req 5 (5.1-5.4)**.
+
+## Architecture
+
+### Affected Boundaries and Data Flow
+```
++-------------------------------------------------------------+
+|                 frontend-admin (React 19)                   |
+|  - ProductFormPage: Simple vs Multi-Variant Mode Toggle     |
+|  - OptionGroupBuilder: Dynamic Groups & Values Inputs       |
+|  - VariantMatrixTable: Cartesian Product Grid (Price/Stock) |
++-------------------------------------------------------------+
+                              |
+                              | HTTP JSON /api/v1/admin/products
+                              v
++-------------------------------------------------------------+
+|                 backend (Go + Gin Delivery)                 |
+|  - AdminCatalogHandler: Request DTO parsing & validation    |
+|  - CatalogUseCase: Business validation & range calculation  |
+|  - CatalogRepository: Atomic Transactional Persistence       |
++-------------------------------------------------------------+
+                              |
+                              | SQL / pgxpool
+                              v
++-------------------------------------------------------------+
+|                  PostgreSQL Database Layer                  |
+|  - products (id, name, model, brand, category_id, etc.)     |
+|  - product_images (product_id, url, position)               |
+|  - product_option_groups (id, product_id, label, position)  |
+|  - product_option_values (id, product_id, group_id, label)  |
+|  - product_variants (id, product_id, price_vnd, stock, ...) |
+|  - variant_option_values (variant_id, group_id, value_id)   |
++-------------------------------------------------------------+
+```
+
+## Components And Interfaces
+
+### 1. Administrative API Wire Contracts
+
+#### `SaveProductRequest` (Admin Create / Update payload)
+When creating new option groups and values, the frontend generates standard client-side UUIDs (`crypto.randomUUID()`) for `id` fields (or preserves existing database UUIDs when editing). The backend accepts and persists these UUIDs directly, ensuring `VariantInput.optionValueIds` deterministically maps `groupId -> optionValueId`.
+
+```typescript
+interface OptionValueInput {
+  id: string;                             // Client-generated UUID for new values, or existing UUID
+  label: string;
+  position?: number;
+}
+
+interface OptionGroupInput {
+  id: string;                             // Client-generated UUID for new groups, or existing UUID
+  label: string;
+  position?: number;
+  values: OptionValueInput[];
+}
+
+interface VariantInput {
+  id?: string;                            // Client-generated UUID or existing UUID
+  optionValueIds: Record<string, string>; // group_id -> option_value_id
+  priceVND: string;                       // VND integer string, e.g. "4990000"
+  stockQuantity: number;                  // non-negative integer
+  active: boolean;
+}
+
+interface SaveProductRequest {
+  name: string;
+  model: string;
+  brand: string;
+  description: string;
+  categoryId: string;
+  price: string;                          // Base price (or minPrice when hasVariants)
+  currency: string;                       // "VND"
+  stockQuantity: number;                  // Total stock (or sum of active variant stock when hasVariants)
+  featured: boolean;
+  imageUrls: string[];
+  hasVariants?: boolean;
+  optionGroups?: OptionGroupInput[];
+  variants?: VariantInput[];
+}
+```
+
+#### `ProductDetailResponse` (Admin and Storefront detail payload)
+Returns existing domain `ProductDetail` enriched with `optionGroups` and `variants`:
+```typescript
+interface ProductDetail {
+  id: string;
+  name: string;
+  model: string;
+  brand: string;
+  description: string;
+  category: { id: string; name: string };
+  featured: boolean;
+  active: boolean;
+  minPriceVND: string;
+  maxPriceVND: string;
+  price: string;
+  currency: string;
+  stockQuantity: number;
+  imageUrls: string[];
+  optionGroups: {
+    id: string;
+    label: string;
+    position: number;
+    values: { id: string; label: string }[];
+  }[];
+  variants: {
+    id: string;
+    optionValueIds: Record<string, string>;
+    priceVND: string;
+    stockQuantity: number;
+    active: boolean;
+  }[];
+}
+```
+
+### 2. Frontend Admin Components (`frontend-admin`)
+- **`ProductFormPage.tsx`**: Adds a toggle switch between "Sản phẩm đơn giản" and "Sản phẩm có biến thể".
+  - When Simple: Displays base `price` and `stockQuantity` fields.
+  - When Multi-Variant: Displays `OptionGroupBuilder` and `VariantMatrixTable`. Base price is auto-populated from min variant price, and total stock is computed as the sum of variant stock.
+- **`OptionGroupBuilder.tsx`**: Allows adding up to 3 option groups (e.g. "Dung lượng", "Màu sắc"), with chip-style tag inputs for values (e.g. "128GB", "256GB"). Generates UUIDs for each group and value.
+- **`VariantMatrixTable.tsx`**: Computes the Cartesian product of all option values using their UUIDs. Renders a responsive table allowing inline editing of `priceVND`, `stockQuantity`, and an `active` checkbox per combination. Includes a "Áp dụng giá/kho hàng loạt" (Bulk Apply) utility.
+
+## Data Models and Persistence
+
+### Atomic Save / Update Transaction
+When `r.SaveProductWithVariants(ctx, id, input)` is invoked:
+1. **Product Row**: Upsert or update `products` row (`name`, `model`, `brand`, `description`, `category_id`, `featured`, `active`, `price`, `stock_quantity`).
+2. **Product Images**: Replace entries in `product_images`.
+3. **If `hasVariants == false` or `len(optionGroups) == 0`**:
+   - Delete all associated `variant_option_values`, `product_variants`, `product_option_values`, `product_option_groups` for that product.
+   - Maintain `products.price = input.Price` and `products.stock_quantity = input.StockQuantity`.
+4. **If `hasVariants == true`**:
+   - Persist each `product_option_groups` and `product_option_values` with their provided UUIDs.
+   - Reconcile `product_variants`: for existing variants (matching UUID or combination), update `price_vnd`, `stock_quantity`, `active`. For new combinations, insert. For deleted combinations, delete (or cascade).
+   - Insert `variant_option_values` links for each variant.
+   - Calculate min/max price from active variants and sum total stock from active variants, updating `products.price` and `products.stock_quantity`. (If all variants are inactive, `minPriceVND` and `maxPriceVND` fallback to the minimum price of all defined variants, and total stock is set to 0).
+
+## Error Handling
+
+| Scenario | HTTP Status | Code | Message (Vietnamese) |
+|---|---|---|---|
+| Empty option group label | `400 Bad Request` | `validation_error` | "Tên nhóm thuộc tính không được để trống" |
+| Option group without values | `400 Bad Request` | `validation_error` | "Nhóm thuộc tính phải có ít nhất một giá trị" |
+| Negative variant price / stock | `400 Bad Request` | `validation_error` | "Giá biến thể phải là số không âm" / "Số lượng tồn kho không được âm" |
+| Incomplete variant matrix links | `400 Bad Request` | `validation_error` | "Mỗi biến thể phải chọn đầy đủ giá trị cho từng nhóm thuộc tính" |
+| Non-admin access to admin variant APIs | `403 Forbidden` | `forbidden` | "Bạn không có quyền thực hiện thao tác này" |
+| Product not found | `404 Not Found` | `product_not_found` | "Không tìm thấy sản phẩm" |
+
+## Testing Strategy
+
+### 1. Backend (`backend/`)
+- **Domain & Validation Tests**: Test validation rules for option groups (empty label, duplicate values, invalid prices/stock in variants).
+- **Repository Integration Tests**: Verify atomic persistence of option groups, option values, variants, and variant links in PostgreSQL.
+- **HTTP Admin Handler Tests**: Test `POST /api/v1/admin/products` and `PUT /api/v1/admin/products/:id` with variant matrix payloads, verifying Full Status Envelope and error status codes.
+
+### 2. Frontend Admin (`frontend-admin/`)
+- **Cartesian Product Helper Tests**: Unit test for generating matrix combinations from N option groups.
+- **`OptionGroupBuilder` Component Tests**: Test adding/removing groups and option values with validation feedback.
+- **`VariantMatrixTable` Component Tests**: Test table rendering, price/stock inputs, bulk apply actions, and validation errors.
+- **`ProductFormPage` Integration Tests**: Test toggling modes, filling variant matrix, and form submission to `createAdminProduct` / `updateAdminProduct`.
+
+## Requirement Traceability
+
+| Requirement ID | Design Element | Verification |
+|---|---|---|
+| 1.1 - 1.4 | `OptionGroupBuilder.tsx`, `OptionGroupInput`, validation | Frontend unit tests + Backend validation tests |
+| 2.1 - 2.5 | `VariantMatrixTable.tsx`, Cartesian product, price/stock sync | Matrix helper tests + Repo integration tests |
+| 3.1 - 3.4 | `ProductFormPage.tsx` mode toggle & detail loader | Form page tests & Mock API tests |
+| 4.1 - 4.3 | `AdminCatalogHandler`, `CatalogRepository.SaveProductWithVariants` | Handler tests + Integration DB tests |
+| 5.1 - 5.4 | Storefront `ProductDetailPage`, Cart flow, `InStock()` | Customer frontend vitest suite |

+# Requirements Document
+
+## Introduction
+The `admin-product-variants` feature enhances the product management capabilities within the admin dashboard (`frontend-admin`) by enabling administrators to define option groups (such as "Màu sắc", "Dung lượng") and manage product variants with individual pricing, stock quantities, and availability statuses. It also updates backend administrative APIs to support saving and querying variant option matrices while preserving complete compatibility with the customer storefront and cart systems.
+
+## Requirements
+
+### Requirement 1: Option Groups and Values Configuration
+**User Story:** As an administrator, I want to define option groups (attributes) and their respective option values for a product, so that customers can choose specific configurations.
+
+#### Acceptance Criteria
+1.1 WHEN an administrator configures a product with multiple variations, THEN the system SHALL allow defining one or more option groups with custom labels (e.g., "Dung lượng", "Màu sắc").
+1.2 WHEN an administrator creates an option group, THEN the system SHALL require at least one option value label (e.g., "128GB", "256GB") within that group.
+1.3 IF an administrator adds, modifies, or removes option values in an option group, THEN the system SHALL dynamically update the list of selectable product variants.
+1.4 IF an administrator attempts to save an option group with an empty label or duplicate value labels within the same group, THEN the system SHALL reject the submission and display a Vietnamese validation error message.
+
+### Requirement 2: Variant Matrix Management and Pricing
+**User Story:** As an administrator, I want to configure individual prices, stock quantities, and active statuses for each product variant, so that different versions of a product can have distinct pricing and inventory levels.
+
+#### Acceptance Criteria
+2.1 WHEN option groups and values are configured, THEN the system SHALL generate all combinations of option values into a variant matrix table.
+2.2 FOR EACH variant in the matrix, the system SHALL allow the administrator to set an individual price in VND (non-negative integer) and stock quantity (non-negative integer).
+2.3 FOR EACH variant in the matrix, the system SHALL allow toggling its active status (`active: true/false`) to enable or disable purchasing that specific combination.
+2.4 IF an administrator enters an invalid price (negative or non-numeric) or invalid stock quantity for any variant, THEN the system SHALL display inline validation errors in Vietnamese adjacent to the invalid input.
+2.5 WHEN a product with variants is saved, THEN the system SHALL automatically compute the overall product price range (`minPriceVND` - `maxPriceVND`) and total stock quantity from its active variants.
+
+### Requirement 3: Admin Product Form Mode Switching
+**User Story:** As an administrator, I want to choose whether a product is a simple single-variant item or a multi-variant item, so that I can manage both accessories and configurable electronics efficiently.
+
+#### Acceptance Criteria
+3.1 WHEN an administrator creates or edits a product, THEN the system SHALL provide a clear toggle between "Sản phẩm đơn giản" (single price/stock) and "Sản phẩm có biến thể" (variant matrix).
+3.2 IF "Sản phẩm đơn giản" is selected, THEN the system SHALL require direct input for product price and stock quantity, hiding the variant matrix.
+3.3 IF "Sản phẩm có biến thể" is selected, THEN the system SHALL require defining at least one option group and valid variant matrix entries, using the matrix values for pricing and inventory.
+3.4 WHEN an administrator edits an existing product that already possesses option groups and variants, THEN the system SHALL load and display the existing option groups, values, and variant matrix in the edit form.
+
+### Requirement 4: Administrative Variant APIs and Data Integrity
+**User Story:** As a system operator, I want administrative APIs to atomically persist and return product option groups and variants, ensuring data consistency and role-based access control.
+
+#### Acceptance Criteria
+4.1 WHEN an administrator sends a `POST /api/v1/admin/products` or `PUT /api/v1/admin/products/:id` request containing option groups and variants, THEN the backend SHALL persist the product, option groups, option values, product variants, and variant option links atomically in a single database transaction.
+4.2 WHEN `GET /api/v1/admin/products/:id` is called by an administrator, THEN the system SHALL return the product detail including all option groups, option values, and product variants with their associated prices and stock quantities.
+4.3 IF a non-admin user or unauthenticated request calls administrative product variant endpoints, THEN the system SHALL reject the request with HTTP 401 Unauthorized or HTTP 403 Forbidden with a Vietnamese error message in the Full Status Envelope.
+
+### Requirement 5: Storefront and Cart Compatibility
+**User Story:** As a customer, I want to select options and purchase available variants on the storefront without regressions or data inconsistencies.
+
+#### Acceptance Criteria
+5.1 WHEN a product with variants is viewed on the customer storefront (`frontend`), THEN the system SHALL display the option selectors (e.g. Storage, Color) and update the price and in-stock badge based on the selected variant.
+5.2 IF all active variants of a product have zero stock quantity, THEN the storefront and admin product list SHALL display "Hết hàng" (out of stock).
+5.3 IF at least one active variant has a stock quantity greater than zero, THEN the storefront and admin product list SHALL display "Còn hàng" (in stock).
+5.4 WHEN a customer adds a specific variant to their cart, THEN the system SHALL validate the variant's active status and available stock without regressions.

+
+func loadEnvFile(path string) {
+	data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return
+	}
+	for _, line := range strings.Split(string(data), "\n") {
+		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
+		if line == "" || strings.HasPrefix(line, "#") {
+			continue
+		}
+		parts := strings.SplitN(line, "=", 2)
+		if len(parts) == 2 {
+			k := strings.TrimSpace(parts[0])
+			v := strings.TrimSpace(parts[1])
+			if os.Getenv(k) == "" {
+				os.Setenv(k, v)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+}

+package config
+
+import (
+	"os"
+	"testing"
+)
+
+func TestConfig_AdminBootstrapEnv(t *testing.T) {
+	os.Setenv("DB_URL", "postgres://user:pass@localhost:5432/db")
+	os.Setenv("JWT_ACCESS_SECRET", "super-secret-key-that-is-at-least-32-chars-long")
+	os.Setenv("ADMIN_BOOTSTRAP_EMAIL", "admin@domain.com")
+	os.Setenv("ADMIN_BOOTSTRAP_PASSWORD", "adminpassword123")
+	defer func() {
+		os.Unsetenv("DB_URL")
+		os.Unsetenv("JWT_ACCESS_SECRET")
+		os.Unsetenv("ADMIN_BOOTSTRAP_EMAIL")
+		os.Unsetenv("ADMIN_BOOTSTRAP_PASSWORD")
+	}()
+
+	cfg, err := Load()
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("failed to load config: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	if cfg.AdminBootstrapEmail != "admin@domain.com" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected AdminBootstrapEmail admin@domain.com, got %s", cfg.AdminBootstrapEmail)
+	}
+	if cfg.AdminBootstrapPassword != "adminpassword123" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected AdminBootstrapPassword adminpassword123, got %s", cfg.AdminBootstrapPassword)
+	}
+}
+
+func TestConfig_AdminBootstrapEnv_DefaultEmpty(t *testing.T) {
+	os.Setenv("DB_URL", "postgres://user:pass@localhost:5432/db")
+	os.Setenv("JWT_ACCESS_SECRET", "super-secret-key-that-is-at-least-32-chars-long")
+	os.Unsetenv("ADMIN_BOOTSTRAP_EMAIL")
+	os.Unsetenv("ADMIN_BOOTSTRAP_PASSWORD")
+	defer func() {
+		os.Unsetenv("DB_URL")
+		os.Unsetenv("JWT_ACCESS_SECRET")
+	}()
+
+	cfg, err := Load()
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("failed to load config: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	if cfg.AdminBootstrapEmail != "" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected empty AdminBootstrapEmail, got %s", cfg.AdminBootstrapEmail)
+	}
+	if cfg.AdminBootstrapPassword != "" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected empty AdminBootstrapPassword, got %s", cfg.AdminBootstrapPassword)
+	}
+}

+package http
+
+import (
+	"net/http"
+	"strconv"
+
+	"github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
+
+	"commerce-backend/internal/domain"
+	cataloguc "commerce-backend/internal/usecase/catalog"
+)
+
+type adminCatalogHandler struct {
+	uc *cataloguc.UseCase
+}
+
+func newAdminCatalogHandler(uc *cataloguc.UseCase) *adminCatalogHandler {
+	return &adminCatalogHandler{uc: uc}
+}
+
+type optionValueInputDTO struct {
+	ID       string `json:"id"`
+	Label    string `json:"label"`
+	Position int    `json:"position"`
+}
+
+type optionGroupInputDTO struct {
+	ID       string                `json:"id"`
+	Label    string                `json:"label"`
+	Position int                   `json:"position"`
+	Values   []optionValueInputDTO `json:"values"`
+}
+
+type variantInputDTO struct {
+	ID             string            `json:"id"`
+	OptionValueIDs map[string]string `json:"optionValueIds"`
+	PriceVND       string            `json:"priceVnd"`
+	PriceVNDAlt    string            `json:"priceVND,omitempty"`
+	StockQuantity  int64             `json:"stockQuantity"`
+	Active         bool              `json:"active"`
+}
+
+type saveProductRequest struct {
+	Name          string                `json:"name"`
+	Model         string                `json:"model"`
+	Brand         string                `json:"brand"`
+	Description   string                `json:"description"`
+	CategoryID    string                `json:"categoryId"`
+	Price         string                `json:"price"`
+	Currency      string                `json:"currency"`
+	StockQuantity int64                 `json:"stockQuantity"`
+	Featured      bool                  `json:"featured"`
+	ImageURLs     []string              `json:"imageUrls"`
+	HasVariants   bool                  `json:"hasVariants"`
+	OptionGroups  []optionGroupInputDTO `json:"optionGroups"`
+	Variants      []variantInputDTO     `json:"variants"`
+}
+
+func (req saveProductRequest) toDomain() domain.SaveProductInput {
+	var optionGroups []domain.OptionGroupInput
+	if len(req.OptionGroups) > 0 {
+		optionGroups = make([]domain.OptionGroupInput, 0, len(req.OptionGroups))
+		for i, g := range req.OptionGroups {
+			pos := g.Position
+			if pos == 0 {
+				pos = i
+			}
+			var vals []domain.OptionValueInput
+			if len(g.Values) > 0 {
+				vals = make([]domain.OptionValueInput, 0, len(g.Values))
+				for j, v := range g.Values {
+					vPos := v.Position
+					if vPos == 0 {
+						vPos = j
+					}
+					vals = append(vals, domain.OptionValueInput{
+						ID:       v.ID,
+						Label:    v.Label,
+						Position: vPos,
+					})
+				}
+			}
+			optionGroups = append(optionGroups, domain.OptionGroupInput{
+				ID:       g.ID,
+				Label:    g.Label,
+				Position: pos,
+				Values:   vals,
+			})
+		}
+	}
+
+	var variants []domain.VariantInput
+	if len(req.Variants) > 0 {
+		variants = make([]domain.VariantInput, 0, len(req.Variants))
+		for _, v := range req.Variants {
+			price := v.PriceVND
+			if price == "" && v.PriceVNDAlt != "" {
+				price = v.PriceVNDAlt
+			}
+			optIDs := v.OptionValueIDs
+			if optIDs == nil {
+				optIDs = make(map[string]string)
+			}
+			variants = append(variants, domain.VariantInput{
+				ID:             v.ID,
+				OptionValueIDs: optIDs,
+				PriceVND:       price,
+				StockQuantity:  v.StockQuantity,
+				Active:         v.Active,
+			})
+		}
+	}
+
+	return domain.SaveProductInput{
+		Name:          req.Name,
+		Model:         req.Model,
+		Brand:         req.Brand,
+		Description:   req.Description,
+		CategoryID:    req.CategoryID,
+		Price:         req.Price,
+		Currency:      req.Currency,
+		StockQuantity: req.StockQuantity,
+		Featured:      req.Featured,
+		ImageURLs:     req.ImageURLs,
+		HasVariants:   req.HasVariants,
+		OptionGroups:  optionGroups,
+		Variants:      variants,
+	}
+}
+
+func (h *adminCatalogHandler) ListProducts(c *gin.Context) {
+	pageStr, pageSet := c.GetQuery("page")
+	limitStr, limitSet := c.GetQuery("limit")
+
+	fields := map[string]string{}
+	page := 1
+	limit := 10
+
+	if pageSet {
+		if pageStr == "" {
+			fields["page"] = "must not be empty"
+		} else {
+			p, err := strconv.Atoi(pageStr)
+			if err != nil || p < 1 {
+				fields["page"] = "must be a positive integer"
+			} else {
+				page = p
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	if limitSet {
+		if limitStr == "" {
+			fields["limit"] = "must not be empty"
+		} else {
+			l, err := strconv.Atoi(limitStr)
+			if err != nil || l < 1 || l > 100 {
+				fields["limit"] = "must be a positive integer between 1 and 100"
+			} else {
+				limit = l
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	if len(fields) > 0 {
+		writeError(c, domain.NewValidationError(fields))
+		return
+	}
+
+	products, err := h.uc.ListAdminProducts(c, c.Query("q"))
+	if err != nil {
+		writeError(c, err)
+		return
+	}
+
+	out := make([]productSummaryResponse, 0, len(products))
+	for _, p := range products {
+		out = append(out, toSummaryResponse(p))
+	}
+
+	total := len(out)
+	totalPages := 0
+	if total > 0 {
+		totalPages = (total + limit - 1) / limit
+	}
+	offset := (page - 1) * limit
+	var pageItems []productSummaryResponse
+	if offset < total {
+		end := offset + limit
+		if end > total {
+			end = total
+		}
+		pageItems = out[offset:end]
+	} else {
+		pageItems = []productSummaryResponse{}
+	}
+
+	pagination := PaginationMeta{
+		Page:       page,
+		Limit:      limit,
+		Total:      total,
+		TotalPages: totalPages,
+		HasNext:    page < totalPages,
+		HasPrev:    page > 1,
+	}
+
+	writePaginatedSuccess(c, http.StatusOK, "", pageItems, pagination)
+}
+
+func (h *adminCatalogHandler) GetProduct(c *gin.Context) {
+	product, err := h.uc.GetAdminProduct(c, c.Param("id"))
+	if err != nil {
+		writeError(c, err)
+		return
+	}
+	writeSuccess(c, http.StatusOK, "", toDetailResponse(*product))
+}
+
+func (h *adminCatalogHandler) CreateProduct(c *gin.Context) {
+	var req saveProductRequest
+	if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&req); err != nil {
+		writeError(c, domain.NewValidationError(map[string]string{"body": "invalid JSON body"}))
+		return
+	}
+
+	input := req.toDomain()
+	created, err := h.uc.CreateProduct(c, input)
+	if err != nil {
+		writeError(c, err)
+		return
+	}
+
+	writeSuccess(c, http.StatusCreated, "Tạo sản phẩm thành công", toDetailResponse(*created))
+}
+
+func (h *adminCatalogHandler) UpdateProduct(c *gin.Context) {
+	var req saveProductRequest
+	if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&req); err != nil {
+		writeError(c, domain.NewValidationError(map[string]string{"body": "invalid JSON body"}))
+		return
+	}
+
+	input := req.toDomain()
+	updated, err := h.uc.UpdateProduct(c, c.Param("id"), input)
+	if err != nil {
+		writeError(c, err)
+		return
+	}
+
+	writeSuccess(c, http.StatusOK, "Cập nhật sản phẩm thành công", toDetailResponse(*updated))
+}
+
+func (h *adminCatalogHandler) DeleteProduct(c *gin.Context) {
+	err := h.uc.DeleteProduct(c, c.Param("id"))
+	if err != nil {
+		writeError(c, err)
+		return
+	}
+	c.Status(http.StatusNoContent)
+}

+package http
+
+import (
+	"bytes"
+	"encoding/json"
+	"net/http"
+	"net/http/httptest"
+	"testing"
+	"time"
+
+	"github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
+
+	"commerce-backend/internal/domain"
+	"commerce-backend/internal/security/jwt"
+	cataloguc "commerce-backend/internal/usecase/catalog"
+	"commerce-backend/internal/usecase/catalog/fakerepos"
+)
+
+func setupAdminCatalogTestRouter() (*gin.Engine, *jwt.Manager, *fakerepos.Catalog, *cataloguc.UseCase) {
+	gin.SetMode(gin.TestMode)
+	manager := jwt.NewManager(testSecret, "commerce-backend")
+	repo := fakerepos.NewCatalog()
+	repo.AddCategory(domain.Category{ID: "11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111", Name: "Điện thoại"})
+	repo.AddCategory(domain.Category{ID: "22222222-2222-2222-2222-222222222222", Name: "Máy tính"})
+
+	uc := cataloguc.New(cataloguc.Dependencies{Catalog: repo})
+	r := gin.New()
+
+	NewRouter(r, Dependencies{
+		Catalog:      uc,
+		JWT:          manager,
+		RefreshTTL:   7 * 24 * time.Hour,
+		CookieSecure: false,
+	})
+
+	return r, manager, repo, uc
+}
+
+func adminBearerToken(t *testing.T, manager *jwt.Manager) string {
+	t.Helper()
+	token, err := manager.GenerateAccessToken("admin-user-id", domain.RoleAdmin, 15*time.Minute)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("generate admin token: %v", err)
+	}
+	return "Bearer " + token
+}
+
+func customerBearerToken(t *testing.T, manager *jwt.Manager) string {
+	t.Helper()
+	token, err := manager.GenerateAccessToken("customer-user-id", domain.RoleCustomer, 15*time.Minute)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("generate customer token: %v", err)
+	}
+	return "Bearer " + token
+}
+
+func TestAdminCatalog_AuthGuards(t *testing.T) {
+	router, manager, repo, _ := setupAdminCatalogTestRouter()
+	prodID := "aaaaaaaa-aaaa-aaaa-aaaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa"
+	repo.AddProduct(domain.Product{
+		ID:       prodID,
+		Name:     "Test Phone",
+		Category: domain.Category{ID: "11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111", Name: "Điện thoại"},
+		Active:   true,
+	})
+
+	endpoints := []struct {
+		method string
+		url    string
+		body   string
+	}{
+		{http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/admin/products", ""},
+		{http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/admin/products/" + prodID, ""},
+		{http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/admin/products", `{"name":"New","categoryId":"11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111","price":"100"}`},
+		{http.MethodPut, "/api/v1/admin/products/" + prodID, `{"name":"Update","categoryId":"11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111","price":"100"}`},
+		{http.MethodDelete, "/api/v1/admin/products/" + prodID, ""},
+	}
+
+	for _, ep := range endpoints {
+		t.Run("NoAuth_"+ep.method+"_"+ep.url, func(t *testing.T) {
+			req := httptest.NewRequest(ep.method, ep.url, bytes.NewBufferString(ep.body))
+			if ep.body != "" {
+				req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
+			}
+			w := httptest.NewRecorder()
+			router.ServeHTTP(w, req)
+
+			if w.Code != http.StatusUnauthorized {
+				t.Fatalf("expected 401 Unauthorized, got %d for %s %s", w.Code, ep.method, ep.url)
+			}
+		})
+
+		t.Run("CustomerForbidden_"+ep.method+"_"+ep.url, func(t *testing.T) {
+			req := httptest.NewRequest(ep.method, ep.url, bytes.NewBufferString(ep.body))
+			if ep.body != "" {
+				req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
+			}
+			req.Header.Set("Authorization", customerBearerToken(t, manager))
+			w := httptest.NewRecorder()
+			router.ServeHTTP(w, req)
+
+			if w.Code != http.StatusForbidden {
+				t.Fatalf("expected 403 Forbidden, got %d for %s %s", w.Code, ep.method, ep.url)
+			}
+			var env ResponseEnvelope
+			if err := json.Unmarshal(w.Body.Bytes(), &env); err != nil {
+				t.Fatalf("unmarshal error envelope: %v", err)
+			}
+			if env.Error == nil || env.Error.Code != "forbidden" {
+				t.Errorf("expected error.code 'forbidden', got %+v", env.Error)
+			}
+			if env.Error.Message != "Bạn không có quyền thực hiện thao tác này" {
+				t.Errorf("expected Vietnamese forbidden message, got %q", env.Error.Message)
+			}
+		})
+	}
+}
+
+func TestAdminCatalog_ListProducts_SuccessAndPagination(t *testing.T) {
+	router, manager, repo, _ := setupAdminCatalogTestRouter()
+	token := adminBearerToken(t, manager)
+
+	// Add a product with NO variants
+	repo.AddProduct(domain.Product{
+		ID:            "aaaaaaaa-aaaa-aaaa-aaaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa",
+		Name:          "Xiaomi 14 Pro",
+		Model:         "23116PN5BC",
+		Brand:         "Xiaomi",
+		Category:      domain.Category{ID: "11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111", Name: "Điện thoại"},
+		Price:         "15000000",
+		Currency:      "VND",
+		StockQuantity: 30,
+		Active:        true,
+		ImageURLs:     []string{"https://example.com/xiaomi14.jpg"},
+	})
+
+	// Add a soft-deleted product (should not appear)
+	repo.AddProductWithActiveState(domain.Product{
+		ID:       "bbbbbbbb-bbbb-bbbb-bbbb-bbbbbbbbbbbb",
+		Name:     "Old Deleted Phone",
+		Category: domain.Category{ID: "11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111", Name: "Điện thoại"},
+		Active:   false,
+	}, false)
+
+	req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/admin/products", nil)
+	req.Header.Set("Authorization", token)
+	w := httptest.NewRecorder()
+	router.ServeHTTP(w, req)
+
+	if w.Code != http.StatusOK {
+		t.Fatalf("expected status 200, got %d. Body: %s", w.Code, w.Body.String())
+	}
+
+	var env struct {
+		Success bool                     `json:"success"`
+		Message string                   `json:"message"`
+		Data    []productSummaryResponse `json:"data"`
+		Meta    ResponseMeta             `json:"meta"`
+	}
+	if err := json.Unmarshal(w.Body.Bytes(), &env); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("unmarshal response: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	if !env.Success {
+		t.Fatalf("expected success=true")
+	}
+	if len(env.Data) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("expected 1 product (excluding soft deleted), got %d", len(env.Data))
+	}
+	p := env.Data[0]
+	if p.Name != "Xiaomi 14 Pro" || p.Brand != "Xiaomi" || p.Category.Name != "Điện thoại" {
+		t.Errorf("unexpected product data: %+v", p)
+	}
+	if env.Meta.Pagination == nil {
+		t.Fatalf("expected pagination metadata in admin product list")
+	}
+	if env.Meta.Pagination.Total != 1 || env.Meta.Pagination.Page != 1 {
+		t.Errorf("unexpected pagination meta: %+v", env.Meta.Pagination)
+	}
+}
+

+		Brand:    "Apple",

+	}

+		Data []productSummaryResponse `json:"data"`
+		Meta ResponseMeta             `json:"meta"`
+	}
+	if err := json.Unmarshal(w.Body.Bytes(), &env); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("unmarshal response: %v", err)
+	}
+	if len(env.Data) != 1 || env.Data[0].Name != "MacBook Pro 16" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected MacBook Pro 16, got: %+v", env.Data)
+	}

+	token := adminBearerToken(t, manager)
+
+	payload := `{
+		"name": "iPad Pro M4",
+		"model": "A2836",
+		"brand": "Apple",
+		"description": "Ultra thin tablet",
+		"categoryId": "22222222-2222-2222-2222-222222222222",
+		"price": "28990000",
+		"currency": "VND",
+		"stockQuantity": 15,
+		"featured": true,
+		"imageUrls": ["https://example.com/ipad1.jpg", "https://example.com/ipad2.jpg"]
+	}`
+
+	req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/admin/products", bytes.NewBufferString(payload))
+	req.Header.Set("Authorization", token)
+	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
+	w := httptest.NewRecorder()
+	router.ServeHTTP(w, req)
+
+	if w.Code != http.StatusCreated {
+		t.Fatalf("expected status 201 Created, got %d. Body: %s", w.Code, w.Body.String())
+	}
+
+	var env struct {
+		Success bool                  `json:"success"`
+		Message string                `json:"message"`
+		Data    productDetailResponse `json:"data"`

+	}
+	if !env.Success || env.Data.ID == "" || env.Data.Name != "iPad Pro M4" || env.Data.Brand != "Apple" {
+		t.Errorf("unexpected created detail: %+v", env.Data)
+	}
+	if len(env.Data.ImageURLs) != 2 {
+		t.Errorf("expected 2 image URLs, got %d", len(env.Data.ImageURLs))
+	}

+		"stockQuantity": -5
+	}`
+
+	req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/admin/products", bytes.NewBufferString(payload))
+	req.Header.Set("Authorization", token)
+	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
+	w := httptest.NewRecorder()
+	router.ServeHTTP(w, req)
+
+	if w.Code != http.StatusBadRequest {
+		t.Fatalf("expected 400 Bad Request, got %d. Body: %s", w.Code, w.Body.String())
+	}
+
+	var env ResponseEnvelope
+	if err := json.Unmarshal(w.Body.Bytes(), &env); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("unmarshal: %v", err)
+	}
+	if env.Error == nil || env.Error.Code != "validation_error" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected error.code 'validation_error', got %+v", env.Error)
+	}
+	if env.Error.Fields["name"] == "" || env.Error.Fields["categoryId"] == "" || env.Error.Fields["price"] == "" || env.Error.Fields["stockQuantity"] == "" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected validation errors on fields, got: %+v", env.Error.Fields)
+	}

+func TestAdminCatalog_UpdateProduct_Success(t *testing.T) {
+	router, manager, repo, _ := setupAdminCatalogTestRouter()
+	token := adminBearerToken(t, manager)
+
+	id := "aaaaaaaa-aaaa-aaaa-aaaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa"
+	repo.AddProduct(domain.Product{
+		ID:            id,
+		Name:          "Old Product",
+		Model:         "V1",
+		Brand:         "OldBrand",
+		Category:      domain.Category{ID: "11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111", Name: "Điện thoại"},
+		Price:         "100000",
+		Currency:      "VND",
+		StockQuantity: 5,
+		Active:        true,
+	})
+
+	payload := `{
+		"name": "Updated Product",
+		"model": "V2",
+		"brand": "NewBrand",
+		"description": "Updated desc",
+		"categoryId": "22222222-2222-2222-2222-222222222222",
+		"price": "250000",
+		"currency": "VND",
+		"stockQuantity": 12,
+		"featured": false,
+		"imageUrls": ["https://example.com/new.jpg"]
+	}`
+
+	req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodPut, "/api/v1/admin/products/"+id, bytes.NewBufferString(payload))
+	req.Header.Set("Authorization", token)
+	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
+	w := httptest.NewRecorder()
+	router.ServeHTTP(w, req)
+
+	if w.Code != http.StatusOK {
+		t.Fatalf("expected status 200 OK, got %d. Body: %s", w.Code, w.Body.String())
+	}
+
+	var env struct {
+		Success bool                  `json:"success"`
+		Data    productDetailResponse `json:"data"`
+	}
+	if err := json.Unmarshal(w.Body.Bytes(), &env); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("unmarshal: %v", err)
+	}
+	if env.Data.Name != "Updated Product" || env.Data.Brand != "NewBrand" || env.Data.Category.ID != "22222222-2222-2222-2222-222222222222" {
+		t.Errorf("unexpected updated data: %+v", env.Data)
+	}
+}

+func TestAdminCatalog_DeleteProduct_SoftDelete_Returns204(t *testing.T) {
+	router, manager, repo, _ := setupAdminCatalogTestRouter()
+	token := adminBearerToken(t, manager)
+
+	id := "aaaaaaaa-aaaa-aaaa-aaaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa"
+	repo.AddProduct(domain.Product{
+		ID:            id,
+		Name:          "Product To Delete",
+		Category:      domain.Category{ID: "11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111", Name: "Điện thoại"},
+		Price:         "100000",
+		StockQuantity: 10,
+		Active:        true,
+	})
+
+	// DELETE request
+	req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodDelete, "/api/v1/admin/products/"+id, nil)
+	req.Header.Set("Authorization", token)
+	w := httptest.NewRecorder()
+	router.ServeHTTP(w, req)
+
+	if w.Code != http.StatusNoContent {
+		t.Fatalf("expected status 204 No Content, got %d. Body: %s", w.Code, w.Body.String())
+	}
+	if w.Body.Len() > 0 {
+		t.Errorf("expected empty body for 204 No Content, got %q", w.Body.String())
+	}
+
+	// Verify subsequent GET returns 404
+	getReq := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/admin/products/"+id, nil)
+	getReq.Header.Set("Authorization", token)
+	wGet := httptest.NewRecorder()
+	router.ServeHTTP(wGet, getReq)
+
+	if wGet.Code != http.StatusNotFound {
+		t.Fatalf("expected 404 for soft-deleted product, got %d", wGet.Code)
+	}
+
+	// Verify list endpoint does NOT return deleted product
+	listReq := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/admin/products", nil)
+	listReq.Header.Set("Authorization", token)
+	wList := httptest.NewRecorder()
+	router.ServeHTTP(wList, listReq)
+
+	var env struct {
+		Data []productSummaryResponse `json:"data"`
+	}
+	if err := json.Unmarshal(wList.Body.Bytes(), &env); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("unmarshal: %v", err)
+	}
+	if len(env.Data) != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("expected 0 products in list, got %d", len(env.Data))
+	}
+}
+

+func TestAdminCatalog_CreateProduct_WithVariants_Success(t *testing.T) {
+	router, manager, _, _ := setupAdminCatalogTestRouter()
+	token := adminBearerToken(t, manager)
+
+	payload := `{
+		"name": "iPhone 15 Pro",
+		"model": "A3102",
+		"brand": "Apple",
+		"description": "Titanium smartphone",
+		"categoryId": "11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111",
+		"hasVariants": true,
+		"optionGroups": [
+			{
+				"id": "group-color",
+				"label": "Màu sắc",
+				"position": 0,
+				"values": [
+					{"id": "val-color-natural", "label": "Titan Tự Nhiên"},
+					{"id": "val-color-blue", "label": "Titan Xanh"}
+				]
+			},
+			{
+				"id": "group-storage",
+				"label": "Dung lượng",
+				"position": 1,
+				"values": [
+					{"id": "val-storage-128", "label": "128GB"},
+					{"id": "val-storage-256", "label": "256GB"}
+				]
+			}
+		],
+		"variants": [
+			{
+				"id": "var-1",
+				"optionValueIds": {"group-color": "val-color-natural", "group-storage": "val-storage-128"},
+				"priceVnd": "24990000",
+				"stockQuantity": 10,
+				"active": true
+			},
+			{
+				"id": "var-2",
+				"optionValueIds": {"group-color": "val-color-natural", "group-storage": "val-storage-256"},
+				"priceVnd": "27990000",
+				"stockQuantity": 15,
+				"active": true
+			},
+			{
+				"id": "var-3",
+				"optionValueIds": {"group-color": "val-color-blue", "group-storage": "val-storage-128"},
+				"priceVnd": "24990000",
+				"stockQuantity": 5,
+				"active": true
+			},
+			{
+				"id": "var-4",
+				"optionValueIds": {"group-color": "val-color-blue", "group-storage": "val-storage-256"},
+				"priceVnd": "27990000",
+				"stockQuantity": 0,
+				"active": false
+			}
+		],
+		"featured": true,
+		"imageUrls": ["https://example.com/ip15.jpg"]
+	}`
+
+	req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/admin/products", bytes.NewBufferString(payload))
+	req.Header.Set("Authorization", token)
+	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
+	w := httptest.NewRecorder()
+	router.ServeHTTP(w, req)
+
+	if w.Code != http.StatusCreated {
+		t.Fatalf("expected status 201 Created, got %d. Body: %s", w.Code, w.Body.String())
+	}
+
+	var env struct {
+		Success bool                  `json:"success"`
+		Message string                `json:"message"`
+		Data    productDetailResponse `json:"data"`
+	}
+	if err := json.Unmarshal(w.Body.Bytes(), &env); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("unmarshal: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	if !env.Success {
+		t.Fatalf("expected success=true")
+	}
+	if len(env.Data.OptionGroups) != 2 {
+		t.Fatalf("expected 2 option groups in detail response, got %d", len(env.Data.OptionGroups))
+	}
+	if len(env.Data.Variants) != 4 {
+		t.Fatalf("expected 4 variants in detail response, got %d", len(env.Data.Variants))
+	}
+	if env.Data.Price != "24990000" {
+		t.Errorf("expected computed min price 24990000, got %s", env.Data.Price)
+	}
+	if env.Data.StockQuantity != 30 {
+		t.Errorf("expected computed active stock sum 30, got %d", env.Data.StockQuantity)
+	}

+func TestAdminCatalog_CreateProduct_VariantValidationErrors(t *testing.T) {
+	router, manager, _, _ := setupAdminCatalogTestRouter()
+	token := adminBearerToken(t, manager)
+
+	tests := []struct {
+		name          string
+		payload       string
+		expectedField string
+		expectedMsg   string
+	}{
+		{
+			name: "hasVariants true with empty option groups",
+			payload: `{
+				"name": "Invalid Variant Product",
+				"categoryId": "11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111",
+				"hasVariants": true,
+				"optionGroups": []
+			}`,
+			expectedField: "optionGroups",
+			expectedMsg:   "Phải có ít nhất một nhóm thuộc tính",
+		},
+		{
+			name: "empty option group label",
+			payload: `{
+				"name": "Invalid Variant Product",
+				"categoryId": "11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111",
+				"hasVariants": true,
+				"optionGroups": [
+					{
+						"label": "   ",
+						"values": [{"label": "128GB"}]
+					}
+				],
+				"variants": [
+					{"priceVnd": "10000", "stockQuantity": 5, "active": true}
+				]
+			}`,
+			expectedField: "optionGroups[0].label",
+			expectedMsg:   "Tên nhóm thuộc tính không được để trống",
+		},
+		{
+			name: "option group with empty values",
+			payload: `{
+				"name": "Invalid Variant Product",
+				"categoryId": "11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111",
+				"hasVariants": true,
+				"optionGroups": [
+					{
+						"label": "Dung lượng",
+						"values": []
+					}
+				],
+				"variants": [
+					{"priceVnd": "10000", "stockQuantity": 5, "active": true}
+				]
+			}`,
+			expectedField: "optionGroups[0].values",
+			expectedMsg:   "Nhóm thuộc tính phải có ít nhất một giá trị",
+		},
+		{
+			name: "duplicate option values within group",
+			payload: `{
+				"name": "Invalid Variant Product",
+				"categoryId": "11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111",
+				"hasVariants": true,
+				"optionGroups": [
+					{
+						"label": "Màu sắc",
+						"values": [
+							{"label": "Đen"},
+							{"label": " đen "}
+						]
+					}
+				],
+				"variants": [
+					{"priceVnd": "10000", "stockQuantity": 5, "active": true}
+				]
+			}`,
+			expectedField: "optionGroups[0].values",
+			expectedMsg:   "Giá trị thuộc tính không được trùng nhau",
+		},
+		{
+			name: "negative variant price",
+			payload: `{
+				"name": "Invalid Variant Product",
+				"categoryId": "11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111",
+				"hasVariants": true,
+				"optionGroups": [
+					{
+						"label": "Màu sắc",
+						"values": [{"label": "Đen"}]
+					}
+				],
+				"variants": [
+					{"priceVnd": "-5000", "stockQuantity": 5, "active": true}
+				]
+			}`,
+			expectedField: "variants[0].priceVnd",
+			expectedMsg:   "Giá biến thể phải là số không âm",
+		},
+		{
+			name: "negative variant stock",
+			payload: `{
+				"name": "Invalid Variant Product",
+				"categoryId": "11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111",
+				"hasVariants": true,
+				"optionGroups": [
+					{
+						"label": "Màu sắc",
+						"values": [{"label": "Đen"}]
+					}
+				],
+				"variants": [
+					{"priceVnd": "50000", "stockQuantity": -3, "active": true}
+				]
+			}`,
+			expectedField: "variants[0].stockQuantity",
+			expectedMsg:   "Số lượng tồn kho không được âm",
+		},
+	}
+
+	for _, tt := range tests {
+		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
+			req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/admin/products", bytes.NewBufferString(tt.payload))
+			req.Header.Set("Authorization", token)
+			req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
+			w := httptest.NewRecorder()
+			router.ServeHTTP(w, req)
+
+			if w.Code != http.StatusBadRequest {
+				t.Fatalf("expected status 400 Bad Request, got %d. Body: %s", w.Code, w.Body.String())
+			}
+
+			var env ResponseEnvelope
+			if err := json.Unmarshal(w.Body.Bytes(), &env); err != nil {
+				t.Fatalf("unmarshal response: %v", err)
+			}
+			if env.Error == nil || env.Error.Code != "validation_error" {
+				t.Fatalf("expected error.code 'validation_error', got %+v", env.Error)
+			}
+			msg, ok := env.Error.Fields[tt.expectedField]
+			if !ok {
+				t.Fatalf("expected error field %q, got fields: %+v", tt.expectedField, env.Error.Fields)
+			}
+			if msg != tt.expectedMsg {
+				t.Errorf("expected error message %q, got %q", tt.expectedMsg, msg)
+			}
+		})
+	}
+}

+func TestAdminCatalog_UpdateProduct_WithVariants_Success(t *testing.T) {
+	router, manager, repo, _ := setupAdminCatalogTestRouter()
+	token := adminBearerToken(t, manager)
+
+	id := "aaaaaaaa-aaaa-aaaa-aaaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa"
+	repo.AddProduct(domain.Product{
+		ID:            id,
+		Name:          "Initial Simple Product",
+		Category:      domain.Category{ID: "11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111", Name: "Điện thoại"},
+		Price:         "5000000",
+		StockQuantity: 10,
+		Active:        true,
+	})
+
+	payload := `{
+		"name": "Updated Multi-Variant Product",
+		"categoryId": "11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111",
+		"hasVariants": true,
+		"optionGroups": [
+			{
+				"id": "grp-1",
+				"label": "Màu sắc",
+				"position": 0,
+				"values": [{"id": "val-1", "label": "Xanh"}]
+			}
+		],
+		"variants": [
+			{
+				"id": "var-1",
+				"optionValueIds": {"grp-1": "val-1"},
+				"priceVnd": "7500000",
+				"stockQuantity": 18,
+				"active": true
+			}
+		]
+	}`
+
+	req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodPut, "/api/v1/admin/products/"+id, bytes.NewBufferString(payload))
+	req.Header.Set("Authorization", token)
+	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
+	w := httptest.NewRecorder()
+	router.ServeHTTP(w, req)
+
+	if w.Code != http.StatusOK {
+		t.Fatalf("expected status 200 OK, got %d. Body: %s", w.Code, w.Body.String())
+	}
+
+	var env struct {
+		Success bool                  `json:"success"`
+		Data    productDetailResponse `json:"data"`
+	}
+	if err := json.Unmarshal(w.Body.Bytes(), &env); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("unmarshal: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	if env.Data.Name != "Updated Multi-Variant Product" {
+		t.Errorf("expected updated name, got %q", env.Data.Name)
+	}
+	if len(env.Data.OptionGroups) != 1 || len(env.Data.Variants) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("expected 1 option group and 1 variant, got groups=%d, variants=%d", len(env.Data.OptionGroups), len(env.Data.Variants))
+	}
+	if env.Data.Price != "7500000" || env.Data.StockQuantity != 18 {
+		t.Errorf("expected price 7500000 and stock 18, got price=%s stock=%d", env.Data.Price, env.Data.StockQuantity)
+	}
+}

+func setupAdminTestRouter() (*gin.Engine, *jwt.Manager) {
+	gin.SetMode(gin.TestMode)
+	manager := jwt.NewManager(testSecret, "commerce-backend")
+	guard := newGuard(manager)
+
+	r := gin.New()
+	admin := r.Group("/api/v1/admin")
+	admin.Use(guard.AuthRequired, guard.RequireAdmin())
+	{
+		admin.GET("/ping", func(c *gin.Context) {
+			writeSuccess(c, http.StatusOK, "admin pong", gin.H{"status": "ok"})
+		})
+	}
+	return r, manager
+}
+
+func TestAdminGuard_NoAuthHeader_Returns401(t *testing.T) {
+	router, _ := setupAdminTestRouter()
+
+	req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/admin/ping", nil)
+	w := httptest.NewRecorder()
+	router.ServeHTTP(w, req)
+
+	if w.Code != http.StatusUnauthorized {
+		t.Fatalf("expected status 401, got %d. Body: %s", w.Code, w.Body.String())
+	}
+}

+func TestAdminGuard_CustomerRole_Returns403ForbiddenWithVietnameseMessage(t *testing.T) {
+	router, manager := setupAdminTestRouter()
+
+	customerToken, err := manager.GenerateAccessToken("cust-1", domain.RoleCustomer, 15*time.Minute)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("generate token: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/admin/ping", nil)
+	req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+customerToken)
+	w := httptest.NewRecorder()
+	router.ServeHTTP(w, req)
+
+	if w.Code != http.StatusForbidden {
+		t.Fatalf("expected status 403, got %d. Body: %s", w.Code, w.Body.String())
+	}
+
+	var env ResponseEnvelope
+	if err := json.Unmarshal(w.Body.Bytes(), &env); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("unmarshal envelope: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	if env.Success {
+		t.Fatalf("expected success to be false, got true")
+	}
+	if env.Error == nil {
+		t.Fatalf("expected error object, got nil")
+	}
+	if env.Error.Code != "forbidden" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected error.code to be 'forbidden', got %s", env.Error.Code)
+	}
+	if env.Error.Message != "Bạn không có quyền thực hiện thao tác này" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected Vietnamese error message, got %s", env.Error.Message)
+	}
+}

+func TestCatalog_BrandAsStringAndCategoryAsTaxonomy(t *testing.T) {
+	router := newCatalogTestRouterWithPhone(t, true)
+
+	// Summary endpoint
+	w1, _ := doRequest(t, router, http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/products?q=Phone", nil)
+	if w1.Code != http.StatusOK {
+		t.Fatalf("summary status = %d", w1.Code)
+	}
+	var summaryRaw struct {
+		Data []map[string]any `json:"data"`
+	}
+	if err := json.Unmarshal(w1.Body.Bytes(), &summaryRaw); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if len(summaryRaw.Data) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("expected 1 summary product, got %d", len(summaryRaw.Data))
+	}
+	summaryBrand, ok := summaryRaw.Data[0]["brand"].(string)
+	if !ok || summaryBrand != "Apple" {
+		t.Fatalf("summary brand expected string 'Apple', got %v (%T)", summaryRaw.Data[0]["brand"], summaryRaw.Data[0]["brand"])
+	}
+	summaryCat, ok := summaryRaw.Data[0]["category"].(map[string]any)
+	if !ok || summaryCat["id"] != catElectronics || summaryCat["name"] != "Apple" {
+		t.Fatalf("summary category expected {id, name}, got %v", summaryRaw.Data[0]["category"])
+	}
+
+	// Detail endpoint
+	w2, _ := doRequest(t, router, http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/products/"+productPhone, nil)
+	if w2.Code != http.StatusOK {
+		t.Fatalf("detail status = %d", w2.Code)
+	}
+	var detailRaw struct {
+		Data map[string]any `json:"data"`
+	}
+	if err := json.Unmarshal(w2.Body.Bytes(), &detailRaw); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	detailBrand, ok := detailRaw.Data["brand"].(string)
+	if !ok || detailBrand != "Apple" {
+		t.Fatalf("detail brand expected string 'Apple', got %v (%T)", detailRaw.Data["brand"], detailRaw.Data["brand"])
+	}
+	detailCat, ok := detailRaw.Data["category"].(map[string]any)
+	if !ok || detailCat["id"] != catElectronics || detailCat["name"] != "Apple" {
+		t.Fatalf("detail category expected {id, name}, got %v", detailRaw.Data["category"])
+	}
+}

+func (g *guard) RequireRole(role string) gin.HandlerFunc {
+	return func(c *gin.Context) {
+		currentRole := callerRole(c)
+		if currentRole != role {
+			respondError(c, http.StatusForbidden, "forbidden", "Bạn không có quyền thực hiện thao tác này", nil)
+			c.Abort()
+			return
+		}
+		c.Next()
+	}

new file mode 100644

+++ b/backend/internal/domain/catalog_test.go

+package domain
+
+import (
+	"testing"
+)
+
+func TestProductInStock_WithoutVariants(t *testing.T) {
+	inStockProd := Product{StockQuantity: 5}
+	if !inStockProd.InStock() {
+		t.Errorf("expected product with stock=5 to be in stock")
+	}
+
+	outOfStockProd := Product{StockQuantity: 0}
+	if outOfStockProd.InStock() {
+		t.Errorf("expected product with stock=0 to be out of stock")
+	}
+}
+
+func TestProductInStock_WithVariants(t *testing.T) {
+	tests := []struct {
+		name     string
+		variants []ProductVariant
+		want     bool
+	}{
+		{
+			name: "single active variant with stock > 0",
+			variants: []ProductVariant{
+				{ID: "v-1", Active: true, StockQuantity: 10},
+			},
+			want: true,
+		},
+		{
+			name: "single active variant with stock = 0",
+			variants: []ProductVariant{
+				{ID: "v-1", Active: true, StockQuantity: 0},
+			},
+			want: false,
+		},
+		{
+			name: "single inactive variant with stock > 0",
+			variants: []ProductVariant{
+				{ID: "v-1", Active: false, StockQuantity: 10},
+			},
+			want: false,
+		},
+		{
+			name: "multiple variants - one active in stock, one out of stock",
+			variants: []ProductVariant{
+				{ID: "v-1", Active: true, StockQuantity: 0},
+				{ID: "v-2", Active: true, StockQuantity: 5},
+			},
+			want: true,
+		},
+		{
+			name: "multiple variants - one inactive with stock, one active with 0 stock",
+			variants: []ProductVariant{
+				{ID: "v-1", Active: false, StockQuantity: 10},
+				{ID: "v-2", Active: true, StockQuantity: 0},
+			},
+			want: false,
+		},
+		{
+			name: "multiple variants - all inactive",
+			variants: []ProductVariant{
+				{ID: "v-1", Active: false, StockQuantity: 10},
+				{ID: "v-2", Active: false, StockQuantity: 5},
+			},
+			want: false,
+		},
+	}
+
+	for _, tt := range tests {
+		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
+			p := Product{
+				Variants: tt.variants,
+			}
+			if got := p.InStock(); got != tt.want {
+				t.Errorf("Product.InStock() = %v, want %v", got, tt.want)
+			}
+		})
+	}
+}

+package domain

+func TestUser_Public_IncludesRole(t *testing.T) {
+	u := &User{
+		ID:           "u123",
+		Email:        "admin@example.com",
+		PasswordHash: "hash",
+		Role:         RoleAdmin,
+		CreatedAt:    time.Now(),
+		UpdatedAt:    time.Now(),
+	}
+
+	pub := u.Public()
+	if pub.ID != "u123" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected ID u123, got %s", pub.ID)
+	}
+	if pub.Email != "admin@example.com" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected Email admin@example.com, got %s", pub.Email)
+	}
+	if pub.Role != RoleAdmin {
+		t.Fatalf("expected Role admin, got %s", pub.Role)
+	}
+
+	data, err := json.Marshal(pub)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("json marshal failed: %v", err)
+	}
+	var raw map[string]interface{}
+	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("json unmarshal failed: %v", err)
+	}
+	if raw["role"] != "admin" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected serialized role to be 'admin', got %v", raw["role"])
+	}
+}

+	Begin(context.Context) (pgx.Tx, error)

 func NewCatalogRepository(pool Querier) *CatalogRepository {
-	return &CatalogRepository{q: sqlc.New(pool)}
+	return &CatalogRepository{
+		db: pool,
+		q:  sqlc.New(pool),
+	}
+}
+
+func (r *CatalogRepository) withTx(ctx context.Context, fn func(q *sqlc.Queries) error) error {
+	if starter, ok := r.db.(txStarter); ok {
+		tx, err := starter.Begin(ctx)
+		if err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		defer tx.Rollback(ctx)
+		if err := fn(sqlc.New(tx)); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return tx.Commit(ctx)
+	}
+	return fn(r.q)
 }

+			return nil, err
+		}
+		out = append(out, p)
+	}
+	return out, nil

+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err

+func (r *CatalogRepository) CreateProduct(ctx context.Context, input domain.SaveProductInput) (*domain.Product, error) {
+	currency := input.Currency
+	if currency == "" {
+		currency = "VND"
+	}
+	price := input.Price
+	stockQuantity := input.StockQuantity
+
+	if input.HasVariants && len(input.OptionGroups) > 0 {
+		minP, _, totalStock, err := computeVariantTotals(input.Variants)
+		if err != nil {
+			return nil, err
+		}
+		price = minP
+		stockQuantity = totalStock
+	}
+
+	var productID string
+
+	err := r.withTx(ctx, func(q *sqlc.Queries) error {
+		row, err := q.CreateProduct(ctx, sqlc.CreateProductParams{
+			CategoryID:    input.CategoryID,
+			Name:          input.Name,
+			Description:   input.Description,
+			Price:         toNumeric(price),
+			Currency:      currency,
+			StockQuantity: stockQuantity,
+			Model:         input.Model,
+			Brand:         input.Brand,
+			Featured:      input.Featured,
+		})
+		if err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		productID = row.ID
+
+		for i, url := range input.ImageURLs {
+			if err := q.CreateProductImage(ctx, sqlc.CreateProductImageParams{
+				ProductID: row.ID,
+				Url:       url,
+				Position:  int32(i),
+			}); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+
+		if input.HasVariants && len(input.OptionGroups) > 0 {
+			for _, group := range input.OptionGroups {
+				groupID := group.ID
+				if groupID == "" {
+					groupID = uuid.New().String()
+				}
+				if err := q.CreateProductOptionGroup(ctx, sqlc.CreateProductOptionGroupParams{
+					ID:        groupID,
+					ProductID: row.ID,
+					Label:     group.Label,
+					Position:  int32(group.Position),
+				}); err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+				for _, val := range group.Values {
+					valID := val.ID
+					if valID == "" {
+						valID = uuid.New().String()
+					}
+					if err := q.CreateProductOptionValue(ctx, sqlc.CreateProductOptionValueParams{
+						ID:        valID,
+						ProductID: row.ID,
+						GroupID:   groupID,
+						Label:     val.Label,
+						Position:  int32(val.Position),
+					}); err != nil {
+						return err
+					}
+				}
+			}
+
+			for _, v := range input.Variants {
+				variantID := v.ID
+				if variantID == "" {
+					variantID = uuid.New().String()
+				}
+				if err := q.CreateProductVariant(ctx, sqlc.CreateProductVariantParams{
+					ID:            variantID,
+					ProductID:     row.ID,
+					PriceVnd:      toNumeric(v.PriceVND),
+					StockQuantity: v.StockQuantity,
+					Active:        v.Active,
+				}); err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+				for groupID, valID := range v.OptionValueIDs {
+					if err := q.CreateVariantOptionValue(ctx, sqlc.CreateVariantOptionValueParams{
+						VariantID:     variantID,
+						ProductID:     row.ID,
+						GroupID:       groupID,
+						OptionValueID: valID,
+					}); err != nil {
+						return err
+					}
+				}
+			}
+		}
+
+		return nil
+	})
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+
+	return r.GetAdminProductByID(ctx, productID)
+}

+func (r *CatalogRepository) UpdateProduct(ctx context.Context, id string, input domain.SaveProductInput) (*domain.Product, error) {
+	currency := input.Currency
+	if currency == "" {
+		currency = "VND"
+	}
+	price := input.Price
+	stockQuantity := input.StockQuantity
+
+	if input.HasVariants && len(input.OptionGroups) > 0 {
+		minP, _, totalStock, err := computeVariantTotals(input.Variants)
+		if err != nil {
+			return nil, err
+		}
+		price = minP
+		stockQuantity = totalStock
+	}
+
+	err := r.withTx(ctx, func(q *sqlc.Queries) error {
+		_, err := q.UpdateProduct(ctx, sqlc.UpdateProductParams{
+			ID:            id,
+			CategoryID:    input.CategoryID,
+			Name:          input.Name,
+			Description:   input.Description,
+			Price:         toNumeric(price),
+			Currency:      currency,
+			StockQuantity: stockQuantity,
+			Model:         input.Model,
+			Brand:         input.Brand,
+			Featured:      input.Featured,
+		})
+		if err != nil {
+			if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
+				return domain.ErrProductNotFound
+			}
+			return err
+		}
+
+		if err := q.DeleteProductImages(ctx, id); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for i, url := range input.ImageURLs {
+			if err := q.CreateProductImage(ctx, sqlc.CreateProductImageParams{
+				ProductID: id,
+				Url:       url,
+				Position:  int32(i),
+			}); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+
+		if !input.HasVariants || len(input.OptionGroups) == 0 {
+			if err := q.DeleteVariantOptionValues(ctx, id); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := q.DeleteProductVariants(ctx, id); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := q.DeleteProductOptionValues(ctx, id); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := q.DeleteProductOptionGroups(ctx, id); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		} else {
+			if err := q.DeleteVariantOptionValues(ctx, id); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := q.DeleteProductOptionValues(ctx, id); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := q.DeleteProductOptionGroups(ctx, id); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+
+			existingVariants, err := q.ListProductVariants(ctx, id)
+			if err != nil && !errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
+				return err
+			}
+			existingMap := make(map[string]bool, len(existingVariants))
+			for _, ev := range existingVariants {
+				existingMap[ev.ID] = true
+			}
+			incomingMap := make(map[string]bool, len(input.Variants))
+			for _, v := range input.Variants {
+				if v.ID != "" {
+					incomingMap[v.ID] = true
+				}
+			}
+
+			for _, ev := range existingVariants {
+				if !incomingMap[ev.ID] {
+					if err := q.DeleteProductVariant(ctx, sqlc.DeleteProductVariantParams{
+						ID:        ev.ID,
+						ProductID: id,
+					}); err != nil {
+						return err
+					}
+				}
+			}
+
+			for i, v := range input.Variants {
+				variantID := v.ID
+				if variantID == "" {
+					variantID = uuid.New().String()
+					input.Variants[i].ID = variantID
+				}
+				if existingMap[variantID] {
+					if err := q.UpdateProductVariant(ctx, sqlc.UpdateProductVariantParams{
+						ID:            variantID,
+						ProductID:     id,
+						PriceVnd:      toNumeric(v.PriceVND),
+						StockQuantity: v.StockQuantity,
+						Active:        v.Active,
+					}); err != nil {
+						return err
+					}
+				} else {
+					if err := q.CreateProductVariant(ctx, sqlc.CreateProductVariantParams{
+						ID:            variantID,
+						ProductID:     id,
+						PriceVnd:      toNumeric(v.PriceVND),
+						StockQuantity: v.StockQuantity,
+						Active:        v.Active,
+					}); err != nil {
+						return err
+					}
+				}
+			}
+
+			for _, group := range input.OptionGroups {
+				groupID := group.ID
+				if groupID == "" {
+					groupID = uuid.New().String()
+				}
+				if err := q.CreateProductOptionGroup(ctx, sqlc.CreateProductOptionGroupParams{
+					ID:        groupID,
+					ProductID: id,
+					Label:     group.Label,
+					Position:  int32(group.Position),
+				}); err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+				for _, val := range group.Values {
+					valID := val.ID
+					if valID == "" {
+						valID = uuid.New().String()
+					}
+					if err := q.CreateProductOptionValue(ctx, sqlc.CreateProductOptionValueParams{
+						ID:        valID,
+						ProductID: id,
+						GroupID:   groupID,
+						Label:     val.Label,
+						Position:  int32(val.Position),
+					}); err != nil {
+						return err
+					}
+				}
+			}
+
+			for _, v := range input.Variants {
+				for groupID, valID := range v.OptionValueIDs {
+					if err := q.CreateVariantOptionValue(ctx, sqlc.CreateVariantOptionValueParams{
+						VariantID:     v.ID,
+						ProductID:     id,
+						GroupID:       groupID,
+						OptionValueID: valID,
+					}); err != nil {
+						return err
+					}
+				}
+			}
+		}
+
+		return nil
+	})
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+
+	return r.GetAdminProductByID(ctx, id)
+}
+
+func (r *CatalogRepository) DeleteProduct(ctx context.Context, id string) error {
+	_, err := r.q.SoftDeleteProduct(ctx, id)
+	if err != nil {
+		if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
+			return domain.ErrProductNotFound
+		}
+		return err
+	}
+	return nil
+}

+func computeVariantTotals(variants []domain.VariantInput) (minPrice string, maxPrice string, totalStock int64, err error) {
+	var minActive, maxActive *big.Int
+	var minAll, maxAll *big.Int
+	var activeStock int64
+
+	for _, v := range variants {
+		priceStr := v.PriceVND
+		if priceStr == "" {
+			return "", "", 0, fmt.Errorf("variant price cannot be empty")
+		}
+		p, ok := new(big.Int).SetString(priceStr, 10)
+		if !ok || p.Sign() < 0 {
+			return "", "", 0, fmt.Errorf("invalid VND price %q", priceStr)
+		}
+		if minAll == nil || p.Cmp(minAll) < 0 {
+			minAll = p
+		}
+		if maxAll == nil || p.Cmp(maxAll) > 0 {
+			maxAll = p
+		}
+		if v.Active {
+			if minActive == nil || p.Cmp(minActive) < 0 {
+				minActive = p
+			}
+			if maxActive == nil || p.Cmp(maxActive) > 0 {
+				maxActive = p
+			}
+			activeStock += v.StockQuantity
+		}
+	}
+
+	if minActive != nil {
+		return minActive.String(), maxActive.String(), activeStock, nil
+	}
+	if minAll != nil {
+		return minAll.String(), maxAll.String(), 0, nil
+	}
+	return "0", "0", 0, nil
+}

 		return pgtype.Numeric{}

+		if err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT price::text, stock_quantity::text FROM products WHERE id=$1`, created.ID).Scan(&dbPrice, &dbStock); err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		if dbPrice != "28990000" || dbStock != "15" {
+			t.Fatalf("db product row mismatch: price=%s stock=%s", dbPrice, dbStock)
+		}
+
+		// 2. Update existing product variants
+		updateInput := input
+		updateInput.Name = "iPhone 15 Pro Max"
+		updateInput.Variants[0].PriceVND = "27990000"
+		updateInput.Variants[0].StockQuantity = 8
+		updateInput.Variants[1].Active = false // now inactive
+
+		updated, err := repo.UpdateProduct(ctx, created.ID, updateInput)
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatalf("UpdateProduct failed: %v", err)
+		}
+		if updated.Name != "iPhone 15 Pro Max" || updated.Price != "27990000" || updated.MinPriceVND != "27990000" || updated.MaxPriceVND != "28990000" {
+			t.Fatalf("updated product range mismatch: min=%s max=%s price=%s", updated.MinPriceVND, updated.MaxPriceVND, updated.Price)
+		}
+		if updated.StockQuantity != 8 {
+			t.Fatalf("expected updated total active stock = 8, got %d", updated.StockQuantity)
+		}
+
+		// 3. Switch to simple product (remove all variants)
+		simpleInput := domain.SaveProductInput{
+			Name:          "iPhone 15 Pro Max Simple",
+			Model:         "IP15PM",
+			Brand:         "Apple",
+			Description:   "Now a simple product",
+			CategoryID:    integrationApple,
+			Price:         "29990000",
+			StockQuantity: 100,
+			HasVariants:   false,
+		}
+
+		updatedSimple, err := repo.UpdateProduct(ctx, created.ID, simpleInput)
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatalf("UpdateProduct simple switch failed: %v", err)
+		}
+		if updatedSimple.Price != "29990000" || updatedSimple.StockQuantity != 100 {
+			t.Fatalf("simple product price/stock mismatch: price=%s stock=%d", updatedSimple.Price, updatedSimple.StockQuantity)
+		}
+		if len(updatedSimple.OptionGroups) != 0 || len(updatedSimple.Variants) != 0 {
+			t.Fatalf("expected 0 option groups and 0 variants, got groups=%d variants=%d", len(updatedSimple.OptionGroups), len(updatedSimple.Variants))
+		}
+
+		// Verify database tables are cleaned
+		var grpCount, valCount, varCount, linkCount int
+		if err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT count(*) FROM product_option_groups WHERE product_id=$1`, created.ID).Scan(&grpCount); err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		if err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT count(*) FROM product_option_values WHERE product_id=$1`, created.ID).Scan(&valCount); err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		if err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT count(*) FROM product_variants WHERE product_id=$1`, created.ID).Scan(&varCount); err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		if err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT count(*) FROM variant_option_values WHERE product_id=$1`, created.ID).Scan(&linkCount); err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		if grpCount != 0 || valCount != 0 || varCount != 0 || linkCount != 0 {
+			t.Fatalf("expected all variant tables to be empty, got groups=%d values=%d variants=%d links=%d", grpCount, valCount, varCount, linkCount)
+		}
+	})
 }

+func TestComputeVariantTotals(t *testing.T) {
+	t.Run("all active variants", func(t *testing.T) {
+		variants := []domain.VariantInput{
+			{PriceVND: "1000000", StockQuantity: 5, Active: true},
+			{PriceVND: "2000000", StockQuantity: 10, Active: true},
+			{PriceVND: "1500000", StockQuantity: 3, Active: true},
+		}
+		minP, maxP, totalStock, err := computeVariantTotals(variants)
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		if minP != "1000000" || maxP != "2000000" || totalStock != 18 {
+			t.Fatalf("unexpected totals: min=%s max=%s stock=%d", minP, maxP, totalStock)
+		}
+	})
+
+	t.Run("mixed active and inactive", func(t *testing.T) {
+		variants := []domain.VariantInput{
+			{PriceVND: "500000", StockQuantity: 50, Active: false},
+			{PriceVND: "1000000", StockQuantity: 5, Active: true},
+			{PriceVND: "3000000", StockQuantity: 2, Active: true},
+		}
+		minP, maxP, totalStock, err := computeVariantTotals(variants)
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		if minP != "1000000" || maxP != "3000000" || totalStock != 7 {
+			t.Fatalf("unexpected totals: min=%s max=%s stock=%d", minP, maxP, totalStock)
+		}
+	})
+
+	t.Run("all inactive fallback", func(t *testing.T) {
+		variants := []domain.VariantInput{
+			{PriceVND: "1000000", StockQuantity: 5, Active: false},
+			{PriceVND: "3000000", StockQuantity: 2, Active: false},
+		}
+		minP, maxP, totalStock, err := computeVariantTotals(variants)
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		if minP != "1000000" || maxP != "3000000" || totalStock != 0 {
+			t.Fatalf("unexpected totals: min=%s max=%s stock=%d", minP, maxP, totalStock)
+		}
+	})
+
+	t.Run("empty variants", func(t *testing.T) {
+		minP, maxP, totalStock, err := computeVariantTotals(nil)
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		if minP != "0" || maxP != "0" || totalStock != 0 {
+			t.Fatalf("unexpected totals: min=%s max=%s stock=%d", minP, maxP, totalStock)
+		}
+	})
+
+	t.Run("invalid price error", func(t *testing.T) {
+		variants := []domain.VariantInput{
+			{PriceVND: "invalid", StockQuantity: 5, Active: true},
+		}
+		_, _, _, err := computeVariantTotals(variants)
+		if err == nil {
+			t.Fatal("expected error on invalid price")
+		}
+	})
+}

+type CreateProductParams struct {

+	Currency      string

+	Brand         string

+type CreateProductRow struct {

+	Currency      string

+	Model         string

+const listAdminProducts = `-- name: ListAdminProducts :many
+SELECT p.id,
+       p.name,
+       p.model,
+       p.brand,
+       p.description,
+       p.price::text AS price,
+       p.currency,
+       p.stock_quantity,
+       c.id AS category_id,
+       c.name AS category_name,
+       p.featured,
+       p.active,
+       p.created_at,
+       p.updated_at,
+       COALESCE((SELECT pi.url
+        FROM product_images pi
+        WHERE pi.product_id = p.id
+        ORDER BY pi.position ASC, pi.id ASC
+        LIMIT 1), '')::text AS primary_image_url
+FROM products p
+JOIN categories c ON c.id = p.category_id
+WHERE p.active
+  AND ($1::text IS NULL OR lower(c.name) LIKE '%' || lower($1::text) || '%'
+       OR lower(p.brand) LIKE '%' || lower($1::text) || '%'
+       OR lower(p.name) LIKE '%' || lower($1::text) || '%'
+       OR lower(p.model) LIKE '%' || lower($1::text) || '%')
+ORDER BY p.name ASC, p.model ASC, p.id ASC
+`
+
+type ListAdminProductsRow struct {
+	ID              string
+	Name            string
+	Model           string
+	Brand           string
+	Description     string
+	Price           string
+	Currency        string
+	StockQuantity   int64
+	CategoryID      string
+	CategoryName    string
+	Featured        bool
+	Active          bool
+	CreatedAt       time.Time
+	UpdatedAt       time.Time
+	PrimaryImageUrl string
+}
+
+func (q *Queries) ListAdminProducts(ctx context.Context, q_ pgtype.Text) ([]ListAdminProductsRow, error) {
+	rows, err := q.db.Query(ctx, listAdminProducts, q_)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	defer rows.Close()
+	var items []ListAdminProductsRow
+	for rows.Next() {
+		var i ListAdminProductsRow
+		if err := rows.Scan(
+			&i.ID,
+			&i.Name,
+			&i.Model,
+			&i.Brand,
+			&i.Description,
+			&i.Price,
+			&i.Currency,
+			&i.StockQuantity,
+			&i.CategoryID,
+			&i.CategoryName,
+			&i.Featured,
+			&i.Active,
+			&i.CreatedAt,
+			&i.UpdatedAt,
+			&i.PrimaryImageUrl,
+		); err != nil {
+			return nil, err
+		}
+		items = append(items, i)
+	}
+	if err := rows.Err(); err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	return items, nil
+}

+package jwt
+
+import (
+	"testing"
+	"time"
+)
+
+func TestJWT_GenerateAndParseWithRole(t *testing.T) {
+	secret := "a-secret-that-is-at-least-thirty-two-characters"
+	mgr := NewManager(secret, "test-issuer")
+
+	token, err := mgr.GenerateAccessToken("user-1", "admin", time.Minute)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	userID, role, err := mgr.ParseAccessToken(token)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("unexpected error parsing token: %v", err)
+	}
+	if userID != "user-1" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected userID user-1, got %s", userID)
+	}
+	if role != "admin" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected role admin, got %s", role)
+	}
+}
+
+func TestJWT_ParseInvalidToken(t *testing.T) {
+	secret := "a-secret-that-is-at-least-thirty-two-characters"
+	mgr := NewManager(secret, "test-issuer")
+
+	_, _, err := mgr.ParseAccessToken("invalid-token")
+	if err == nil {
+		t.Fatal("expected error for invalid token")
+	}
+}

diff --git a/backend/internal/usecase/auth/bootstrap.go b/backend/internal/usecase/auth/bootstrap.go

+package auth
+
+import (
+	"context"
+	"errors"
+	"strings"
+
+	"commerce-backend/internal/domain"
+)
+
+func (uc *UseCase) BootstrapAdmin(ctx context.Context, email, password string) error {
+	email = strings.TrimSpace(email)
+	password = strings.TrimSpace(password)
+	if email == "" || password == "" {
+		return nil
+	}
+
+	count, err := uc.repos.Users.CountByRole(ctx, domain.RoleAdmin)
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	if count > 0 {
+		return nil
+	}
+
+	existing, err := uc.repos.Users.GetByEmail(ctx, email)
+	if err != nil && !errors.Is(err, domain.ErrNotFound) {
+		return err
+	}
+	if existing != nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+
+	passwordHash, err := uc.hashPassword(password)
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	_, err = uc.repos.Users.Create(ctx, email, passwordHash, domain.RoleAdmin)
+	if err != nil && !errors.Is(err, domain.ErrEmailInUse) {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	return nil
+}

+package auth
+
+import (
+	"context"
+	"testing"
+
+	"commerce-backend/internal/domain"
+)
+
+func TestBootstrapAdmin_Success(t *testing.T) {
+	uc, users := newTestEnv(t)
+
+	err := uc.BootstrapAdmin(context.Background(), "admin@example.com", "adminpassword123")
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	u, err := users.GetByEmail(context.Background(), "admin@example.com")
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("failed to find bootstrapped admin: %v", err)
+	}
+	if u.Role != domain.RoleAdmin {
+		t.Fatalf("expected role %s, got %s", domain.RoleAdmin, u.Role)
+	}
+
+	// Verify the admin can login
+	sess, err := uc.Login(context.Background(), "admin@example.com", "adminpassword123")
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("login as bootstrapped admin failed: %v", err)
+	}
+	if sess.User.Role != domain.RoleAdmin {
+		t.Fatalf("expected session role %s, got %s", domain.RoleAdmin, sess.User.Role)
+	}
+}
+
+func TestBootstrapAdmin_Idempotent_ReRunningSameConfig(t *testing.T) {
+	uc, users := newTestEnv(t)
+
+	err := uc.BootstrapAdmin(context.Background(), "admin@example.com", "adminpassword123")
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("first bootstrap error: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	// Re-running the same config does not error or create duplicate
+	err = uc.BootstrapAdmin(context.Background(), "admin@example.com", "adminpassword123")
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("second bootstrap error: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	count, err := users.CountByRole(context.Background(), domain.RoleAdmin)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("count error: %v", err)
+	}
+	if count != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("expected exactly 1 admin, got %d", count)
+	}
+}
+
+func TestBootstrapAdmin_AdminAlreadyExists(t *testing.T) {
+	uc, users := newTestEnv(t)
+
+	// Create an existing admin first
+	_, err := users.Create(context.Background(), "existing_admin@example.com", "hash", domain.RoleAdmin)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("failed to create existing admin: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	// Bootstrap with different credentials
+	err = uc.BootstrapAdmin(context.Background(), "new_admin@example.com", "adminpassword123")
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("bootstrap error: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	// Should not create new admin since an admin already exists
+	count, err := users.CountByRole(context.Background(), domain.RoleAdmin)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("count error: %v", err)
+	}
+	if count != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("expected exactly 1 admin, got %d", count)
+	}
+	_, err = users.GetByEmail(context.Background(), "new_admin@example.com")
+	if err != domain.ErrNotFound {
+		t.Fatalf("expected ErrNotFound for new_admin, got %v", err)
+	}
+}
+
+func TestBootstrapAdmin_EmptyCredentials(t *testing.T) {
+	uc, users := newTestEnv(t)
+
+	if err := uc.BootstrapAdmin(context.Background(), "", "password"); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("unexpected error on empty email: %v", err)
+	}
+	if err := uc.BootstrapAdmin(context.Background(), "admin@example.com", ""); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("unexpected error on empty password: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	count, _ := users.CountByRole(context.Background(), domain.RoleAdmin)
+	if count != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("expected 0 admins, got %d", count)
+	}
+}

+func (uc *UseCase) CreateProduct(ctx context.Context, input domain.SaveProductInput) (*domain.Product, error) {
+	if err := uc.validateSaveProductInput(&input); err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	_, err := uc.catalog.GetCategoryByID(ctx, input.CategoryID)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, domain.NewValidationError(map[string]string{"categoryId": "Danh mục không tồn tại"})
+	}
+	return uc.catalog.CreateProduct(ctx, input)
+}

+func (uc *UseCase) UpdateProduct(ctx context.Context, id string, input domain.SaveProductInput) (*domain.Product, error) {
+	if !uuidPattern.MatchString(id) {
+		return nil, domain.ErrInvalidProductID
+	}
+	if err := uc.validateSaveProductInput(&input); err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	_, err := uc.catalog.GetCategoryByID(ctx, input.CategoryID)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, domain.NewValidationError(map[string]string{"categoryId": "Danh mục không tồn tại"})
+	}

+	if !uuidPattern.MatchString(id) {
+		return domain.ErrInvalidProductID
+	}
+	return uc.catalog.DeleteProduct(ctx, id)
