+func (uc *UseCase) validateSaveProductInput(input *domain.SaveProductInput) error {
+	input.Name = strings.TrimSpace(input.Name)
+	input.Model = strings.TrimSpace(input.Model)
+	input.Brand = strings.TrimSpace(input.Brand)
+	input.Description = strings.TrimSpace(input.Description)
+	input.CategoryID = strings.TrimSpace(input.CategoryID)
+	input.Price = strings.TrimSpace(input.Price)
+	input.Currency = strings.TrimSpace(input.Currency)
+	if input.Currency == "" {
+		input.Currency = "VND"
+	}
+
+	fields := map[string]string{}
+	if input.Name == "" {
+		fields["name"] = "Tên sản phẩm không được để trống"
+	}
+	if input.CategoryID == "" || !uuidPattern.MatchString(input.CategoryID) {
+		fields["categoryId"] = "Danh mục không hợp lệ"
+	}
+
+	if !input.HasVariants {
+		if input.Price == "" {
+			fields["price"] = "Giá sản phẩm không được để trống"
+		} else if !validPriceInput(input.Price) {
+			fields["price"] = "Giá sản phẩm phải là số không âm"
+		}
+		if input.StockQuantity < 0 {
+			fields["stockQuantity"] = "Số lượng tồn kho không được âm"
+		}
+	} else {
+		if len(input.OptionGroups) == 0 {
+			fields["optionGroups"] = "Phải có ít nhất một nhóm thuộc tính"
+		} else {
+			for i, group := range input.OptionGroups {
+				trimmedLabel := strings.TrimSpace(group.Label)
+				input.OptionGroups[i].Label = trimmedLabel
+				if trimmedLabel == "" {
+					fields[fmt.Sprintf("optionGroups[%d].label", i)] = "Tên nhóm thuộc tính không được để trống"
+				}
+				if len(group.Values) == 0 {
+					fields[fmt.Sprintf("optionGroups[%d].values", i)] = "Nhóm thuộc tính phải có ít nhất một giá trị"
+				} else {
+					seenVals := make(map[string]bool)
+					hasDup := false
+					for j, val := range group.Values {
+						trimmedVal := strings.TrimSpace(val.Label)
+						input.OptionGroups[i].Values[j].Label = trimmedVal
+						if trimmedVal != "" {
+							lower := strings.ToLower(trimmedVal)
+							if seenVals[lower] {
+								hasDup = true
+							}
+							seenVals[lower] = true
+						}
+					}
+					if hasDup {
+						fields[fmt.Sprintf("optionGroups[%d].values", i)] = "Giá trị thuộc tính không được trùng nhau"
+					}
+				}
+			}
+		}
+
+		if len(input.OptionGroups) > 0 && len(input.Variants) == 0 {
+			fields["variants"] = "Phải có ít nhất một biến thể"
+		} else {
+			for i, variant := range input.Variants {
+				trimmedPrice := strings.TrimSpace(variant.PriceVND)
+				input.Variants[i].PriceVND = trimmedPrice
+				if trimmedPrice == "" || !validPriceInput(trimmedPrice) {
+					fields[fmt.Sprintf("variants[%d].priceVnd", i)] = "Giá biến thể phải là số không âm"
+				}
+				if variant.StockQuantity < 0 {
+					fields[fmt.Sprintf("variants[%d].stockQuantity", i)] = "Số lượng tồn kho không được âm"
+				}
+			}
+		}
+
+		if input.Price != "" && !validPriceInput(input.Price) {
+			fields["price"] = "Giá sản phẩm phải là số không âm"
+		}
+		if input.StockQuantity < 0 {
+			fields["stockQuantity"] = "Số lượng tồn kho không được âm"
+		}
+	}
+
+	if len(fields) > 0 {
+		return domain.NewValidationError(fields)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func validPriceInput(s string) bool {
+	if s == "" {
+		return false
+	}
+	if domain.MoneyVND(s).IsCanonical() {
+		return true
+	}
+	return regexp.MustCompile(`^(?:[0-9]+|[0-9]*\.[0-9]{1,2})$`).MatchString(s)
+}
+
 func validMoney(s string) bool {
 	return len(s) <= 38 && domain.MoneyVND(s).IsCanonical()
 }

+		Active:   true,
+	})
+	repo.AddProduct(domain.Product{
+		ID:       "bbbbbbbb-bbbb-bbbb-bbbb-bbbbbbbbbbbb",
+		Name:     "MacBook Pro",
+		Model:    "M3 Max",
+		Brand:    "Apple",
+		Category: domain.Category{ID: "22222222-2222-2222-2222-222222222222", Name: "Máy tính"},
+		Active:   true,
+	})
+
+	// Search by name
+	res, err := uc.ListAdminProducts(ctx, "iPhone")
+	if err != nil || len(res) != 1 || res[0].ID != "aaaaaaaa-aaaa-aaaa-aaaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa" {
+		t.Fatalf("failed search by name: %v, len: %d", err, len(res))
+	}
+
+	// Search by category name
+	res, err = uc.ListAdminProducts(ctx, "Máy tính")
+	if err != nil || len(res) != 1 || res[0].ID != "bbbbbbbb-bbbb-bbbb-bbbb-bbbbbbbbbbbb" {
+		t.Fatalf("failed search by category: %v, len: %d", err, len(res))
+	}
+
+	// Search by brand
+	res, err = uc.ListAdminProducts(ctx, "apple")
+	if err != nil || len(res) != 2 {
+		t.Fatalf("failed search by brand: %v, len: %d", err, len(res))
+	}

+func TestAdminUseCase_CreateProduct_Validations(t *testing.T) {
+	uc, _ := setupAdminUseCase()
+	ctx := context.Background()
+
+	tests := []struct {
+		name          string
+		input         domain.SaveProductInput
+		expectedField string
+	}{
+		{
+			name: "missing name",
+			input: domain.SaveProductInput{
+				Name:       "",
+				CategoryID: "11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111",
+				Price:      "100000",
+			},
+			expectedField: "name",
+		},
+		{
+			name: "invalid category UUID",
+			input: domain.SaveProductInput{
+				Name:       "Test",
+				CategoryID: "not-uuid",
+				Price:      "100000",
+			},
+			expectedField: "categoryId",
+		},
+		{
+			name: "invalid price format",
+			input: domain.SaveProductInput{
+				Name:       "Test",
+				CategoryID: "11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111",
+				Price:      "-5000",
+			},
+			expectedField: "price",
+		},
+		{
+			name: "negative stock quantity",
+			input: domain.SaveProductInput{
+				Name:          "Test",
+				CategoryID:    "11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111",
+				Price:         "100000",
+				StockQuantity: -1,
+			},
+			expectedField: "stockQuantity",
+		},
+	}
+
+	for _, tt := range tests {
+		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
+			_, err := uc.CreateProduct(ctx, tt.input)
+			if err == nil {
+				t.Fatalf("expected validation error, got nil")
+			}
+			var valErr *domain.ValidationError
+			if !errors.As(err, &valErr) {
+				t.Fatalf("expected domain.ValidationError, got %v", err)
+			}
+			if valErr.Fields[tt.expectedField] == "" {
+				t.Fatalf("expected error on field %q, got fields: %+v", tt.expectedField, valErr.Fields)
+			}
+		})
+	}
+}
+

+		Price:         "20000",
+		Currency:      "VND",
+		StockQuantity: 10,
+		Featured:      true,
+		ImageURLs:     []string{"https://example.com/updated.jpg"},
+	}
+
+	updated, err := uc.UpdateProduct(ctx, id, input)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
+	}
+	if updated.Name != "New Name" || updated.Brand != "New Brand" || updated.Category.ID != "22222222-2222-2222-2222-222222222222" {
+		t.Errorf("mismatched updated fields: %+v", updated)
+	}
+	if !updated.Active {
+		t.Errorf("expected product to stay active")
+	}

+func TestAdminUseCase_DeleteProduct_SoftDelete(t *testing.T) {
+	uc, repo := setupAdminUseCase()
+	ctx := context.Background()
+
+	id := "aaaaaaaa-aaaa-aaaa-aaaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa"
+	repo.AddProduct(domain.Product{
+		ID:       id,
+		Name:     "To Delete",
+		Category: domain.Category{ID: "11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111", Name: "Điện thoại"},
+		Active:   true,
+	})
+
+	// Delete the product
+	err := uc.DeleteProduct(ctx, id)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("unexpected error deleting product: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	// Should not be retrievable by ID
+	_, err = uc.GetAdminProduct(ctx, id)
+	if !errors.Is(err, domain.ErrProductNotFound) {
+		t.Fatalf("expected ErrProductNotFound after delete, got %v", err)
+	}
+
+	// Should not appear in admin list
+	list, err := uc.ListAdminProducts(ctx, "")
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("unexpected error listing products: %v", err)
+	}
+	if len(list) != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("expected 0 products in list after soft delete, got %d", len(list))
+	}
+}

+func TestAdminUseCase_CreateProduct_WithVariants_Success(t *testing.T) {
+	uc, _ := setupAdminUseCase()
+	ctx := context.Background()
+
+	input := domain.SaveProductInput{
+		Name:        "iPhone 15 Pro Max",
+		Model:       "A3106",
+		Brand:       "Apple",
+		Description: "Flagship iPhone with titanium design",
+		CategoryID:  "11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111",
+		Currency:    "VND",
+		Featured:    true,
+		ImageURLs:   []string{"https://example.com/ip15_1.jpg"},
+		HasVariants: true,
+		OptionGroups: []domain.OptionGroupInput{
+			{
+				ID:       "group-color",
+				Label:    "Màu sắc",
+				Position: 0,
+				Values: []domain.OptionValueInput{
+					{ID: "val-color-natural", Label: "Titan Tự Nhiên", Position: 0},
+					{ID: "val-color-black", Label: "Titan Đen", Position: 1},
+				},
+			},
+			{
+				ID:       "group-storage",
+				Label:    "Dung lượng",
+				Position: 1,
+				Values: []domain.OptionValueInput{
+					{ID: "val-storage-256", Label: "256GB", Position: 0},
+					{ID: "val-storage-512", Label: "512GB", Position: 1},
+				},
+			},
+		},
+		Variants: []domain.VariantInput{
+			{
+				ID:             "var-natural-256",
+				OptionValueIDs: map[string]string{"group-color": "val-color-natural", "group-storage": "val-storage-256"},
+				PriceVND:       "29990000",
+				StockQuantity:  10,
+				Active:         true,
+			},
+			{
+				ID:             "var-natural-512",
+				OptionValueIDs: map[string]string{"group-color": "val-color-natural", "group-storage": "val-storage-512"},
+				PriceVND:       "34990000",
+				StockQuantity:  5,
+				Active:         true,
+			},
+			{
+				ID:             "var-black-256",
+				OptionValueIDs: map[string]string{"group-color": "val-color-black", "group-storage": "val-storage-256"},
+				PriceVND:       "29990000",
+				StockQuantity:  8,
+				Active:         true,
+			},
+			{
+				ID:             "var-black-512",
+				OptionValueIDs: map[string]string{"group-color": "val-color-black", "group-storage": "val-storage-512"},
+				PriceVND:       "34990000",
+				StockQuantity:  0,
+				Active:         false,
+			},
+		},
+	}
+
+	created, err := uc.CreateProduct(ctx, input)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("unexpected error creating product with variants: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	if len(created.OptionGroups) != 2 {
+		t.Fatalf("expected 2 option groups, got %d", len(created.OptionGroups))
+	}
+	if len(created.Variants) != 4 {
+		t.Fatalf("expected 4 variants, got %d", len(created.Variants))
+	}
+	if created.Price != "29990000" {
+		t.Errorf("expected computed price 29990000, got %s", created.Price)
+	}
+	if created.StockQuantity != 23 {
+		t.Errorf("expected active stock sum 23, got %d", created.StockQuantity)
+	}
+}

+func TestAdminUseCase_CreateProduct_VariantValidations(t *testing.T) {

+						Values: []domain.OptionValueInput{
+							{Label: "Đen"},
+						},
+					},
+				},
+				Variants: []domain.VariantInput{
+					{PriceVND: "50000", StockQuantity: -2, Active: true},
+				},
+			},
+			expectedField: "variants[0].stockQuantity",
+			expectedMsg:   "Số lượng tồn kho không được âm",
+		},
+	}
+
+	for _, tt := range tests {
+		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
+			_, err := uc.CreateProduct(ctx, tt.input)
+			if err == nil {
+				t.Fatalf("expected validation error, got nil")
+			}
+			var valErr *domain.ValidationError
+			if !errors.As(err, &valErr) {
+				t.Fatalf("expected domain.ValidationError, got %v", err)
+			}
+			msg, ok := valErr.Fields[tt.expectedField]
+			if !ok {
+				t.Fatalf("expected error field %q, got fields: %+v", tt.expectedField, valErr.Fields)
+			}
+			if msg != tt.expectedMsg {
+				t.Errorf("expected message %q, got %q", tt.expectedMsg, msg)
+			}
+		})
+	}
+}

+func TestAdminUseCase_UpdateProduct_WithVariants_Success(t *testing.T) {
+	uc, repo := setupAdminUseCase()
+	ctx := context.Background()
+
+	id := "aaaaaaaa-aaaa-aaaa-aaaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa"
+	repo.AddProduct(domain.Product{
+		ID:            id,
+		Name:          "Old Product",
+		Category:      domain.Category{ID: "11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111", Name: "Điện thoại"},
+		Price:         "10000000",
+		StockQuantity: 10,
+		Active:        true,
+	})
+
+	input := domain.SaveProductInput{
+		Name:        "Updated Multi-Variant Phone",
+		Model:       "V2",
+		Brand:       "Apple",
+		CategoryID:  "11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111",
+		HasVariants: true,
+		OptionGroups: []domain.OptionGroupInput{
+			{
+				ID:       "grp-1",
+				Label:    "Bộ nhớ",
+				Position: 0,
+				Values: []domain.OptionValueInput{
+					{ID: "val-1", Label: "128GB"},
+				},
+			},
+		},
+		Variants: []domain.VariantInput{
+			{
+				ID:             "var-1",
+				OptionValueIDs: map[string]string{"grp-1": "val-1"},
+				PriceVND:       "15000000",
+				StockQuantity:  20,
+				Active:         true,
+			},
+		},
+	}
+
+	updated, err := uc.UpdateProduct(ctx, id, input)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("unexpected error updating product with variants: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	if len(updated.OptionGroups) != 1 || len(updated.Variants) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("expected updated option groups and variants, got groups=%d, variants=%d", len(updated.OptionGroups), len(updated.Variants))
+	}
+	if updated.Price != "15000000" || updated.StockQuantity != 20 {
+		t.Errorf("expected price 15000000 and stock 20, got price=%s stock=%d", updated.Price, updated.StockQuantity)
+	}
+}

+func (f *Catalog) GetCategoryByID(ctx context.Context, id string) (*domain.Category, error) {
+	f.mu.Lock()
+	defer f.mu.Unlock()
+	for _, c := range f.categories {
+		if c.ID == id {
+			res := c
+			return &res, nil
+		}
+	}
+	return nil, domain.ErrNotFound
+}
+
+func (f *Catalog) ListAdminProducts(ctx context.Context, query string) ([]domain.Product, error) {
+	f.mu.Lock()
+	defer f.mu.Unlock()
+
+	q := strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(query))
+	matches := make([]domain.Product, 0, len(f.products))
+	for _, p := range f.products {
+		if !p.Active {
+			continue
+		}
+		if q != "" && !strings.Contains(strings.ToLower(p.Name), q) &&
+			!strings.Contains(strings.ToLower(p.Model), q) &&
+			!strings.Contains(strings.ToLower(p.Brand), q) &&
+			!strings.Contains(strings.ToLower(p.Category.Name), q) {
+			continue
+		}
+		matches = append(matches, cloneAdminProduct(*p))
+	}
+	sort.Slice(matches, func(i, j int) bool {
+		if matches[i].Name != matches[j].Name {
+			return matches[i].Name < matches[j].Name
+		}
+		return matches[i].ID < matches[j].ID
+	})
+	return matches, nil
+}
+
+func (f *Catalog) GetAdminProductByID(ctx context.Context, id string) (*domain.Product, error) {
+	f.mu.Lock()
+	defer f.mu.Unlock()
+	for _, p := range f.products {
+		if p.ID == id && p.Active {
+			out := cloneAdminProduct(*p)
+			return &out, nil
+		}
+	}
+	return nil, domain.ErrProductNotFound
+}
+
+func (f *Catalog) CreateProduct(ctx context.Context, input domain.SaveProductInput) (*domain.Product, error) {
+	f.mu.Lock()
+	defer f.mu.Unlock()
+
+	var matchedCategory *domain.Category
+	for _, c := range f.categories {
+		if c.ID == input.CategoryID {
+			res := c
+			matchedCategory = &res
+			break
+		}
+	}
+	if matchedCategory == nil {
+		return nil, domain.NewValidationError(map[string]string{"categoryId": "Danh mục không tồn tại"})
+	}
+
+	id := uuid.New().String()
+	now := time.Now()
+	currency := input.Currency
+	if currency == "" {
+		currency = "VND"
+	}
+
+	var optionGroups []domain.OptionGroup
+	var variants []domain.ProductVariant
+	price := input.Price
+	stockQuantity := input.StockQuantity
+
+	if input.HasVariants && len(input.OptionGroups) > 0 {
+		for _, g := range input.OptionGroups {
+			gid := g.ID
+			if gid == "" {
+				gid = uuid.New().String()
+			}
+			vals := make([]domain.OptionValue, 0, len(g.Values))
+			for _, v := range g.Values {
+				vid := v.ID
+				if vid == "" {
+					vid = uuid.New().String()
+				}
+				vals = append(vals, domain.OptionValue{ID: vid, Label: v.Label})
+			}
+			optionGroups = append(optionGroups, domain.OptionGroup{
+				ID:       gid,
+				Label:    g.Label,
+				Position: g.Position,
+				Values:   vals,
+			})
+		}
+		var minActive, maxActive *big.Int
+		var minAll, maxAll *big.Int
+		var activeStock int64
+		for _, v := range input.Variants {
+			varID := v.ID
+			if varID == "" {
+				varID = uuid.New().String()
+			}
+			optIDs := make(map[string]string, len(v.OptionValueIDs))
+			for gid, vid := range v.OptionValueIDs {
+				optIDs[gid] = vid
+			}
+			variants = append(variants, domain.ProductVariant{
+				ID:             varID,
+				OptionValueIDs: optIDs,
+				PriceVND:       domain.MoneyVND(v.PriceVND),
+				StockQuantity:  v.StockQuantity,
+				Active:         v.Active,
+			})
+			pInt, ok := new(big.Int).SetString(v.PriceVND, 10)
+			if ok {
+				if minAll == nil || pInt.Cmp(minAll) < 0 {
+					minAll = pInt
+				}
+				if maxAll == nil || pInt.Cmp(maxAll) > 0 {
+					maxAll = pInt
+				}
+				if v.Active {
+					if minActive == nil || pInt.Cmp(minActive) < 0 {
+						minActive = pInt
+					}
+					if maxActive == nil || pInt.Cmp(maxActive) > 0 {
+						maxActive = pInt
+					}
+					activeStock += v.StockQuantity
+				}
+			}
+		}
+		if minActive != nil {
+			price = minActive.String()
+			stockQuantity = activeStock
+		} else if minAll != nil {
+			price = minAll.String()
+			stockQuantity = 0
+		}
+	}
+
+	p := &domain.Product{
+		ID:            id,
+		Name:          input.Name,
+		Model:         input.Model,
+		Brand:         input.Brand,
+		Description:   input.Description,
+		Category:      *matchedCategory,
+		Price:         price,
+		Currency:      currency,
+		StockQuantity: stockQuantity,
+		Featured:      input.Featured,
+		Active:        true,
+		ImageURLs:     append([]string(nil), input.ImageURLs...),
+		CreatedAt:     now,
+		UpdatedAt:     now,
+		OptionGroups:  optionGroups,
+		Variants:      variants,
+		MinPriceVND:   domain.MoneyVND(price),
+		MaxPriceVND:   domain.MoneyVND(price),
+	}
+	f.products = append(f.products, p)
+	out := cloneAdminProduct(*p)
+	return &out, nil
+}
+
+func (f *Catalog) UpdateProduct(ctx context.Context, id string, input domain.SaveProductInput) (*domain.Product, error) {
+	f.mu.Lock()
+	defer f.mu.Unlock()
+
+	var target *domain.Product
+	for _, p := range f.products {
+		if p.ID == id && p.Active {
+			target = p
+			break
+		}
+	}
+	if target == nil {
+		return nil, domain.ErrProductNotFound
+	}
+
+	var matchedCategory *domain.Category
+	for _, c := range f.categories {
+		if c.ID == input.CategoryID {
+			res := c
+			matchedCategory = &res
+			break
+		}
+	}
+	if matchedCategory == nil {
+		return nil, domain.NewValidationError(map[string]string{"categoryId": "Danh mục không tồn tại"})
+	}
+
+	currency := input.Currency
+	if currency == "" {
+		currency = "VND"
+	}
+
+	var optionGroups []domain.OptionGroup
+	var variants []domain.ProductVariant
+	price := input.Price
+	stockQuantity := input.StockQuantity
+
+	if input.HasVariants && len(input.OptionGroups) > 0 {
+		for _, g := range input.OptionGroups {
+			gid := g.ID
+			if gid == "" {
+				gid = uuid.New().String()
+			}
+			vals := make([]domain.OptionValue, 0, len(g.Values))
+			for _, v := range g.Values {
+				vid := v.ID
+				if vid == "" {
+					vid = uuid.New().String()
+				}
+				vals = append(vals, domain.OptionValue{ID: vid, Label: v.Label})
+			}
+			optionGroups = append(optionGroups, domain.OptionGroup{
+				ID:       gid,
+				Label:    g.Label,
+				Position: g.Position,
+				Values:   vals,
+			})
+		}
+		var minActive, maxActive *big.Int
+		var minAll, maxAll *big.Int
+		var activeStock int64
+		for _, v := range input.Variants {
+			varID := v.ID
+			if varID == "" {
+				varID = uuid.New().String()
+			}
+			optIDs := make(map[string]string, len(v.OptionValueIDs))
+			for gid, vid := range v.OptionValueIDs {
+				optIDs[gid] = vid
+			}
+			variants = append(variants, domain.ProductVariant{
+				ID:             varID,
+				OptionValueIDs: optIDs,
+				PriceVND:       domain.MoneyVND(v.PriceVND),
+				StockQuantity:  v.StockQuantity,
+				Active:         v.Active,
+			})
+			pInt, ok := new(big.Int).SetString(v.PriceVND, 10)
+			if ok {
+				if minAll == nil || pInt.Cmp(minAll) < 0 {
+					minAll = pInt
+				}
+				if maxAll == nil || pInt.Cmp(maxAll) > 0 {
+					maxAll = pInt
+				}
+				if v.Active {
+					if minActive == nil || pInt.Cmp(minActive) < 0 {
+						minActive = pInt
+					}
+					if maxActive == nil || pInt.Cmp(maxActive) > 0 {
+						maxActive = pInt
+					}
+					activeStock += v.StockQuantity
+				}
+			}
+		}
+		if minActive != nil {
+			price = minActive.String()
+			stockQuantity = activeStock
+		} else if minAll != nil {
+			price = minAll.String()
+			stockQuantity = 0
+		}
+	}
+
+	target.Name = input.Name
+	target.Model = input.Model
+	target.Brand = input.Brand
+	target.Description = input.Description
+	target.Category = *matchedCategory
+	target.Price = price
+	target.Currency = currency
+	target.StockQuantity = stockQuantity
+	target.Featured = input.Featured
+	target.ImageURLs = append([]string(nil), input.ImageURLs...)
+	target.OptionGroups = optionGroups
+	target.Variants = variants
+	target.UpdatedAt = time.Now()
+	target.MinPriceVND = domain.MoneyVND(price)
+	target.MaxPriceVND = domain.MoneyVND(price)
+
+	out := cloneAdminProduct(*target)
+	return &out, nil
+}
+
+func (f *Catalog) DeleteProduct(ctx context.Context, id string) error {
+	f.mu.Lock()
+	defer f.mu.Unlock()
+
+	for _, p := range f.products {
+		if p.ID == id && p.Active {
+			p.Active = false
+			p.UpdatedAt = time.Now()
+			return nil
+		}
+	}
+	return domain.ErrProductNotFound
+}
+
+func cloneAdminProduct(product domain.Product) domain.Product {
+	product.ImageURLs = append([]string(nil), product.ImageURLs...)
+	groups := make([]domain.OptionGroup, 0, len(product.OptionGroups))
+	for _, g := range product.OptionGroups {
+		vals := append([]domain.OptionValue(nil), g.Values...)
+		groups = append(groups, domain.OptionGroup{
+			ID:       g.ID,
+			Label:    g.Label,
+			Position: g.Position,
+			Values:   vals,
+		})
+	}
+	product.OptionGroups = groups
+
+	vars := make([]domain.ProductVariant, 0, len(product.Variants))
+	for _, v := range product.Variants {
+		optIDs := make(map[string]string, len(v.OptionValueIDs))
+		for k, val := range v.OptionValueIDs {
+			optIDs[k] = val
+		}
+		vars = append(vars, domain.ProductVariant{
+			ID:             v.ID,
+			OptionValueIDs: optIDs,
+			PriceVND:       v.PriceVND,
+			StockQuantity:  v.StockQuantity,
+			Active:         v.Active,
+		})
+	}
+	product.Variants = vars
+
+	if len(product.Variants) > 0 {
+		var minPrice, maxPrice *big.Int
+		var minAll, maxAll *big.Int
+		var activeStock int64
+		hasActive := false
+		for _, v := range product.Variants {
+			price, ok := new(big.Int).SetString(string(v.PriceVND), 10)
+			if ok {
+				if minAll == nil || price.Cmp(minAll) < 0 {
+					minAll = price
+				}
+				if maxAll == nil || price.Cmp(maxAll) > 0 {
+					maxAll = price
+				}
+				if v.Active {
+					hasActive = true
+					if minPrice == nil || price.Cmp(minPrice) < 0 {
+						minPrice = price
+					}
+					if maxPrice == nil || price.Cmp(maxPrice) > 0 {
+						maxPrice = price
+					}
+					activeStock += v.StockQuantity
+				}
+			}
+		}
+		if hasActive && minPrice != nil {
+			product.MinPriceVND = domain.MoneyVND(minPrice.String())
+			product.MaxPriceVND = domain.MoneyVND(maxPrice.String())
+			product.Price = minPrice.String()
+			product.StockQuantity = activeStock
+		} else if minAll != nil {
+			product.MinPriceVND = domain.MoneyVND(minAll.String())
+			product.MaxPriceVND = domain.MoneyVND(maxAll.String())
+			product.Price = minAll.String()
+			product.StockQuantity = 0
+		}
+	} else if product.Price != "" {
+		product.MinPriceVND = domain.MoneyVND(product.Price)
+		product.MaxPriceVND = domain.MoneyVND(product.Price)
+	}
+	return product
 }

+func TestFakeCatalog_CreateAndUpdateProductWithVariants(t *testing.T) {
+	ctx := context.Background()
+	repo := NewCatalog()
+	repo.AddCategory(domain.Category{ID: "cat-phone", Name: "Phones"})
+
+	// 1. Create with variants
+	input := domain.SaveProductInput{
+		Name:        "iPhone 15",
+		Model:       "IP15",
+		Brand:       "Apple",
+		Description: "Latest model",
+		CategoryID:  "cat-phone",
+		HasVariants: true,
+		OptionGroups: []domain.OptionGroupInput{
+			{
+				ID:    "grp-color",
+				Label: "Màu sắc",
+				Values: []domain.OptionValueInput{
+					{ID: "val-blue", Label: "Xanh"},
+					{ID: "val-black", Label: "Đen"},
+				},
+			},
+		},
+		Variants: []domain.VariantInput{
+			{
+				ID:             "var-1",
+				OptionValueIDs: map[string]string{"grp-color": "val-blue"},
+				PriceVND:       "20000000",
+				StockQuantity:  5,
+				Active:         true,
+			},
+			{
+				ID:             "var-2",
+				OptionValueIDs: map[string]string{"grp-color": "val-black"},
+				PriceVND:       "22000000",
+				StockQuantity:  3,
+				Active:         true,
+			},
+			{
+				ID:             "var-3",
+				OptionValueIDs: map[string]string{"grp-color": "val-black"},
+				PriceVND:       "18000000",
+				StockQuantity:  10,
+				Active:         false,
+			},
+		},
+	}
+
+	created, err := repo.CreateProduct(ctx, input)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("create product with variants failed: %v", err)
+	}
+	if created.Price != "20000000" || created.MinPriceVND != "20000000" || created.MaxPriceVND != "22000000" {
+		t.Fatalf("created price range mismatch: min=%s max=%s price=%s", created.MinPriceVND, created.MaxPriceVND, created.Price)
+	}
+	if created.StockQuantity != 8 {
+		t.Fatalf("expected total active stock = 8, got %d", created.StockQuantity)
+	}
+	if len(created.OptionGroups) != 1 || len(created.Variants) != 3 {
+		t.Fatalf("expected 1 group and 3 variants, got %d groups and %d variants", len(created.OptionGroups), len(created.Variants))
+	}
+
+	// 2. Update to simple product mode (no variants)
+	updateSimple := domain.SaveProductInput{
+		Name:          "iPhone 15 Simple",
+		Model:         "IP15",
+		Brand:         "Apple",
+		Description:   "Converted to simple product",
+		CategoryID:    "cat-phone",
+		Price:         "19900000",
+		StockQuantity: 50,
+		HasVariants:   false,
+	}
+
+	updated, err := repo.UpdateProduct(ctx, created.ID, updateSimple)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("update to simple product failed: %v", err)
+	}
+	if updated.Price != "19900000" || updated.StockQuantity != 50 {
+		t.Fatalf("updated simple product mismatch: price=%s stock=%d", updated.Price, updated.StockQuantity)
+	}
+	if len(updated.OptionGroups) != 0 || len(updated.Variants) != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("expected 0 groups and 0 variants after simple switch, got %d groups and %d variants", len(updated.OptionGroups), len(updated.Variants))
+	}
+
+	// 3. Update back to variant mode
+	updateVariant := domain.SaveProductInput{
+		Name:        "iPhone 15 Multi",
+		Model:       "IP15",
+		Brand:       "Apple",
+		Description: "Converted back to multi-variant",
+		CategoryID:  "cat-phone",
+		HasVariants: true,
+		OptionGroups: []domain.OptionGroupInput{
+			{
+				ID:    "grp-storage",
+				Label: "Dung lượng",
+				Values: []domain.OptionValueInput{
+					{ID: "val-128", Label: "128GB"},
+				},
+			},
+		},
+		Variants: []domain.VariantInput{
+			{
+				ID:             "var-128",
+				OptionValueIDs: map[string]string{"grp-storage": "val-128"},
+				PriceVND:       "25000000",
+				StockQuantity:  12,
+				Active:         true,
+			},
+		},
+	}
+
+	updatedVar, err := repo.UpdateProduct(ctx, created.ID, updateVariant)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("update to variant product failed: %v", err)
+	}
+	if updatedVar.Price != "25000000" || updatedVar.StockQuantity != 12 {
+		t.Fatalf("updated variant product mismatch: price=%s stock=%d", updatedVar.Price, updatedVar.StockQuantity)
+	}
+	if len(updatedVar.OptionGroups) != 1 || len(updatedVar.Variants) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("expected 1 group and 1 variant, got %d groups and %d variants", len(updatedVar.OptionGroups), len(updatedVar.Variants))
+	}
+}

++func (r *CatalogRepository) withTx(ctx context.Context, fn func(q *sqlc.Queries) error) error {
++	if starter, ok := r.db.(txStarter); ok {
++		tx, err := starter.Begin(ctx)
++		if err != nil {
++			return err
++		}
++		defer tx.Rollback(ctx)
++		if err := fn(sqlc.New(tx)); err != nil {
++			return err
++		}
++		return tx.Commit(ctx)
++	}
++	return fn(r.q)
+ }

++func (r *CatalogRepository) CreateProduct(ctx context.Context, input domain.SaveProductInput) (*domain.Product, error) {

++		}

++					fields[fmt.Sprintf("variants[%d].priceVnd", i)] = "Giá biến thể phải là số không âm"
++				}
++				if variant.StockQuantity < 0 {
++					fields[fmt.Sprintf("variants[%d].stockQuantity", i)] = "Số lượng tồn kho không được âm"
++				}
++			}
++		}
++
++		if input.Price != "" && !validPriceInput(input.Price) {
++			fields["price"] = "Giá sản phẩm phải là số không âm"
++		}
++		if input.StockQuantity < 0 {
++			fields["stockQuantity"] = "Số lượng tồn kho không được âm"
++		}
++	}
++
++	if len(fields) > 0 {
++		return domain.NewValidationError(fields)
++	}
++	return nil
++}
++
++func validPriceInput(s string) bool {
++	if s == "" {
++		return false
++	}
++	if domain.MoneyVND(s).IsCanonical() {
++		return true
++	}
++	return regexp.MustCompile(`^(?:[0-9]+|[0-9]*\.[0-9]{1,2})$`).MatchString(s)
++}
++
+ func validMoney(s string) bool {
+ 	return len(s) <= 38 && domain.MoneyVND(s).IsCanonical()
+ }

++func cloneAdminProduct(product domain.Product) domain.Product {
++	product.ImageURLs = append([]string(nil), product.ImageURLs...)

++			ID:       g.ID,
++			Label:    g.Label,
++			Position: g.Position,
++			Values:   vals,
++		})
++	}
++	product.OptionGroups = groups
++

++	for _, v := range product.Variants {
++		optIDs := make(map[string]string, len(v.OptionValueIDs))

++			optIDs[k] = val
++		}

++			PriceVND:       v.PriceVND,
++			StockQuantity:  v.StockQuantity,
++			Active:         v.Active,
++		})
++	}
++	product.Variants = vars
++
++	if len(product.Variants) > 0 {
++		var minPrice, maxPrice *big.Int

++				if minAll == nil || price.Cmp(minAll) < 0 {
++					minAll = price
++				}
++				if maxAll == nil || price.Cmp(maxAll) > 0 {
++					maxAll = price
++				}
++				if v.Active {
++					hasActive = true
++					if minPrice == nil || price.Cmp(minPrice) < 0 {
++						minPrice = price
++					}
++					if maxPrice == nil || price.Cmp(maxPrice) > 0 {
++						maxPrice = price
++					}
++					activeStock += v.StockQuantity
++				}
++			}
++		}
++		if hasActive && minPrice != nil {
++			product.MinPriceVND = domain.MoneyVND(minPrice.String())
++			product.MaxPriceVND = domain.MoneyVND(maxPrice.String())
++			product.Price = minPrice.String()

++		product.MinPriceVND = domain.MoneyVND(product.Price)
++		product.MaxPriceVND = domain.MoneyVND(product.Price)

diff --git a/frontend-admin/AGENTS.md b/frontend-admin/AGENTS.md
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..1c3eb60
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend-admin/AGENTS.md
@@ -0,0 +1,27 @@
+# Frontend Admin Agent Instructions
+
+## Architecture
+
+* `src/main.tsx` mounts `App.tsx`; `App.tsx` owns the layout shell and routing structure.
+* Keep endpoint-specific calls and wire types in `src/api/`. Domain API service modules return direct payloads or status envelope responses.
+* All requests must go through `src/api/client.ts` with credentials, bearer tokens, 401 handling, and `ApiError` mapping.
+* Handle errors via `ApiError`: use `err.isValidationError()` to bind `err.fields` to form inputs, and user-facing Vietnamese messages for toasts/alerts.
+* Styling is centralized in `src/index.css`.
+
+## UI Stack
+
+Use Tailwind CSS and shadcn/ui as the primary UI foundation.
+
+Preferred supporting libraries:
+* `lucide-react` for icons.
+* `@tanstack/react-query` for data fetching & server state caching.
+* `clsx`, `tailwind-merge`, `class-variance-authority` for styling primitives.
+
+## Commands
+
+Run these from `frontend-admin/`:
+* Install dependencies: `npm install` (or `npm ci`).
+* Start Vite dev server: `npm run dev` (configured on port 5174, proxies `/api` to `http://localhost:${HTTP_PORT:-8080}`).
+* Run tests: `npm test` (`vitest run`).
+* Lint: `npm run lint` (`oxlint`).
+* Typecheck and production build: `npm run build` (`tsc -b && vite build`).

diff --git a/frontend-admin/src/App.tsx b/frontend-admin/src/App.tsx
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..e8ea46b
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend-admin/src/App.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,55 @@
+import { BrowserRouter, Routes, Route, Navigate } from 'react-router-dom'
+import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query'
+import { AuthProvider } from '@/context/AuthContext'
+import { ProtectedRoute } from '@/components/ProtectedRoute'
+import { AdminLayout } from '@/components/layout/AdminLayout'
+import { LoginPage } from '@/pages/LoginPage'
+import { DashboardPage } from '@/pages/DashboardPage'
+import { ProductsPage } from '@/pages/ProductsPage'
+import { ProductFormPage } from '@/pages/ProductFormPage'
+
+const queryClient = new QueryClient({
+  defaultOptions: {
+    queries: {
+      retry: 1,
+      refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
+    },
+  },
+})
+
+export function AppRoutes() {
+  return (
+    <Routes>
+      <Route path="/login" element={<LoginPage />} />
+      <Route
+        path="/"
+        element={
+          <ProtectedRoute>
+            <AdminLayout />
+          </ProtectedRoute>
+        }
+      >
+        <Route index element={<DashboardPage />} />
+        <Route path="products" element={<ProductsPage />} />
+        <Route path="products/new" element={<ProductFormPage mode="create" />} />
+        <Route path="products/:id" element={<ProductFormPage mode="edit" />} />
+        <Route path="products/:id/edit" element={<ProductFormPage mode="edit" />} />
+      </Route>
+      <Route path="*" element={<Navigate to="/" replace />} />
+    </Routes>
+  )
+}
+
+export function App() {
+  return (
+    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
+      <AuthProvider>
+        <BrowserRouter>
+          <AppRoutes />
+        </BrowserRouter>
+      </AuthProvider>
+    </QueryClientProvider>
+  )
+}
+
+export default App
diff --git a/frontend-admin/src/api/auth.ts b/frontend-admin/src/api/auth.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..a8399a6
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend-admin/src/api/auth.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,29 @@
+import { apiClient } from './client'
+import type { SessionResponse, User } from './types'
+
+export const authApi = {
+  async login(email: string, password: string): Promise<SessionResponse> {
+    return apiClient<SessionResponse>('/auth/login', {
+      method: 'POST',
+      body: { email, password },
+    })
+  },
+
+  async refresh(): Promise<SessionResponse> {
+    return apiClient<SessionResponse>('/auth/refresh', {
+      method: 'POST',
+      skipAuthRefresh: true,
+    })
+  },
+
+  async logout(): Promise<void> {
+    return apiClient<void>('/auth/logout', {
+      method: 'POST',
+      skipAuthRefresh: true,
+    })
+  },
+
+  async getMe(): Promise<User> {
+    return apiClient<User>('/auth/me')
+  },
+}

+import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach } from 'vitest'
+import { getCategories } from './categories'
+import * as client from './client'
+import type { Category } from './types'
+
+vi.mock('./client', () => ({
+  apiClient: vi.fn(),
+}))
+
+describe('categories API service', () => {
+  beforeEach(() => {
+    vi.clearAllMocks()
+  })
+
+  describe('getCategories', () => {
+    it('calls /categories and returns Category list', async () => {
+      const mockCategories: Category[] = [
+        { id: 'cat-1', name: 'Điện thoại' },
+        { id: 'cat-2', name: 'Máy tính bảng' },
+      ]
+
+      vi.mocked(client.apiClient).mockResolvedValueOnce(mockCategories)
+
+      const result = await getCategories()
+
+      expect(client.apiClient).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/categories')
+      expect(result).toEqual(mockCategories)
+    })
+  })
+})

diff --git a/frontend-admin/src/api/client.test.ts b/frontend-admin/src/api/client.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..0283044
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend-admin/src/api/client.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,262 @@
+import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, vi, afterEach } from 'vitest'
+import { ApiError } from './types'
+import { tokenStore } from './tokenStore'
+import { apiClient, apiClientWithMeta, fetchEnvelope } from './client'
+
+describe('ApiError', () => {
+  it('instantiates with status, code, message and fields', () => {
+    const err = new ApiError(400, {
+      code: 'validation_error',
+      message: 'Dữ liệu không hợp lệ',
+      fields: { email: 'Email không đúng định dạng' },
+    })
+
+    expect(err.name).toBe('ApiError')
+    expect(err.status).toBe(400)
+    expect(err.code).toBe('validation_error')
+    expect(err.message).toBe('Dữ liệu không hợp lệ')
+    expect(err.fields).toEqual({ email: 'Email không đúng định dạng' })
+    expect(err.isValidationError()).toBe(true)
+    expect(err.isBusinessError()).toBe(false)
+  })
+
+  it('identifies business errors correctly', () => {
+    const err = new ApiError(403, {
+      code: 'forbidden',
+      message: 'Tài khoản không có quyền truy cập trang quản trị',
+    })
+
+    expect(err.status).toBe(403)
+    expect(err.code).toBe('forbidden')
+    expect(err.isValidationError()).toBe(false)
+    expect(err.isBusinessError()).toBe(true)
+  })
+
+  it('identifies 500 server errors as not business errors', () => {
+    const err = new ApiError(500, {
+      code: 'internal',
+      message: 'Lỗi máy chủ',
+    })
+
+    expect(err.status).toBe(500)
+    expect(err.isValidationError()).toBe(false)
+    expect(err.isBusinessError()).toBe(false)
+  })
+})
+
+describe('tokenStore', () => {
+  beforeEach(() => {
+    tokenStore.clearToken()
+  })
+
+  it('gets, sets, and clears tokens', () => {
+    expect(tokenStore.getToken()).toBeNull()
+    tokenStore.setToken('test-admin-jwt-token')
+    expect(tokenStore.getToken()).toBe('test-admin-jwt-token')
+    tokenStore.clearToken()
+    expect(tokenStore.getToken()).toBeNull()
+  })
+})
+
+describe('API Client', () => {
+  beforeEach(() => {
+    tokenStore.clearToken()
+    vi.restoreAllMocks()
+  })
+
+  afterEach(() => {
+    vi.restoreAllMocks()
+  })
+
+  it('unwraps data payload on success 200 response', async () => {
+    const mockResponse = {
+      success: true,
+      message: 'Thành công',
+      data: { id: 'prod-1', name: 'iPhone 15' },
+      error: null,
+      meta: { timestamp: '2026-08-16T00:00:00Z' },
+    }
+
+    vi.spyOn(globalThis, 'fetch').mockResolvedValueOnce(
+      new Response(JSON.stringify(mockResponse), {
+        status: 200,
+        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
+      })
+    )
+
+    const result = await apiClient<{ id: string; name: string }>('/admin/products/prod-1')
+    expect(result).toEqual({ id: 'prod-1', name: 'iPhone 15' })
+  })
+
+  it('unwraps data with meta when using apiClientWithMeta', async () => {
+    const mockResponse = {
+      success: true,
+      message: 'Danh sách sản phẩm',
+      data: [{ id: 'prod-1' }],
+      error: null,
+      meta: {
+        timestamp: '2026-08-16T00:00:00Z',
+        pagination: {
+          page: 1,
+          limit: 10,
+          total: 1,
+          totalPages: 1,
+          hasNext: false,
+          hasPrev: false,
+        },
+      },
+    }
+
+    vi.spyOn(globalThis, 'fetch').mockResolvedValueOnce(
+      new Response(JSON.stringify(mockResponse), {
+        status: 200,
+        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
+      })
+    )
+
+    const result = await apiClientWithMeta<Array<{ id: string }>>('/admin/products')
+    expect(result.data).toEqual([{ id: 'prod-1' }])
+    expect(result.meta.pagination?.total).toBe(1)
+    expect(result.message).toBe('Danh sách sản phẩm')
+  })
+
+  it('attaches Authorization header when tokenStore has token', async () => {
+    tokenStore.setToken('bearer-token-123')
+
+    const fetchSpy = vi.spyOn(globalThis, 'fetch').mockResolvedValueOnce(
+      new Response(
+        JSON.stringify({
+          success: true,
+          message: '',
+          data: { id: 'admin-1' },
+          error: null,
+          meta: { timestamp: '2026-08-16T00:00:00Z' },
+        }),
+        { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
+      )
+    )
+
+    await apiClient('/auth/me')
+
+    expect(fetchSpy).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
+    const callArgs = fetchSpy.mock.calls[0]
+    const options = callArgs[1] as RequestInit
+    expect((options.headers as Record<string, string>)['Authorization']).toBe('Bearer bearer-token-123')
+  })
+
+  it('handles 204 No Content responses', async () => {
+    vi.spyOn(globalThis, 'fetch').mockResolvedValueOnce(
+      new Response(null, { status: 204 })
+    )
+
+    const envelope = await fetchEnvelope('/admin/products/1', { method: 'DELETE' })
+    expect(envelope.success).toBe(true)
+  })
+
+  it('throws ApiError on HTTP error status with parsed envelope error', async () => {
+    const errorBody = {
+      success: false,
+      message: 'Không tìm thấy sản phẩm',
+      data: null,
+      error: {
+        code: 'not_found',
+        message: 'Không tìm thấy sản phẩm',
+      },
+      meta: { timestamp: '2026-08-16T00:00:00Z' },
+    }
+
+    vi.spyOn(globalThis, 'fetch').mockResolvedValueOnce(
+      new Response(JSON.stringify(errorBody), {
+        status: 404,
+        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
+      })
+    )
+
+    await expect(apiClient('/admin/products/non-existent')).rejects.toThrow('Không tìm thấy sản phẩm')
+  })
+
+  it('handles 401 error and attempts refresh, retrying on success', async () => {
+    tokenStore.setToken('expired-token')
+
+    const fetchSpy = vi.spyOn(globalThis, 'fetch')
+      // First call -> 401
+      .mockResolvedValueOnce(
+        new Response(
+          JSON.stringify({
+            success: false,
+            message: 'Token hết hạn',
+            data: null,
+            error: { code: 'unauthorized', message: 'Token hết hạn' },
+            meta: { timestamp: '2026-08-16T00:00:00Z' },
+          }),
+          { status: 401, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
+        )
+      )
+      // Refresh call -> 200 with new token
+      .mockResolvedValueOnce(
+        new Response(
+          JSON.stringify({
+            success: true,
+            message: '',
+            data: { accessToken: 'fresh-token', user: { id: '1', email: 'admin@test.com', role: 'admin' } },
+            error: null,
+            meta: { timestamp: '2026-08-16T00:00:00Z' },
+          }),
+          { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
+        )
+      )
+      // Retried original call -> 200
+      .mockResolvedValueOnce(
+        new Response(
+          JSON.stringify({
+            success: true,
+            message: '',
+            data: { id: 'admin-1' },
+            error: null,
+            meta: { timestamp: '2026-08-16T00:00:00Z' },
+          }),
+          { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
+        )
+      )
+
+    const result = await apiClient<{ id: string }>('/auth/me')
+    expect(result).toEqual({ id: 'admin-1' })
+    expect(tokenStore.getToken()).toBe('fresh-token')
+    expect(fetchSpy).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3)
+  })
+
+  it('clears token and throws ApiError if 401 refresh fails', async () => {
+    tokenStore.setToken('expired-token')
+
+    vi.spyOn(globalThis, 'fetch')
+      // First call -> 401
+      .mockResolvedValueOnce(
+        new Response(
+          JSON.stringify({
+            success: false,
+            message: 'Unauthorized',
+            data: null,
+            error: { code: 'unauthorized', message: 'Unauthorized' },
+            meta: { timestamp: '2026-08-16T00:00:00Z' },
+          }),
+          { status: 401, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
+        )
+      )
+      // Refresh call -> 401
+      .mockResolvedValueOnce(
+        new Response(
+          JSON.stringify({
+            success: false,
+            message: 'Invalid refresh token',
+            data: null,
+            error: { code: 'invalid_token', message: 'Invalid refresh token' },
+            meta: { timestamp: '2026-08-16T00:00:00Z' },
+          }),
+          { status: 401, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
+        )
+      )
+
+    await expect(apiClient('/admin/products')).rejects.toThrow()
+    expect(tokenStore.getToken()).toBeNull()
+  })
+})
diff --git a/frontend-admin/src/api/client.ts b/frontend-admin/src/api/client.ts

+import { ApiError, type ApiResponseEnvelope, type ApiResponseMeta, type ApiErrorDetail } from './types'
+import { tokenStore } from './tokenStore'
+
+const BASE_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL || '/api/v1'
+
+export interface RequestOptions extends Omit<RequestInit, 'body'> {
+  body?: unknown
+  skipAuthRefresh?: boolean
+}
+
+export interface ApiResponseWithMeta<T> {
+  data: T
+  meta: ApiResponseMeta
+  message: string
+}
+
+let isRefreshing = false
+let refreshPromise: Promise<string | null> | null = null
+
+async function refreshAccessToken(): Promise<string | null> {
+  if (isRefreshing && refreshPromise) {
+    return refreshPromise
+  }
+  isRefreshing = true
+  refreshPromise = (async () => {
+    try {
+      const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/auth/refresh`, {
+        method: 'POST',
+        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
+        credentials: 'include',
+      })
+      if (!res.ok) {
+        tokenStore.clearToken()
+        return null
+      }
+      const json = await res.json()
+      const token = json?.data?.accessToken || json?.accessToken
+      if (token) {
+        tokenStore.setToken(token)
+        return token
+      }
+      tokenStore.clearToken()
+      return null
+    } catch {
+      tokenStore.clearToken()
+      return null
+    } finally {
+      isRefreshing = false
+      refreshPromise = null
+    }
+  })()
+  return refreshPromise
+}
+
+export async function fetchEnvelope<T>(path: string, options: RequestOptions = {}): Promise<ApiResponseEnvelope<T>> {
+  const { body, headers = {}, skipAuthRefresh = false, ...rest } = options
+
+  const formattedPath = path.startsWith('/') ? path : `/${path}`
+  const url = `${BASE_URL}${formattedPath}`
+
+  const requestHeaders: Record<string, string> = {
+    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
+    ...(headers as Record<string, string>),
+  }
+
+  const token = tokenStore.getToken()
+  if (token && !requestHeaders['Authorization']) {
+    requestHeaders['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${token}`
+  }
+
+  const fetchOptions: RequestInit = {
+    ...rest,
+    headers: requestHeaders,
+    credentials: 'include',
+  }
+
+  if (body !== undefined) {
+    fetchOptions.body = JSON.stringify(body)
+  }
+
+  let response = await fetch(url, fetchOptions)
+
+  // Handle 401 refresh retry
+  const isAuthRoute =
+    formattedPath.startsWith('/auth/login') ||
+    formattedPath.startsWith('/auth/register') ||
+    formattedPath.startsWith('/auth/refresh')
+
+  if (response.status === 401 && !skipAuthRefresh && !isAuthRoute) {
+    const newToken = await refreshAccessToken()
+    if (newToken) {
+      requestHeaders['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${newToken}`
+      fetchOptions.headers = requestHeaders
+      response = await fetch(url, fetchOptions)
+    }
+  }
+
+  if (response.status === 204) {
+    return {
+      success: true,
+      message: '',
+      data: {} as T,
+      error: null,
+      meta: {
+        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
+      },
+    }
+  }
+
+  let json: ApiResponseEnvelope<T> | null = null
+  try {
+    json = await response.json()
+  } catch {
+    // Body not JSON
+  }
+
+  if (!response.ok || (json && json.success === false)) {
+    let errorDetail: ApiErrorDetail = {
+      code: 'unknown_error',
+      message: `Request failed with status ${response.status}`,
+    }
+    let message: string | undefined
+
+    if (json?.error) {
+      errorDetail = json.error
+      message = json.message || json.error.message
+    } else if (json?.message) {
+      errorDetail = { code: 'unknown_error', message: json.message }
+      message = json.message
+    }
+
+    throw new ApiError(response.status, errorDetail, message)
+  }
+
+  if (!json) {
+    throw new ApiError(response.status, {
+      code: 'invalid_response',
+      message: 'Invalid JSON response from server',
+    })
+  }
+
+  return json
+}
+
+export async function apiClient<T>(path: string, options: RequestOptions = {}): Promise<T> {
+  const envelope = await fetchEnvelope<T>(path, options)
+  return envelope.data as T
+}
+
+export async function apiClientWithMeta<T>(path: string, options: RequestOptions = {}): Promise<ApiResponseWithMeta<T>> {
+  const envelope = await fetchEnvelope<T>(path, options)
+  return {
+    data: envelope.data as T,
+    meta: envelope.meta,
+    message: envelope.message,
+  }
+}

+import { apiClient, apiClientWithMeta, type ApiResponseWithMeta } from './client'
+import type { ProductDetail, ProductSummary, SaveProductRequest } from './types'
+
+export interface GetAdminProductsParams {
+  q?: string
+  page?: number
+  limit?: number
+}
+
+export async function getAdminProducts(
+  params: GetAdminProductsParams = {}
+): Promise<ApiResponseWithMeta<ProductSummary[]>> {
+  const searchParams = new URLSearchParams()
+  if (params.q !== undefined && params.q.trim() !== '') {
+    searchParams.set('q', params.q.trim())
+  }
+  if (params.page !== undefined) {
+    searchParams.set('page', String(params.page))
+  }
+  if (params.limit !== undefined) {
+    searchParams.set('limit', String(params.limit))
+  }
+
+  const queryString = searchParams.toString()
+  const path = queryString ? `/admin/products?${queryString}` : '/admin/products'
+
+  return apiClientWithMeta<ProductSummary[]>(path)
+}
+
+export async function getAdminProductById(id: string): Promise<ProductDetail> {
+  return apiClient<ProductDetail>(`/admin/products/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`)
+}
+
+export async function createAdminProduct(payload: SaveProductRequest): Promise<ProductDetail> {
+  return apiClient<ProductDetail>('/admin/products', {
+    method: 'POST',
+    body: payload,
+  })
+}
+
+export async function updateAdminProduct(id: string, payload: SaveProductRequest): Promise<ProductDetail> {
+  return apiClient<ProductDetail>(`/admin/products/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, {
+    method: 'PUT',
+    body: payload,
+  })
+}
+
+export async function deleteAdminProduct(id: string): Promise<void> {
+  return apiClient<void>(`/admin/products/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, {
+    method: 'DELETE',
+  })
+}
+

+let inMemoryAccessToken: string | null = null
+
+export const tokenStore = {
+  getToken(): string | null {
+    return inMemoryAccessToken
+  },
+  setToken(token: string | null): void {
+    inMemoryAccessToken = token
+  },
+  clearToken(): void {
+    inMemoryAccessToken = null
+  },
+}

+export interface PaginationMeta {
+  page: number
+  limit: number
+  total: number
+  totalPages: number
+  hasNext: boolean
+  hasPrev: boolean
+}
+
+export interface ApiResponseMeta {
+  timestamp: string
+  pagination?: PaginationMeta
+}
+
+export interface ApiErrorDetail {
+  code: string
+  message: string
+  fields?: Record<string, string>
+}
+
+export interface ApiResponseEnvelope<T> {
+  success: boolean
+  message: string
+  data: T | null
+  error: ApiErrorDetail | null
+  meta: ApiResponseMeta
+}
+
+export class ApiError extends Error {
+  readonly status: number
+  readonly code: string
+  readonly fields?: Record<string, string>
+
+  constructor(status: number, detail: ApiErrorDetail, message?: string) {
+    super(message || detail.message || `API Error ${status}`)
+    this.name = 'ApiError'
+    this.status = status
+    this.code = detail.code || 'unknown_error'
+    this.fields = detail.fields
+  }
+
+  isValidationError(): boolean {
+    return Boolean(this.fields && Object.keys(this.fields).length > 0)
+  }
+
+  isBusinessError(): boolean {
+    return !this.isValidationError() && this.status < 500
+  }
+}
+
+// User & Auth
+export interface User {
+  id: string
+  email: string
+  role: string
+}
+
+export interface SessionResponse {
+  user: User
+  accessToken: string
+}
+
+export interface AuthMeResponse {
+  user: User
+}
+
+// Category & Catalog
+export interface Category {
+  id: string
+  name: string
+}
+
+export interface CategoriesResponse {
+  categories: Category[]
+}
+
+export interface ProductSummary {
+  id: string
+  name: string
+  model: string
+  brand: string
+  category: Category
+  minPriceVnd: string
+  maxPriceVnd: string
+  price: string
+  currency: string
+  primaryImageUrl: string
+  inStock: boolean
+}
+
+export interface OptionValueInput {
+  id: string
+  label: string
+  position?: number
+}
+
+export interface OptionGroupInput {
+  id: string
+  label: string
+  position?: number
+  values: OptionValueInput[]
+}
+
+export interface VariantInput {
+  id?: string
+  optionValueIds: Record<string, string>
+  priceVND?: string
+  priceVnd?: string
+  stockQuantity: number
+  active: boolean
+}
+
+export interface ProductOptionValue {
+  id: string
+  label: string
+}
+
+export interface ProductOptionGroup {
+  id: string
+  label: string
+  position: number
+  values: ProductOptionValue[]
+}
+
+export interface ProductVariant {
+  id: string
+  optionValueIds: Record<string, string>
+  priceVnd?: string
+  priceVND?: string
+  stockQuantity: number
+  active: boolean
+}
+
+export interface ProductDetail extends ProductSummary {
+  description: string
+  imageUrls: string[]
+  stockQuantity: number
+  featured: boolean
+  active?: boolean
+  optionGroups?: ProductOptionGroup[]
+  variants?: ProductVariant[]
+  createdAt: string
+  updatedAt: string
+}
+
+export interface SaveProductRequest {
+  name: string
+  model: string
+  brand: string
+  description: string
+  categoryId: string
+  price: string
+  currency: string
+  stockQuantity: number
+  featured: boolean
+  imageUrls: string[]
+  hasVariants?: boolean
+  optionGroups?: OptionGroupInput[]
+  variants?: VariantInput[]
+}
+

+import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest'
+import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react'
+import { MemoryRouter, Routes, Route } from 'react-router-dom'
+import { ProtectedRoute } from './ProtectedRoute'
+import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
+
+vi.mock('@/context/AuthContext', () => ({
+  useAuth: vi.fn(),
+}))
+
+describe('ProtectedRoute', () => {
+  it('renders loading indicator while auth is resolving', () => {
+    vi.mocked(useAuth).mockReturnValue({
+      user: null,
+      role: null,
+      isAuthenticated: false,
+      isLoading: true,
+      login: vi.fn(),
+      logout: vi.fn(),
+    })
+
+    render(
+      <MemoryRouter initialEntries={['/dashboard']}>
+        <Routes>
+          <Route
+            path="/dashboard"
+            element={
+              <ProtectedRoute>
+                <div>Secret Admin Dashboard</div>
+              </ProtectedRoute>
+            }
+          />
+        </Routes>
+      </MemoryRouter>
+    )
+
+    expect(screen.getByTestId('auth-loading')).toBeInTheDocument()
+    expect(screen.queryByText('Secret Admin Dashboard')).not.toBeInTheDocument()
+  })
+
+  it('redirects unauthenticated users to /login', () => {
+    vi.mocked(useAuth).mockReturnValue({
+      user: null,
+      role: null,
+      isAuthenticated: false,
+      isLoading: false,
+      login: vi.fn(),
+      logout: vi.fn(),
+    })
+
+    render(
+      <MemoryRouter initialEntries={['/dashboard']}>
+        <Routes>
+          <Route
+            path="/dashboard"
+            element={
+              <ProtectedRoute>
+                <div>Secret Admin Dashboard</div>
+              </ProtectedRoute>
+            }
+          />
+          <Route path="/login" element={<div>Trang Đăng Nhập</div>} />
+        </Routes>
+      </MemoryRouter>
+    )
+
+    expect(screen.getByText('Trang Đăng Nhập')).toBeInTheDocument()
+    expect(screen.queryByText('Secret Admin Dashboard')).not.toBeInTheDocument()
+  })
+
+  it('renders children when user is authenticated as admin', () => {
+    vi.mocked(useAuth).mockReturnValue({
+      user: { id: '1', email: 'admin@auraphone.vn', role: 'admin' },
+      role: 'admin',
+      isAuthenticated: true,
+      isLoading: false,
+      login: vi.fn(),
+      logout: vi.fn(),
+    })
+
+    render(
+      <MemoryRouter initialEntries={['/dashboard']}>
+        <Routes>
+          <Route
+            path="/dashboard"
+            element={
+              <ProtectedRoute>
+                <div>Secret Admin Dashboard</div>
+              </ProtectedRoute>
+            }
+          />
+        </Routes>
+      </MemoryRouter>
+    )
+
+    expect(screen.getByText('Secret Admin Dashboard')).toBeInTheDocument()
+  })
+})

new file mode 100644
index 0000000..b461c43
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend-admin/src/components/ProtectedRoute.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,32 @@
+import type { ReactNode } from 'react'
+import { Navigate, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom'
+import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
+
+export interface ProtectedRouteProps {
+  children?: ReactNode
+}
+
+export function ProtectedRoute({ children }: ProtectedRouteProps) {
+  const { isAuthenticated, isLoading, role } = useAuth()
+  const location = useLocation()
+
+  if (isLoading) {
+    return (
+      <div
+        data-testid="auth-loading"
+        className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-slate-50"
+      >
+        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3">
+          <div className="h-8 w-8 animate-spin rounded-full border-3 border-blue-600 border-t-transparent" />
+          <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-500">Đang kiểm tra quyền truy cập...</p>
+        </div>
+      </div>
+    )
+  }
+
+  if (!isAuthenticated || role !== 'admin') {
+    return <Navigate to="/login" state={{ from: location }} replace />
+  }
+
+  return children ? <>{children}</> : null
+}

+import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest'
+import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react'
+import { OptionGroupBuilder } from './OptionGroupBuilder'
+import type { OptionGroupItem } from '@/lib/cartesian'
+
+describe('OptionGroupBuilder Component', () => {
+  it('renders add group button and allows adding a new group', () => {
+    const onChange = vi.fn()
+    render(<OptionGroupBuilder groups={[]} onChange={onChange} />)
+
+    const addButton = screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Thêm nhóm phân loại/i })
+    expect(addButton).toBeInTheDocument()
+    expect(addButton).not.toBeDisabled()
+
+    fireEvent.click(addButton)
+    expect(onChange).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
+    const newGroups: OptionGroupItem[] = onChange.mock.calls[0][0]

+    expect(newGroups[0].values).toEqual([])
+  })
+

+    const onChange = vi.fn()

+      { id: '1', label: 'Màu sắc', values: [{ id: 'v1', label: 'Đen' }] },
+      { id: '2', label: 'Dung lượng', values: [{ id: 'v2', label: '128GB' }] },
+      { id: '3', label: 'Phiên bản', values: [{ id: 'v3', label: 'LTE' }] },
+    ]
+
+    render(<OptionGroupBuilder groups={groups} onChange={onChange} />)
+
+    const addButton = screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Thêm nhóm phân loại/i })
+    expect(addButton).toBeDisabled()
+  })
+
+  it('allows updating the group label', () => {
+    const onChange = vi.fn()
+    const groups: OptionGroupItem[] = [
+      { id: '1', label: 'Màu', values: [] },
+    ]
+
+    render(<OptionGroupBuilder groups={groups} onChange={onChange} />)
+
+    const labelInput = screen.getByPlaceholderText(/Ví dụ: Màu sắc, Dung lượng/i)
+    fireEvent.change(labelInput, { target: { value: 'Màu sắc mới' } })
+
+    expect(onChange).toHaveBeenCalledWith([
+      { id: '1', label: 'Màu sắc mới', values: [] },
+    ])
+  })
+
+  it('allows adding value chips by typing and pressing Enter or clicking Add', () => {
+    const onChange = vi.fn()
+    const groups: OptionGroupItem[] = [
+      { id: '1', label: 'Màu sắc', values: [] },
+    ]
+
+    render(<OptionGroupBuilder groups={groups} onChange={onChange} />)
+
+    const valueInput = screen.getByPlaceholderText(/Nhập giá trị/i)
+    fireEvent.change(valueInput, { target: { value: 'Đen nhám' } })
+    fireEvent.keyDown(valueInput, { key: 'Enter', code: 'Enter' })
+
+    expect(onChange).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
+    const updated = onChange.mock.calls[0][0]
+    expect(updated[0].values).toHaveLength(1)
+    expect(updated[0].values[0].label).toBe('Đen nhám')
+    expect(updated[0].values[0].id).toBeDefined()
+  })
+
+  it('prevents adding duplicate values within the same group (case-insensitive)', () => {
+    const onChange = vi.fn()
+    const groups: OptionGroupItem[] = [
+      {
+        id: '1',

+        values: [{ id: 'v1', label: 'Đen' }],

+
+    render(<OptionGroupBuilder groups={groups} onChange={onChange} />)

+    fireEvent.keyDown(valueInput, { key: 'Enter', code: 'Enter' })
+
+    expect(onChange).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
+    expect(screen.getByText(/Giá trị phân loại này đã tồn tại/i)).toBeInTheDocument()
+  })
+

+    const onChange = vi.fn()
+    const groups: OptionGroupItem[] = [
+      {
+        id: '1',
+        label: 'Màu sắc',
+        values: [
+          { id: 'v1', label: 'Đen' },
+          { id: 'v2', label: 'Trắng' },
+        ],
+      },
+    ]

+      },
+    ])
+  })
+
+  it('allows deleting an entire option group', () => {
+    const onChange = vi.fn()
+    const groups: OptionGroupItem[] = [
+      { id: '1', label: 'Màu sắc', values: [{ id: 'v1', label: 'Đen' }] },
+      { id: '2', label: 'Dung lượng', values: [{ id: 'v2', label: '128GB' }] },
+    ]
+
+    render(<OptionGroupBuilder groups={groups} onChange={onChange} />)
+
+    const deleteGroupButtons = screen.getAllByRole('button', { name: /Xóa nhóm/i })

+      { id: '2', label: 'Dung lượng', values: [{ id: 'v2', label: '128GB' }] },
+    ])
+  })
+

+    const groups: OptionGroupItem[] = [
+      { id: '1', label: '', values: [] },
+    ]

+      <OptionGroupBuilder
+        groups={groups}
+        onChange={vi.fn()}
+        errors={{
+          'optionGroups.0.label': 'Tên nhóm thuộc tính không được để trống',
+          'optionGroups.0.values': 'Nhóm thuộc tính phải có ít nhất một giá trị',
+        }}
+      />
+    )
+
+    expect(
+      screen.getByText('Tên nhóm thuộc tính không được để trống')
+    ).toBeInTheDocument()
+    expect(
+      screen.getByText('Nhóm thuộc tính phải có ít nhất một giá trị')
+    ).toBeInTheDocument()
+  })
+})
diff --git a/frontend-admin/src/components/products/OptionGroupBuilder.tsx b/frontend-admin/src/components/products/OptionGroupBuilder.tsx
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..946362e
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend-admin/src/components/products/OptionGroupBuilder.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,253 @@
+import React, { useState } from 'react'
+import { Plus, Trash2, X } from 'lucide-react'
+import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
+import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
+import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label'
+import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
+import { Card, CardContent } from '@/components/ui/card'
+import type { OptionGroupItem } from '@/lib/cartesian'
+
+export interface OptionGroupBuilderProps {
+  groups: OptionGroupItem[]
+  onChange: (groups: OptionGroupItem[]) => void
+  maxGroups?: number
+  errors?: Record<string, string>
+  disabled?: boolean
+}
+
+export const OptionGroupBuilder: React.FC<OptionGroupBuilderProps> = ({
+  groups,
+  onChange,
+  maxGroups = 3,
+  errors = {},
+  disabled = false,
+}) => {
+  const [draftInputs, setDraftInputs] = useState<Record<string, string>>({})
+  const [duplicateErrors, setDuplicateErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({})
+
+  const handleAddGroup = () => {
+    if (groups.length >= maxGroups || disabled) return
+    const newGroup: OptionGroupItem = {
+      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
+      label: '',
+      values: [],
+    }
+    onChange([...groups, newGroup])
+  }
+
+  const handleRemoveGroup = (groupId: string) => {
+    if (disabled) return
+    const updated = groups.filter((g) => g.id !== groupId)
+    onChange(updated)
+  }
+
+  const handleLabelChange = (groupId: string, newLabel: string) => {
+    if (disabled) return
+    const updated = groups.map((g) => (g.id === groupId ? { ...g, label: newLabel } : g))
+    onChange(updated)
+  }
+
+  const handleAddValue = (groupId: string) => {
+    if (disabled) return
+    const rawText = draftInputs[groupId] || ''
+    const trimmed = rawText.trim()
+    if (!trimmed) return
+
+    const group = groups.find((g) => g.id === groupId)
+    if (!group) return
+
+    const exists = group.values.some(
+      (v) => v.label.trim().toLowerCase() === trimmed.toLowerCase()
+    )
+
+    if (exists) {
+      setDuplicateErrors((prev) => ({
+        ...prev,
+        [groupId]: 'Giá trị phân loại này đã tồn tại trong nhóm',
+      }))
+      return
+    }
+
+    setDuplicateErrors((prev) => {
+      const next = { ...prev }
+      delete next[groupId]
+      return next
+    })
+
+    setDraftInputs((prev) => ({ ...prev, [groupId]: '' }))
+
+    const updated = groups.map((g) => {
+      if (g.id !== groupId) return g
+      return {
+        ...g,
+        values: [...g.values, { id: crypto.randomUUID(), label: trimmed }],
+      }
+    })
+
+    onChange(updated)
+  }
+
+  const handleRemoveValue = (groupId: string, valueId: string) => {
+    if (disabled) return
+    const updated = groups.map((g) => {
+      if (g.id !== groupId) return g
+      return {
+        ...g,
+        values: g.values.filter((v) => v.id !== valueId),
+      }
+    })
+    onChange(updated)
+  }
+
+  const handleKeyDown = (
+    e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>,
+    groupId: string
+  ) => {
+    if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ',') {
+      e.preventDefault()
+      handleAddValue(groupId)
+    }
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <div className="space-y-4">
+      {groups.map((group, index) => {
+        const labelError =
+          errors[`optionGroups.${index}.label`] ||
+          errors[`optionGroups[${index}].label`]
+        const valuesError =
+          errors[`optionGroups.${index}.values`] ||
+          errors[`optionGroups[${index}].values`] ||
+          errors[`optionGroups.${index}`]
+        const duplicateError = duplicateErrors[group.id]
+
+        return (
+          <Card key={group.id} className="border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
+            <CardContent className="p-4 sm:p-5 space-y-4">
+              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
+                <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">
+                  Nhóm phân loại {index + 1}
+                </span>
+                <Button
+                  type="button"
+                  variant="ghost"
+                  size="sm"
+                  aria-label="Xóa nhóm"
+                  disabled={disabled}
+                  onClick={() => handleRemoveGroup(group.id)}
+                  className="text-rose-600 hover:text-rose-700 hover:bg-rose-50 -mr-2"
+                >
+                  <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-1.5" />
+                  Xóa nhóm
+                </Button>
+              </div>
+
+              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
+                <div className="sm:col-span-1 space-y-1.5">
+                  <Label htmlFor={`group-label-${group.id}`} className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-600">
+                    Tên nhóm phân loại <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
+                  </Label>
+                  <Input
+                    id={`group-label-${group.id}`}
+                    placeholder="Ví dụ: Màu sắc, Dung lượng"
+                    value={group.label}
+                    disabled={disabled}
+                    error={Boolean(labelError)}
+                    onChange={(e) => handleLabelChange(group.id, e.target.value)}
+                  />
+                  {labelError && (
+                    <p className="text-xs text-rose-500 mt-1 font-medium">{labelError}</p>
+                  )}
+                </div>
+
+                <div className="sm:col-span-2 space-y-1.5">
+                  <Label htmlFor={`group-value-input-${group.id}`} className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-600">
+                    Giá trị phân loại <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
+                  </Label>
+
+                  {/* Value Chips container */}
+                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 min-h-10 p-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/50">
+                    {group.values.map((val) => (
+                      <Badge
+                        key={val.id}
+                        variant="secondary"
+                        className="pl-2.5 pr-1.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium bg-white text-slate-800 border border-slate-200 shadow-2xs flex items-center gap-1"
+                      >
+                        <span>{val.label}</span>
+                        <button
+                          type="button"
+                          aria-label={`Xóa giá trị ${val.label}`}
+                          disabled={disabled}
+                          onClick={() => handleRemoveValue(group.id, val.id)}
+                          className="text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-600 rounded-full p-0.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
+                        >
+                          <X className="w-3 h-3" />
+                        </button>
+                      </Badge>
+                    ))}
+
+                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1 flex-1 min-w-[180px]">
+                      <input
+                        id={`group-value-input-${group.id}`}
+                        type="text"
+                        placeholder={
+                          group.values.length === 0
+                            ? 'Nhập giá trị (ví dụ: Đen, Trắng) và nhấn Enter...'
+                            : 'Nhập giá trị tiếp theo...'
+                        }
+                        value={draftInputs[group.id] || ''}
+                        disabled={disabled}
+                        onChange={(e) =>
+                          setDraftInputs((prev) => ({
+                            ...prev,
+                            [group.id]: e.target.value,
+                          }))
+                        }
+                        onKeyDown={(e) => handleKeyDown(e, group.id)}
+                        className="flex-1 bg-transparent text-sm text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:outline-none px-1"
+                      />
+                      <Button
+                        type="button"
+                        variant="outline"
+                        size="sm"
+                        disabled={disabled || !(draftInputs[group.id] || '').trim()}
+                        onClick={() => handleAddValue(group.id)}
+                        className="h-7 px-2 text-xs bg-white"
+                      >
+                        <Plus className="w-3 h-3 mr-1" />
+                        Thêm
+                      </Button>
+                    </div>
+                  </div>
+
+                  {duplicateError && (
+                    <p className="text-xs text-rose-500 mt-1 font-medium">{duplicateError}</p>
+                  )}
+                  {valuesError && (
+                    <p className="text-xs text-rose-500 mt-1 font-medium">{valuesError}</p>
+                  )}
+                </div>
+              </div>
+            </CardContent>
+          </Card>
+        )
+      })}
+
+      <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-1">
+        <Button
+          type="button"
+          variant="outline"
+          disabled={disabled || groups.length >= maxGroups}
+          onClick={handleAddGroup}
+          className="border-dashed border-slate-300 hover:border-slate-400 bg-white text-slate-700"
+        >
+          <Plus className="w-4 h-4 mr-1.5" />
+          Thêm nhóm phân loại {groups.length > 0 && `(${groups.length}/${maxGroups})`}
+        </Button>
+        <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">
+          Tối đa {maxGroups} nhóm thuộc tính (ví dụ: Màu sắc, Dung lượng, Phiên bản)
+        </span>
+      </div>
+    </div>
+  )
+}

+import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest'
+import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react'
+import { VariantMatrixTable } from './VariantMatrixTable'
+import type { VariantMatrixRow } from '@/lib/cartesian'
+
+describe('VariantMatrixTable Component', () => {
+  const sampleVariants: VariantMatrixRow[] = [
+    {
+      id: 'v-1',
+      optionValueIds: { 'g-1': 'val-1', 'g-2': 'val-3' },
+      combinationKey: 'g-1:val-1|g-2:val-3',
+      combinationLabel: 'Đen / 128GB',
+      priceVND: '24990000',
+      stockQuantity: 10,
+      active: true,
+    },
+    {
+      id: 'v-2',
+      optionValueIds: { 'g-1': 'val-1', 'g-2': 'val-4' },
+      combinationKey: 'g-1:val-1|g-2:val-4',
+      combinationLabel: 'Đen / 256GB',
+      priceVND: '27990000',
+      stockQuantity: 5,
+      active: true,
+    },
+    {
+      id: 'v-3',
+      optionValueIds: { 'g-1': 'val-2', 'g-2': 'val-3' },
+      combinationKey: 'g-1:val-2|g-2:val-3',
+      combinationLabel: 'Trắng / 128GB',
+      priceVND: '24990000',
+      stockQuantity: 8,
+      active: false,
+    },
+  ]
+

+    render(<VariantMatrixTable variants={[]} onChange={vi.fn()} />)
+    expect(
+      screen.getByText(/Chưa có phân loại hàng/i)
+    ).toBeInTheDocument()
+  })
+
+  it('renders table headers and variant combination rows', () => {
+    render(<VariantMatrixTable variants={sampleVariants} onChange={vi.fn()} />)
+
+    expect(screen.getByText('Phân loại hàng')).toBeInTheDocument()
+    expect(screen.getByText(/Giá bán/i)).toBeInTheDocument()
+    expect(screen.getByText(/Kho hàng/i)).toBeInTheDocument()
+    expect(screen.getByText('Kích hoạt')).toBeInTheDocument()
+
+    expect(screen.getByText('Đen / 128GB')).toBeInTheDocument()
+    expect(screen.getByText('Đen / 256GB')).toBeInTheDocument()
+    expect(screen.getByText('Trắng / 128GB')).toBeInTheDocument()
+  })
+
+  it('allows updating an individual variant price', () => {
+    const onChange = vi.fn()
+    render(<VariantMatrixTable variants={sampleVariants} onChange={onChange} />)
+
+    const priceInputs = screen.getAllByPlaceholderText(/Nhập giá/i)
+    fireEvent.change(priceInputs[0], { target: { value: '25500000' } })
+
+    expect(onChange).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
+    const updated = onChange.mock.calls[0][0]
+    expect(updated[0].priceVND).toBe('25500000')
+    expect(updated[1].priceVND).toBe('27990000')
+  })
+
+  it('allows updating an individual variant stock quantity', () => {
+    const onChange = vi.fn()
+    render(<VariantMatrixTable variants={sampleVariants} onChange={onChange} />)
+
+    const stockInputs = screen.getAllByPlaceholderText(/0/i)
+    fireEvent.change(stockInputs[1], { target: { value: '25' } })
+
+    expect(onChange).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
+    const updated = onChange.mock.calls[0][0]
+    expect(updated[1].stockQuantity).toBe(25)
+  })
+
+  it('allows toggling variant active switch', () => {
+    const onChange = vi.fn()
+    render(<VariantMatrixTable variants={sampleVariants} onChange={onChange} />)
+
+    const checkboxes = screen.getAllByRole('checkbox')
+    // Toggle first active switch (from true to false)
+    fireEvent.click(checkboxes[0])
+
+    expect(onChange).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
+    const updated = onChange.mock.calls[0][0]
+    expect(updated[0].active).toBe(false)
+  })
+

+
+    const bulkPriceInput = screen.getByPlaceholderText(/Giá chung/i)
+    const bulkStockInput = screen.getByPlaceholderText(/Kho chung/i)
+    const applyButton = screen.getByRole('button', {
+      name: /Áp dụng cho tất cả phân loại/i,
+    })
+
+    fireEvent.change(bulkPriceInput, { target: { value: '30000000' } })
+    fireEvent.change(bulkStockInput, { target: { value: '100' } })
+    fireEvent.click(applyButton)
+
+    expect(onChange).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
+    const updated: VariantMatrixRow[] = onChange.mock.calls[0][0]
+    expect(updated).toHaveLength(3)
+    expect(updated[0].priceVND).toBe('30000000')
+    expect(updated[0].stockQuantity).toBe(100)
+    expect(updated[1].priceVND).toBe('30000000')
+    expect(updated[1].stockQuantity).toBe(100)
+    expect(updated[2].priceVND).toBe('30000000')
+    expect(updated[2].stockQuantity).toBe(100)
+  })
+
+  it('displays field errors when provided', () => {
+    render(
+      <VariantMatrixTable
+        variants={sampleVariants}
+        onChange={vi.fn()}
+        errors={{
+          'variants.0.priceVND': 'Giá biến thể phải là số không âm',
+          'variants.1.stockQuantity': 'Số lượng tồn kho không được âm',
+        }}
+      />
+    )
+
+    expect(
+      screen.getByText('Giá biến thể phải là số không âm')
+    ).toBeInTheDocument()
+    expect(
+      screen.getByText('Số lượng tồn kho không được âm')
+    ).toBeInTheDocument()
+  })
+})
