@@ -0,0 +1,223 @@
+import React, { useState } from 'react'
+import { Layers, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react'
+import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
+import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
+import { Switch } from '@/components/ui/switch'
+import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
+import {
+  Table,
+  TableBody,
+  TableCell,
+  TableHead,
+  TableHeader,
+  TableRow,
+} from '@/components/ui/table'
+import {
+  type VariantMatrixRow,
+  applyBulkValues,
+} from '@/lib/cartesian'
+
+export interface VariantMatrixTableProps {
+  variants: VariantMatrixRow[]
+  onChange: (variants: VariantMatrixRow[]) => void
+  errors?: Record<string, string>
+  disabled?: boolean
+}
+
+export const VariantMatrixTable: React.FC<VariantMatrixTableProps> = ({
+  variants,
+  onChange,
+  errors = {},
+  disabled = false,
+}) => {
+  const [bulkPrice, setBulkPrice] = useState('')
+  const [bulkStock, setBulkStock] = useState('')
+
+  const handleBulkApply = () => {
+    if (disabled || (!bulkPrice.trim() && !bulkStock.trim())) return
+
+    const parsedStock = bulkStock.trim() !== '' ? Number(bulkStock) : undefined
+    const updated = applyBulkValues(variants, {
+      priceVND: bulkPrice.trim() !== '' ? bulkPrice : undefined,
+      stockQuantity: parsedStock !== undefined && !isNaN(parsedStock) ? Math.max(0, parsedStock) : undefined,
+    })
+
+    onChange(updated)
+  }
+
+  const handlePriceChange = (index: number, value: string) => {
+    if (disabled) return
+    const updated = [...variants]
+    updated[index] = { ...updated[index], priceVND: value }
+    onChange(updated)
+  }
+
+  const handleStockChange = (index: number, value: string) => {
+    if (disabled) return
+    const parsed = parseInt(value, 10)
+    const updated = [...variants]
+    updated[index] = {
+      ...updated[index],
+      stockQuantity: isNaN(parsed) ? 0 : Math.max(0, parsed),
+    }
+    onChange(updated)
+  }
+
+  const handleActiveToggle = (index: number) => {
+    if (disabled) return
+    const updated = [...variants]
+    updated[index] = {
+      ...updated[index],
+      active: !updated[index].active,
+    }
+    onChange(updated)
+  }
+
+  if (variants.length === 0) {
+    return (
+      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 p-8 text-center bg-slate-50/50 text-slate-500">
+        <Layers className="w-10 h-10 mx-auto text-slate-400 mb-2" />
+        <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Chưa có phân loại hàng</p>
+        <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">
+          Vui lòng thêm các nhóm phân loại và giá trị ở trên để tạo bảng ma trận biến thể.
+        </p>
+      </div>
+    )
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <div className="space-y-4">
+      {/* Bulk Apply Bar */}
+      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 p-3.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/80">
+        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
+          <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-600 shrink-0" />
+          <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 uppercase tracking-wide">
+            Áp dụng hàng loạt
+          </span>
+        </div>
+
+        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
+          <div className="w-36">
+            <Input
+              type="text"
+              placeholder="Giá chung (VND)"
+              value={bulkPrice}
+              disabled={disabled}
+              onChange={(e) => setBulkPrice(e.target.value)}
+              className="h-8 text-xs bg-white"
+            />
+          </div>
+          <div className="w-28">
+            <Input
+              type="number"
+              min="0"
+              placeholder="Kho chung"
+              value={bulkStock}
+              disabled={disabled}
+              onChange={(e) => setBulkStock(e.target.value)}
+              className="h-8 text-xs bg-white"
+            />
+          </div>
+          <Button
+            type="button"
+            variant="primary"
+            size="sm"
+            disabled={disabled || (!bulkPrice.trim() && !bulkStock.trim())}
+            onClick={handleBulkApply}
+            className="h-8 px-3 text-xs"
+          >
+            Áp dụng cho tất cả phân loại
+          </Button>
+        </div>
+      </div>
+
+      {/* Matrix Table */}
+      <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden bg-white shadow-2xs">
+        <Table>
+          <TableHeader>
+            <TableRow>
+              <TableHead className="w-[35%] min-w-[160px]">Phân loại hàng</TableHead>
+              <TableHead className="w-[30%] min-w-[160px]">Giá bán (VND) <span className="text-rose-500">*</span></TableHead>
+              <TableHead className="w-[20%] min-w-[120px]">Kho hàng <span className="text-rose-500">*</span></TableHead>
+              <TableHead className="w-[15%] min-w-[90px] text-center">Kích hoạt</TableHead>
+            </TableRow>
+          </TableHeader>
+          <TableBody>
+            {variants.map((row, idx) => {
+              const priceError =
+                errors[`variants.${idx}.priceVND`] ||
+                errors[`variants.${idx}.priceVnd`] ||
+                errors[`variants[${idx}].priceVND`] ||
+                errors[`variants[${idx}].priceVnd`]
+              const stockError =
+                errors[`variants.${idx}.stockQuantity`] ||
+                errors[`variants[${idx}].stockQuantity`]
+
+              return (
+                <TableRow key={row.combinationKey || row.id || idx}>
+                  <TableCell className="font-medium text-slate-800">
+                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
+                      <Badge variant="outline" className="font-medium text-xs bg-slate-50 border-slate-200">
+                        {row.combinationLabel}
+                      </Badge>
+                      {!row.active && (
+                        <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 font-normal">
+                          (Tắt)
+                        </span>
+                      )}
+                    </div>
+                  </TableCell>
+
+                  <TableCell>
+                    <div className="space-y-1">
+                      <Input
+                        type="text"
+                        placeholder="Nhập giá (VND)"
+                        value={row.priceVND}
+                        disabled={disabled}
+                        error={Boolean(priceError)}
+                        onChange={(e) => handlePriceChange(idx, e.target.value)}
+                        className="h-9 text-sm"
+                      />
+                      {priceError && (
+                        <p className="text-xs text-rose-500 font-medium">{priceError}</p>
+                      )}
+                    </div>
+                  </TableCell>
+
+                  <TableCell>
+                    <div className="space-y-1">
+                      <Input
+                        type="number"
+                        min="0"
+                        placeholder="0"
+                        value={row.stockQuantity === 0 ? '0' : row.stockQuantity || ''}
+                        disabled={disabled}
+                        error={Boolean(stockError)}
+                        onChange={(e) => handleStockChange(idx, e.target.value)}
+                        className="h-9 text-sm"
+                      />
+                      {stockError && (
+                        <p className="text-xs text-rose-500 font-medium">{stockError}</p>
+                      )}
+                    </div>
+                  </TableCell>
+
+                  <TableCell className="text-center">
+                    <div className="flex justify-center items-center">
+                      <Switch
+                        checked={row.active}
+                        disabled={disabled}
+                        onChange={() => handleActiveToggle(idx)}
+                      />
+                    </div>
+                  </TableCell>
+                </TableRow>
+              )
+            })}
+          </TableBody>
+        </Table>
+      </div>
+    </div>
+  )
+}

+import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest'
+import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react'
+import {
+  AlertDialog,
+  AlertDialogContent,
+  AlertDialogHeader,
+  AlertDialogFooter,
+  AlertDialogTitle,
+  AlertDialogDescription,
+  AlertDialogAction,
+  AlertDialogCancel,
+} from './alert-dialog'
+
+describe('AlertDialog', () => {
+  it('does not render content when open is false', () => {
+    render(
+      <AlertDialog open={false}>
+        <AlertDialogContent>
+          <AlertDialogTitle>Xác nhận xóa</AlertDialogTitle>
+        </AlertDialogContent>
+      </AlertDialog>
+    )
+
+    expect(screen.queryByText('Xác nhận xóa')).not.toBeInTheDocument()
+  })
+
+  it('renders title, description, and action buttons when open is true', () => {
+    const onOpenChange = vi.fn()
+    const onConfirm = vi.fn()
+
+    render(
+      <AlertDialog open={true} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
+        <AlertDialogContent>
+          <AlertDialogHeader>
+            <AlertDialogTitle>Xác nhận xóa sản phẩm</AlertDialogTitle>
+            <AlertDialogDescription>
+              Bạn có chắc chắn muốn xóa sản phẩm này không?
+            </AlertDialogDescription>
+          </AlertDialogHeader>
+          <AlertDialogFooter>
+            <AlertDialogCancel>Hủy</AlertDialogCancel>
+            <AlertDialogAction onClick={onConfirm}>Xóa</AlertDialogAction>
+          </AlertDialogFooter>
+        </AlertDialogContent>
+      </AlertDialog>
+    )
+
+    expect(screen.getByRole('alertdialog')).toBeInTheDocument()
+    expect(screen.getByText('Xác nhận xóa sản phẩm')).toBeInTheDocument()
+    expect(
+      screen.getByText('Bạn có chắc chắn muốn xóa sản phẩm này không?')
+    ).toBeInTheDocument()
+
+    const cancelBtn = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Hủy' })
+    fireEvent.click(cancelBtn)
+    expect(onOpenChange).toHaveBeenCalledWith(false)
+
+    const confirmBtn = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Xóa' })
+    fireEvent.click(confirmBtn)
+    expect(onConfirm).toHaveBeenCalled()
+  })
+
+  it('closes dialog on Escape key press', () => {
+    const onOpenChange = vi.fn()
+
+    render(
+      <AlertDialog open={true} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
+        <AlertDialogContent>
+          <AlertDialogTitle>Tiêu đề</AlertDialogTitle>
+        </AlertDialogContent>
+      </AlertDialog>
+    )
+
+    fireEvent.keyDown(document, { key: 'Escape' })
+    expect(onOpenChange).toHaveBeenCalledWith(false)
+  })
+})
diff --git a/frontend-admin/src/components/ui/alert-dialog.tsx b/frontend-admin/src/components/ui/alert-dialog.tsx

+import * as React from 'react'
+import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'
+import { buttonVariants } from './button'
+
+export interface AlertDialogProps {
+  open?: boolean
+  onOpenChange?: (open: boolean) => void
+  children?: React.ReactNode
+}
+
+interface AlertDialogContextValue {
+  open: boolean
+  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
+}
+
+const AlertDialogContext = React.createContext<AlertDialogContextValue>({
+  open: false,
+  onOpenChange: () => {},
+})
+
+export function AlertDialog({
+  open = false,
+  onOpenChange = () => {},
+  children,
+}: AlertDialogProps) {
+  return (
+    <AlertDialogContext.Provider value={{ open, onOpenChange }}>
+      {open ? children : null}
+    </AlertDialogContext.Provider>
+  )
+}
+
+export function AlertDialogContent({
+  className,
+  children,
+  ...props
+}: React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>) {
+  const { open, onOpenChange } = React.useContext(AlertDialogContext)
+
+  React.useEffect(() => {
+    if (!open) return
+    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
+      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
+        onOpenChange(false)
+      }
+    }
+    document.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown)
+    return () => document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown)
+  }, [open, onOpenChange])
+
+  return (
+    <div
+      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4"
+      role="alertdialog"
+      aria-modal="true"
+    >
+      {/* Backdrop */}
+      <div
+        className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-950/50 backdrop-blur-xs transition-opacity animate-in fade-in duration-200"
+        onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}
+        aria-hidden="true"
+      />
+      {/* Dialog card */}
+      <div
+        className={cn(
+          'relative z-50 w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl bg-white p-6 text-slate-900 shadow-2xl border border-slate-200/80 animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-200',
+          className
+        )}
+        {...props}
+      >
+        {children}
+      </div>
+    </div>
+  )
+}
+
+export function AlertDialogHeader({
+  className,
+  ...props
+}: React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>) {
+  return <div className={cn('flex flex-col space-y-2 text-left', className)} {...props} />
+}
+
+export function AlertDialogFooter({
+  className,
+  ...props
+}: React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>) {
+  return (
+    <div
+      className={cn(
+        'flex flex-col-reverse sm:flex-row sm:justify-end gap-2 sm:gap-3 mt-6',
+        className
+      )}
+      {...props}
+    />
+  )
+}
+
+export function AlertDialogTitle({
+  className,
+  ...props
+}: React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLHeadingElement>) {
+  return (
+    <h2
+      className={cn('text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight', className)}
+      {...props}
+    />
+  )
+}
+
+export function AlertDialogDescription({
+  className,
+  ...props
+}: React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLParagraphElement>) {
+  return (
+    <p
+      className={cn('text-sm text-slate-500 leading-relaxed', className)}
+      {...props}
+    />
+  )
+}
+
+export interface AlertDialogActionProps
+  extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
+  variant?: 'default' | 'primary' | 'destructive' | 'outline' | 'secondary' | 'ghost'
+  isLoading?: boolean
+}
+
+export function AlertDialogAction({
+  className,
+  variant = 'destructive',
+  disabled,
+  isLoading,
+  children,
+  ...props
+}: AlertDialogActionProps) {
+  return (
+    <button
+      className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant }), className)}
+      disabled={disabled || isLoading}
+      {...props}
+    >
+      {isLoading && (
+        <svg
+          className="animate-spin -ml-1 mr-2 h-4 w-4 text-current"
+          fill="none"
+          viewBox="0 0 24 24"
+        >
+          <circle
+            className="opacity-25"
+            cx="12"
+            cy="12"
+            r="10"
+            stroke="currentColor"
+            strokeWidth="4"
+          />
+          <path
+            className="opacity-75"
+            fill="currentColor"
+            d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z"
+          />
+        </svg>
+      )}
+      {children}
+    </button>
+  )
+}
+
+export function AlertDialogCancel({
+  className,
+  onClick,
+  disabled,
+  ...props
+}: React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement>) {
+  const { onOpenChange } = React.useContext(AlertDialogContext)
+  return (
+    <button
+      type="button"
+      className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant: 'outline' }), className)}
+      disabled={disabled}
+      onClick={(e) => {
+        if (onClick) onClick(e)
+        onOpenChange(false)
+      }}
+      {...props}
+    />
+  )

+import * as React from 'react'

+const badgeVariants = cva(
+  'inline-flex items-center rounded-full border px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-ring focus:ring-offset-2',
+  {
+    variants: {
+      variant: {
+        default:
+          'border-transparent bg-slate-900 text-white shadow hover:bg-slate-800',
+        secondary:
+          'border-transparent bg-slate-100 text-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-200',
+        destructive:
+          'border-transparent bg-rose-500 text-white shadow hover:bg-rose-600',
+        outline: 'text-slate-950 border-slate-200',
+        success: 'border-transparent bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800',
+      },
+    },
+    defaultVariants: {
+      variant: 'default',
+    },
+  }
+)

+  extends React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>,
+    VariantProps<typeof badgeVariants> {}

+import * as React from 'react'
+import { cva, type VariantProps } from 'class-variance-authority'
+import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'
+
+const buttonVariants = cva(
+  'inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-xl text-sm font-medium transition-all duration-200 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-blue-600 focus-visible:ring-offset-2 disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 active:scale-[0.98] cursor-pointer select-none',
+  {
+    variants: {
+      variant: {
+        default:
+          'bg-slate-900 text-white shadow-sm hover:bg-slate-800 hover:shadow active:bg-slate-950',
+        primary:
+          'bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-sm shadow-blue-600/20 hover:bg-blue-700 hover:shadow-md hover:shadow-blue-600/30 active:bg-blue-800',
+        secondary:
+          'bg-slate-100 text-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-200/80 active:bg-slate-300',
+        outline:
+          'border border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 hover:border-slate-300 active:bg-slate-100',
+        ghost:
+          'text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100/80 active:bg-slate-200',
+        destructive:
+          'bg-rose-500 text-white shadow-sm hover:bg-rose-600 active:bg-rose-700',
+        link: 'text-blue-600 underline-offset-4 hover:underline p-0 h-auto',
+      },
+      size: {
+        default: 'h-10 px-4 py-2',
+        sm: 'h-8 rounded-lg px-3 text-xs',
+        lg: 'h-12 rounded-xl px-6 text-base font-semibold',
+        icon: 'h-10 w-10 p-0',
+        iconSm: 'h-8 w-8 p-0 rounded-lg',
+      },
+    },
+    defaultVariants: {
+      variant: 'default',
+      size: 'default',
+    },
+  }
+)
+
+export interface ButtonProps
+  extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement>,
+    VariantProps<typeof buttonVariants> {
+  isLoading?: boolean
+}
+
+const Button = React.forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, ButtonProps>(
+  ({ className, variant, size, isLoading, children, disabled, ...props }, ref) => {
+    return (
+      <button
+        className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant, size, className }))}
+        ref={ref}
+        disabled={disabled || isLoading}
+        {...props}
+      >
+        {isLoading && (
+          <svg
+            className="animate-spin -ml-1 mr-2 h-4 w-4 text-current"
+            fill="none"
+            viewBox="0 0 24 24"
+          >
+            <circle
+              className="opacity-25"
+              cx="12"
+              cy="12"
+              r="10"
+              stroke="currentColor"
+              strokeWidth="4"
+            />
+            <path
+              className="opacity-75"
+              fill="currentColor"
+              d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z"
+            />
+          </svg>
+        )}
+        {children}
+      </button>
+    )
+  }
+)
+Button.displayName = 'Button'
+
+export { Button, buttonVariants }

+            type="checkbox"
+            className="sr-only peer"
+            checked={checked}
+            ref={ref}
+            {...props}
+          />
+          <div
+            className={cn(
+              "w-11 h-6 bg-slate-200 peer-focus:outline-none peer-focus:ring-2 peer-focus:ring-blue-600/30 rounded-full peer peer-checked:after:translate-x-full peer-checked:after:border-white after:content-[''] after:absolute after:top-[2px] after:left-[2px] after:bg-white after:border-slate-300 after:border after:rounded-full after:h-5 after:w-5 after:transition-all peer-checked:bg-blue-600 transition-colors",
+              className
+            )}
+          />
+        </div>
+        {label && <span className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">{label}</span>}

+    )
+  }

diff --git a/frontend-admin/src/context/AuthContext.test.tsx b/frontend-admin/src/context/AuthContext.test.tsx
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..d0565b6
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend-admin/src/context/AuthContext.test.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,159 @@
+import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach } from 'vitest'
+import { renderHook, act, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react'
+import type { ReactNode } from 'react'
+import { AuthProvider, useAuth } from './AuthContext'
+import { authApi } from '@/api/auth'
+import { tokenStore } from '@/api/tokenStore'
+
+vi.mock('@/api/auth', () => ({
+  authApi: {
+    login: vi.fn(),
+    refresh: vi.fn(),
+    logout: vi.fn(),
+    getMe: vi.fn(),
+  },
+}))
+
+describe('AuthContext', () => {
+  beforeEach(() => {
+    vi.clearAllMocks()
+    tokenStore.clearToken()
+  })
+
+  const wrapper = ({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) => (
+    <AuthProvider>{children}</AuthProvider>
+  )
+
+  it('restores admin session on initial load if refresh succeeds', async () => {
+    const adminUser = { id: 'admin-1', email: 'admin@auraphone.vn', role: 'admin' }
+    vi.mocked(authApi.refresh).mockResolvedValueOnce({
+      user: adminUser,
+      accessToken: 'token-admin-123',
+    })
+
+    const { result } = renderHook(() => useAuth(), { wrapper })
+
+    expect(result.current.isLoading).toBe(true)
+
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(result.current.isLoading).toBe(false)
+    })
+
+    expect(result.current.isAuthenticated).toBe(true)
+    expect(result.current.user).toEqual(adminUser)
+    expect(result.current.role).toBe('admin')
+    expect(tokenStore.getToken()).toBe('token-admin-123')
+  })
+
+  it('sets unauthenticated state if refresh fails on load', async () => {
+    vi.mocked(authApi.refresh).mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error('No session'))
+
+    const { result } = renderHook(() => useAuth(), { wrapper })
+
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(result.current.isLoading).toBe(false)
+    })
+
+    expect(result.current.isAuthenticated).toBe(false)
+    expect(result.current.user).toBeNull()
+    expect(result.current.role).toBeNull()
+  })
+
+  it('rejects session if refreshed user is not admin', async () => {
+    const customerUser = { id: 'user-1', email: 'customer@auraphone.vn', role: 'customer' }
+    vi.mocked(authApi.refresh).mockResolvedValueOnce({
+      user: customerUser,
+      accessToken: 'token-customer-123',
+    })
+
+    const { result } = renderHook(() => useAuth(), { wrapper })
+
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(result.current.isLoading).toBe(false)
+    })
+
+    expect(result.current.isAuthenticated).toBe(false)
+    expect(result.current.user).toBeNull()
+    expect(tokenStore.getToken()).toBeNull()
+  })
+
+  it('logs in successfully with admin credentials', async () => {
+    vi.mocked(authApi.refresh).mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error('No session'))
+    const adminUser = { id: 'admin-1', email: 'admin@auraphone.vn', role: 'admin' }
+    vi.mocked(authApi.login).mockResolvedValueOnce({
+      user: adminUser,
+      accessToken: 'jwt-admin-token',
+    })
+
+    const { result } = renderHook(() => useAuth(), { wrapper })
+
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(result.current.isLoading).toBe(false)
+    })
+
+    await act(async () => {
+      await result.current.login('admin@auraphone.vn', 'password123')
+    })
+
+    expect(authApi.login).toHaveBeenCalledWith('admin@auraphone.vn', 'password123')
+    expect(result.current.isAuthenticated).toBe(true)
+    expect(result.current.user).toEqual(adminUser)
+    expect(result.current.role).toBe('admin')
+    expect(tokenStore.getToken()).toBe('jwt-admin-token')
+  })
+
+  it('rejects login with Vietnamese error if user role is not admin', async () => {
+    vi.mocked(authApi.refresh).mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error('No session'))
+    const customerUser = { id: 'user-2', email: 'user@auraphone.vn', role: 'customer' }
+    vi.mocked(authApi.login).mockResolvedValueOnce({
+      user: customerUser,
+      accessToken: 'jwt-customer-token',
+    })
+
+    const { result } = renderHook(() => useAuth(), { wrapper })
+
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(result.current.isLoading).toBe(false)
+    })
+
+    let errorThrown: unknown
+    await act(async () => {
+      try {
+        await result.current.login('user@auraphone.vn', 'password123')
+      } catch (err) {
+        errorThrown = err
+      }
+    })
+
+    expect(errorThrown).toBeDefined()
+    expect((errorThrown as Error).message).toContain('Tài khoản không có quyền truy cập trang quản trị')
+    expect(result.current.isAuthenticated).toBe(false)
+    expect(result.current.user).toBeNull()
+    expect(tokenStore.getToken()).toBeNull()
+  })
+
+  it('logs out and clears authentication state and tokens', async () => {
+    const adminUser = { id: 'admin-1', email: 'admin@auraphone.vn', role: 'admin' }
+    vi.mocked(authApi.refresh).mockResolvedValueOnce({
+      user: adminUser,
+      accessToken: 'token-admin-123',
+    })
+    vi.mocked(authApi.logout).mockResolvedValueOnce()
+
+    const { result } = renderHook(() => useAuth(), { wrapper })
+
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(result.current.isAuthenticated).toBe(true)
+    })
+
+    await act(async () => {
+      await result.current.logout()
+    })
+
+    expect(authApi.logout).toHaveBeenCalled()
+    expect(result.current.isAuthenticated).toBe(false)
+    expect(result.current.user).toBeNull()
+    expect(result.current.role).toBeNull()
+    expect(tokenStore.getToken()).toBeNull()
+  })
+})

new file mode 100644
index 0000000..c2325ac

+++ b/frontend-admin/src/context/AuthContext.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,87 @@
+import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState, useCallback, type ReactNode } from 'react'
+import { authApi } from '@/api/auth'
+import { tokenStore } from '@/api/tokenStore'
+import { ApiError, type User } from '@/api/types'
+
+export interface AuthContextType {
+  user: User | null
+  role: string | null
+  isAuthenticated: boolean
+  isLoading: boolean
+  login: (email: string, password: string) => Promise<void>
+  logout: () => Promise<void>
+}
+
+const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextType | undefined>(undefined)
+
+export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
+  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null)
+  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true)
+
+  const restoreSession = useCallback(async () => {
+    try {
+      const res = await authApi.refresh()
+      if (res?.user && res.user.role === 'admin') {
+        tokenStore.setToken(res.accessToken)
+        setUser(res.user)
+      } else {
+        tokenStore.clearToken()
+        setUser(null)
+      }
+    } catch {
+      tokenStore.clearToken()
+      setUser(null)
+    } finally {
+      setIsLoading(false)
+    }
+  }, [])
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    restoreSession()
+  }, [restoreSession])
+
+  const login = useCallback(async (email: string, password: string) => {
+    const res = await authApi.login(email, password)
+    if (!res?.user || res.user.role !== 'admin') {
+      tokenStore.clearToken()
+      setUser(null)
+      throw new ApiError(403, {
+        code: 'forbidden',
+        message: 'Tài khoản không có quyền truy cập trang quản trị',
+      })
+    }
+
+    tokenStore.setToken(res.accessToken)
+    setUser(res.user)
+  }, [])
+
+  const logout = useCallback(async () => {
+    try {
+      await authApi.logout()
+    } catch {
+      // Ignore network errors on logout
+    } finally {
+      tokenStore.clearToken()
+      setUser(null)
+    }
+  }, [])
+
+  const value: AuthContextType = {
+    user,
+    role: user?.role ?? null,
+    isAuthenticated: Boolean(user && user.role === 'admin'),
+    isLoading,
+    login,
+    logout,
+  }
+
+  return <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</AuthContext.Provider>
+}
+
+export function useAuth(): AuthContextType {
+  const context = useContext(AuthContext)
+  if (!context) {
+    throw new Error('useAuth must be used within an AuthProvider')
+  }
+  return context
+}

+import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest'
+import {
+  generateVariantMatrix,
+  applyBulkValues,
+  type OptionGroupItem,
+  type VariantMatrixRow,
+} from './cartesian'
+
+describe('Cartesian product & variant matrix helper', () => {
+  it('returns empty array when option groups are empty', () => {
+    const matrix = generateVariantMatrix([])
+    expect(matrix).toEqual([])
+  })
+
+  it('returns empty array when option groups have no values', () => {
+    const groups: OptionGroupItem[] = [
+      { id: 'g1', label: 'Màu sắc', values: [] },
+    ]
+    const matrix = generateVariantMatrix(groups)
+    expect(matrix).toEqual([])
+  })
+
+  it('generates variants for a single option group', () => {
+    const groups: OptionGroupItem[] = [
+      {
+        id: 'g-color',
+        label: 'Màu sắc',
+        values: [
+          { id: 'v-black', label: 'Đen' },
+          { id: 'v-white', label: 'Trắng' },
+        ],
+      },
+    ]
+
+    const matrix = generateVariantMatrix(groups)
+    expect(matrix).toHaveLength(2)
+    expect(matrix[0].combinationLabel).toBe('Đen')
+    expect(matrix[0].optionValueIds).toEqual({ 'g-color': 'v-black' })
+    expect(matrix[0].active).toBe(true)
+    expect(matrix[0].stockQuantity).toBe(0)
+    expect(matrix[0].priceVND).toBe('')
+
+    expect(matrix[1].combinationLabel).toBe('Trắng')
+    expect(matrix[1].optionValueIds).toEqual({ 'g-color': 'v-white' })
+  })
+
+  it('generates Cartesian product for 2 option groups (2 x 3 = 6 variants)', () => {
+    const groups: OptionGroupItem[] = [
+      {
+        id: 'g-color',
+        label: 'Màu sắc',
+        values: [
+          { id: 'v-black', label: 'Đen' },
+          { id: 'v-white', label: 'Trắng' },
+        ],
+      },
+      {
+        id: 'g-storage',
+        label: 'Dung lượng',
+        values: [
+          { id: 'v-128', label: '128GB' },
+          { id: 'v-256', label: '256GB' },
+          { id: 'v-512', label: '512GB' },
+        ],
+      },
+    ]
+
+    const matrix = generateVariantMatrix(groups)
+    expect(matrix).toHaveLength(6)
+
+    expect(matrix.map((r) => r.combinationLabel)).toEqual([
+      'Đen / 128GB',
+      'Đen / 256GB',
+      'Đen / 512GB',
+      'Trắng / 128GB',
+      'Trắng / 256GB',
+      'Trắng / 512GB',
+    ])
+
+    expect(matrix[0].optionValueIds).toEqual({
+      'g-color': 'v-black',
+      'g-storage': 'v-128',
+    })
+  })
+
+  it('generates Cartesian product for 3 option groups (2 x 2 x 2 = 8 variants)', () => {
+    const groups: OptionGroupItem[] = [
+      {
+        id: 'g1',
+        label: 'Màu sắc',
+        values: [
+          { id: 'v1', label: 'Đen' },
+          { id: 'v2', label: 'Trắng' },
+        ],
+      },
+      {
+        id: 'g2',
+        label: 'Dung lượng',
+        values: [
+          { id: 'v3', label: '128GB' },
+          { id: 'v4', label: '256GB' },
+        ],
+      },
+      {
+        id: 'g3',
+        label: 'Bảo hành',
+        values: [
+          { id: 'v5', label: '12 tháng' },
+          { id: 'v6', label: '24 tháng' },
+        ],
+      },
+    ]
+
+    const matrix = generateVariantMatrix(groups)
+    expect(matrix).toHaveLength(8)
+    expect(matrix[0].combinationLabel).toBe('Đen / 128GB / 12 tháng')
+    expect(matrix[7].combinationLabel).toBe('Trắng / 256GB / 24 tháng')
+  })
+
+  it('reconciles with existing variants preserving price, stock, active and id', () => {
+    const groups: OptionGroupItem[] = [
+      {
+        id: 'g-color',
+        label: 'Màu sắc',
+        values: [
+          { id: 'v-black', label: 'Đen' },
+          { id: 'v-white', label: 'Trắng' },
+        ],
+      },
+      {
+        id: 'g-storage',
+        label: 'Dung lượng',
+        values: [
+          { id: 'v-128', label: '128GB' },
+          { id: 'v-256', label: '256GB' },
+        ],
+      },
+    ]
+
+    const existing: VariantMatrixRow[] = [
+      {
+        id: 'var-1',
+        optionValueIds: { 'g-color': 'v-black', 'g-storage': 'v-128' },
+        combinationKey: 'g-color:v-black|g-storage:v-128',
+        combinationLabel: 'Đen / 128GB',
+        priceVND: '25000000',
+        stockQuantity: 15,
+        active: true,
+      },
+      {
+        id: 'var-2',
+        optionValueIds: { 'g-color': 'v-black', 'g-storage': 'v-256' },
+        combinationKey: 'g-color:v-black|g-storage:v-256',
+        combinationLabel: 'Đen / 256GB',
+        priceVND: '28000000',
+        stockQuantity: 5,
+        active: false,
+      },
+    ]
+
+    const matrix = generateVariantMatrix(groups, existing)
+    expect(matrix).toHaveLength(4)
+
+    // First variant matches existing
+    const var1 = matrix.find((r) => r.combinationLabel === 'Đen / 128GB')!
+    expect(var1.id).toBe('var-1')
+    expect(var1.priceVND).toBe('25000000')
+    expect(var1.stockQuantity).toBe(15)
+    expect(var1.active).toBe(true)
+
+    // Second variant matches existing
+    const var2 = matrix.find((r) => r.combinationLabel === 'Đen / 256GB')!
+    expect(var2.id).toBe('var-2')
+    expect(var2.priceVND).toBe('28000000')
+    expect(var2.stockQuantity).toBe(5)
+    expect(var2.active).toBe(false)
+
+    // New combination has default values
+    const var3 = matrix.find((r) => r.combinationLabel === 'Trắng / 128GB')!
+    expect(var3.priceVND).toBe('')
+    expect(var3.stockQuantity).toBe(0)
+    expect(var3.active).toBe(true)
+    expect(var3.id).toBeDefined()
+  })
+
+  it('bulk applies price and stock to all variants', () => {
+    const variants: VariantMatrixRow[] = [
+      {
+        id: '1',
+        optionValueIds: { g: 'v1' },
+        combinationKey: 'g:v1',
+        combinationLabel: 'V1',
+        priceVND: '1000',
+        stockQuantity: 5,
+        active: true,
+      },
+      {
+        id: '2',
+        optionValueIds: { g: 'v2' },
+        combinationKey: 'g:v2',
+        combinationLabel: 'V2',
+        priceVND: '2000',
+        stockQuantity: 10,
+        active: false,
+      },
+    ]
+
+    // Apply price only
+    const updatedPrice = applyBulkValues(variants, { priceVND: '3500000' })
+    expect(updatedPrice[0].priceVND).toBe('3500000')
+    expect(updatedPrice[0].stockQuantity).toBe(5)
+    expect(updatedPrice[1].priceVND).toBe('3500000')
+    expect(updatedPrice[1].stockQuantity).toBe(10)
+
+    // Apply stock only
+    const updatedStock = applyBulkValues(variants, { stockQuantity: 50 })
+    expect(updatedStock[0].priceVND).toBe('1000')
+    expect(updatedStock[0].stockQuantity).toBe(50)
+    expect(updatedStock[1].priceVND).toBe('2000')
+    expect(updatedStock[1].stockQuantity).toBe(50)
+
+    // Apply both
+    const updatedBoth = applyBulkValues(variants, {
+      priceVND: '5000000',
+      stockQuantity: 20,
+    })
+    expect(updatedBoth[0].priceVND).toBe('5000000')
+    expect(updatedBoth[0].stockQuantity).toBe(20)
+    expect(updatedBoth[1].priceVND).toBe('5000000')
+    expect(updatedBoth[1].stockQuantity).toBe(20)
+  })
+})
diff --git a/frontend-admin/src/lib/cartesian.ts b/frontend-admin/src/lib/cartesian.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..62a23b7
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend-admin/src/lib/cartesian.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,153 @@
+export interface OptionValueItem {
+  id: string
+  label: string
+  position?: number
+}
+
+export interface OptionGroupItem {
+  id: string
+  label: string
+  position?: number
+  values: OptionValueItem[]
+}
+
+export interface VariantMatrixRow {
+  id?: string
+  optionValueIds: Record<string, string>
+  combinationKey: string
+  combinationLabel: string
+  priceVND: string
+  stockQuantity: number
+  active: boolean
+}
+
+export function buildCombinationKey(optionValueIds: Record<string, string>): string {
+  const sortedKeys = Object.keys(optionValueIds).sort()
+  return sortedKeys.map((key) => `${key}:${optionValueIds[key]}`).join('|')
+}
+
+export function buildCombinationLabel(
+  optionValueIds: Record<string, string>,
+  optionGroups: OptionGroupItem[]
+): string {
+  const labels: string[] = []
+  for (const group of optionGroups) {
+    const valueId = optionValueIds[group.id]
+    if (valueId) {
+      const val = group.values.find((v) => v.id === valueId)
+      if (val) {
+        labels.push(val.label)
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  return labels.join(' / ')
+}
+
+/**
+ * Generates Cartesian product matrix of variants from option groups,
+ * reconciling with any existing variant definitions to preserve prices, stock, and IDs.
+ */
+export function generateVariantMatrix(
+  optionGroups: OptionGroupItem[],
+  existingVariants?: Partial<VariantMatrixRow>[]
+): VariantMatrixRow[] {
+  // Only process valid groups that have non-empty labels and at least 1 value
+  const activeGroups = optionGroups.filter(
+    (g) => g.label.trim() !== '' && g.values && g.values.length > 0
+  )
+
+  if (activeGroups.length === 0) {
+    return []
+  }
+
+  // Cartesian product accumulator
+  let combinations: { groupId: string; valueId: string; label: string }[][] = [[]]
+
+  for (const group of activeGroups) {
+    const nextCombinations: { groupId: string; valueId: string; label: string }[][] = []
+    for (const current of combinations) {
+      for (const val of group.values) {
+        if (val.label.trim() !== '') {
+          nextCombinations.push([
+            ...current,
+            { groupId: group.id, valueId: val.id, label: val.label },
+          ])
+        }
+      }
+    }
+    combinations = nextCombinations
+  }
+
+  if (combinations.length === 0) {
+    return []
+  }
+
+  return combinations.map((combo) => {
+    const optionValueIds: Record<string, string> = {}
+    for (const item of combo) {
+      optionValueIds[item.groupId] = item.valueId
+    }
+
+    const key = activeGroups.map((g) => `${g.id}:${optionValueIds[g.id]}`).join('|')
+    const label = activeGroups
+      .map((g) => combo.find((c) => c.groupId === g.id)?.label ?? '')
+      .join(' / ')
+
+    // Reconcile with existing variants
+    const match = existingVariants?.find((ev) => {
+      if (!ev.optionValueIds) return false
+      const evKeys = Object.keys(ev.optionValueIds)
+      const currentKeys = Object.keys(optionValueIds)
+      if (evKeys.length !== currentKeys.length) return false
+      return currentKeys.every((k) => ev.optionValueIds?.[k] === optionValueIds[k])
+    })
+
+    if (match) {
+      const rawPrice =
+        match.priceVND !== undefined
+          ? String(match.priceVND)
+          : (match as Record<string, unknown>).priceVnd !== undefined
+            ? String((match as Record<string, unknown>).priceVnd)
+            : ''
+
+      return {
+        id: match.id || crypto.randomUUID(),
+        optionValueIds,
+        combinationKey: key,
+        combinationLabel: label,
+        priceVND: rawPrice,
+        stockQuantity: typeof match.stockQuantity === 'number' ? match.stockQuantity : 0,
+        active: typeof match.active === 'boolean' ? match.active : true,
+      }
+    }
+
+    return {
+      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
+      optionValueIds,
+      combinationKey: key,
+      combinationLabel: label,
+      priceVND: '',
+      stockQuantity: 0,
+      active: true,
+    }
+  })
+}
+
+/**
+ * Bulk applies price and/or stock quantity across an entire array of variants.
+ */
+export function applyBulkValues(
+  variants: VariantMatrixRow[],
+  bulk: { priceVND?: string; stockQuantity?: number }
+): VariantMatrixRow[] {
+  return variants.map((v) => {
+    const updated = { ...v }
+    if (bulk.priceVND !== undefined && bulk.priceVND.trim() !== '') {
+      updated.priceVND = bulk.priceVND.trim()
+    }
+    if (bulk.stockQuantity !== undefined && !isNaN(bulk.stockQuantity)) {
+      updated.stockQuantity = bulk.stockQuantity
+    }
+    return updated
+  })
+}
diff --git a/frontend-admin/src/lib/utils.ts b/frontend-admin/src/lib/utils.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..8ea44db
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend-admin/src/lib/utils.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,24 @@
+import { type ClassValue, clsx } from 'clsx'
+import { twMerge } from 'tailwind-merge'
+
+export function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]): string {
+  return twMerge(clsx(inputs))
+}
+
+/**
+ * Format raw VND integer string/number to Vietnamese standard currency format (e.g. "25.990.000 ₫")
+ */
+export function formatVND(amount: string | number | null | undefined): string {
+  if (amount === null || amount === undefined || amount === '') {
+    return '0 ₫'
+  }
+  const numeric = typeof amount === 'number' ? amount : Number(amount)
+  if (isNaN(numeric)) {
+    return `${amount} ₫`
+  }
+  return new Intl.NumberFormat('vi-VN', {
+    style: 'currency',
+    currency: 'VND',
+    maximumFractionDigits: 0,
+  }).format(numeric)
+}

+import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest'
+import { productFormSchema } from './product'
+
+describe('productFormSchema', () => {
+  describe('Simple Mode (hasVariants: false)', () => {
+    it('validates a valid simple product', () => {
+      const validData = {
+        name: 'iPhone 15 Pro',
+        model: 'A3106',
+        brand: 'Apple',
+        categoryId: 'cat-1',
+        price: '25000000',
+        stockQuantity: 10,
+        description: 'Flagship phone',
+        featured: false,
+        imageUrls: '',
+        hasVariants: false,
+        optionGroups: [],
+        variants: [],
+      }
+
+      const result = productFormSchema.safeParse(validData)
+      expect(result.success).toBe(true)
+    })
+
+    it('rejects empty price or negative price in simple mode', () => {
+      const emptyPrice = {
+        name: 'iPhone 15 Pro',
+        model: 'A3106',
+        brand: 'Apple',
+        categoryId: 'cat-1',
+        price: '',
+        stockQuantity: 10,
+        hasVariants: false,
+      }
+
+      const res1 = productFormSchema.safeParse(emptyPrice)
+      expect(res1.success).toBe(false)
+      if (!res1.success) {
+        expect(res1.error.issues.some((i) => i.path.includes('price'))).toBe(true)
+      }
+
+      const negativePrice = { ...emptyPrice, price: '-500' }
+      const res2 = productFormSchema.safeParse(negativePrice)
+      expect(res2.success).toBe(false)
+      if (!res2.success) {
+        expect(res2.error.issues.some((i) => i.message === 'Giá phải lớn hơn hoặc bằng 0')).toBe(true)
+      }
+    })
+
+    it('rejects negative stock in simple mode', () => {
+      const negativeStock = {
+        name: 'iPhone 15 Pro',
+        brand: 'Apple',
+        categoryId: 'cat-1',
+        price: '25000000',
+        stockQuantity: -5,
+        hasVariants: false,
+      }
+
+      const res = productFormSchema.safeParse(negativeStock)
+      expect(res.success).toBe(false)
+      if (!res.success) {
+        expect(res.error.issues.some((i) => i.message === 'Số lượng tồn kho không được âm')).toBe(true)
+      }
+    })
+  })
+
+  describe('Variant Mode (hasVariants: true)', () => {
+    it('validates a valid multi-variant product without requiring simple price/stock', () => {
+      const validData = {
+        name: 'iPhone 15 Pro',
+        model: 'A3106',
+        brand: 'Apple',
+        categoryId: 'cat-1',
+        price: '',
+        stockQuantity: 0,
+        description: 'Flagship phone',
+        featured: true,
+        imageUrls: '',
+        hasVariants: true,
+        optionGroups: [
+          {
+            id: 'g-color',
+            label: 'Màu sắc',
+            position: 0,
+            values: [
+              { id: 'v-black', label: 'Titan Đen', position: 0 },
+              { id: 'v-white', label: 'Titan Trắng', position: 1 },
+            ],
+          },
+        ],
+        variants: [
+          {
+            id: 'var-1',
+            optionValueIds: { 'g-color': 'v-black' },
+            priceVND: '28000000',
+            stockQuantity: 10,
+            active: true,
+          },
+          {
+            id: 'var-2',
+            optionValueIds: { 'g-color': 'v-white' },
+            priceVND: '28500000',
+            stockQuantity: 5,
+            active: true,
+          },
+        ],
+      }
+
+      const result = productFormSchema.safeParse(validData)
+      expect(result.success).toBe(true)
+    })
+
+    it('rejects variant mode when optionGroups is empty', () => {
+      const data = {
+        name: 'iPhone 15 Pro',
+        brand: 'Apple',
+        categoryId: 'cat-1',
+        hasVariants: true,
+        optionGroups: [],
+        variants: [],
+      }
+
+      const result = productFormSchema.safeParse(data)
+      expect(result.success).toBe(false)
+      if (!result.success) {
+        expect(
+          result.error.issues.some(
+            (i) => i.message === 'Vui lòng thêm ít nhất một nhóm phân loại'
+          )
+        ).toBe(true)
+      }
+    })
+
+    it('rejects option group with empty label or empty values', () => {
+      const data = {
+        name: 'iPhone 15 Pro',
+        brand: 'Apple',
+        categoryId: 'cat-1',
+        hasVariants: true,
+        optionGroups: [
+          {
+            id: 'g-empty',
+            label: '   ',
+            values: [],
+          },
+        ],
+        variants: [],
+      }
+
+      const result = productFormSchema.safeParse(data)
+      expect(result.success).toBe(false)
+      if (!result.success) {
+        expect(
+          result.error.issues.some(
+            (i) => i.message === 'Tên nhóm thuộc tính không được để trống'
+          )
+        ).toBe(true)
+        expect(
+          result.error.issues.some(
+            (i) => i.message === 'Nhóm thuộc tính phải có ít nhất một giá trị'
+          )
+        ).toBe(true)
+      }
+    })
+
+    it('rejects duplicate value labels within the same option group', () => {
+      const data = {
+        name: 'iPhone 15 Pro',
+        brand: 'Apple',
+        categoryId: 'cat-1',
+        hasVariants: true,
+        optionGroups: [
+          {
+            id: 'g-color',
+            label: 'Màu sắc',
+            values: [
+              { id: 'v1', label: 'Đen' },
+              { id: 'v2', label: '  đen  ' },
+            ],
+          },
+        ],
+        variants: [
+          {
+            id: 'var-1',
+            optionValueIds: { 'g-color': 'v1' },
+            priceVND: '10000000',
+            stockQuantity: 5,
+            active: true,
+          },
+        ],
+      }
+
+      const result = productFormSchema.safeParse(data)
+      expect(result.success).toBe(false)
+      if (!result.success) {
+        expect(
+          result.error.issues.some(
+            (i) => i.message === 'Giá trị phân loại không được trùng lặp'
+          )
+        ).toBe(true)
+      }
+    })
+
+    it('rejects invalid variant price or negative stock', () => {
+      const data = {
+        name: 'iPhone 15 Pro',
+        brand: 'Apple',
+        categoryId: 'cat-1',
+        hasVariants: true,
+        optionGroups: [
+          {
+            id: 'g-color',
+            label: 'Màu sắc',
+            values: [{ id: 'v1', label: 'Đen' }],
+          },
+        ],
+        variants: [
+          {
+            id: 'var-1',
+            optionValueIds: { 'g-color': 'v1' },
+            priceVND: '-1000',
+            stockQuantity: -2,
+            active: true,
+          },
+        ],
+      }
+
+      const result = productFormSchema.safeParse(data)
+      expect(result.success).toBe(false)
+      if (!result.success) {
+        expect(
+          result.error.issues.some(
+            (i) => i.message === 'Giá biến thể phải là số không âm'
+          )
+        ).toBe(true)
+        expect(
+          result.error.issues.some(
+            (i) => i.message === 'Số lượng tồn kho không được âm'
+          )
+        ).toBe(true)
+      }
+    })
+  })
+})

+import { z } from 'zod'
+
+export const optionValueSchema = z.object({
+  id: z.string(),
+  label: z.string().trim().min(1, 'Giá trị phân loại không được để trống'),
+  position: z.number().optional(),
+})
+
+export const optionGroupSchema = z.object({
+  id: z.string(),
+  label: z.string().trim().min(1, 'Tên nhóm thuộc tính không được để trống'),
+  position: z.number().optional(),
+  values: z.array(optionValueSchema).min(1, 'Nhóm thuộc tính phải có ít nhất một giá trị'),
+})
+
+export const variantItemSchema = z.object({
+  id: z.string().optional(),
+  optionValueIds: z.record(z.string(), z.string()),
+  combinationKey: z.string().optional(),
+  combinationLabel: z.string().optional(),
+  priceVND: z.string().default(''),
+  stockQuantity: z.union([z.number(), z.string()]).default(0),
+  active: z.boolean().default(true),
+})
+
+export const productFormSchema = z
+  .object({
+    name: z.string().trim().min(1, 'Tên sản phẩm không được để trống'),
+    model: z.string().default(''),
+    brand: z.string().trim().min(1, 'Thương hiệu không được để trống'),
+    categoryId: z.string().trim().min(1, 'Vui lòng chọn danh mục'),
+    price: z.string().default(''),
+    stockQuantity: z.union([z.number(), z.string()]).default(0),
+    description: z.string().default(''),
+    featured: z.boolean().default(false),
+    imageUrls: z.string().default(''),
+    hasVariants: z.boolean().default(false),
+    optionGroups: z.array(optionGroupSchema).default([]),
+    variants: z.array(variantItemSchema).default([]),
+  })
+  .superRefine((data, ctx) => {
+    if (!data.hasVariants) {
+      // Simple mode: price and stock are required
+      const priceStr = (data.price ?? '').trim()
+      if (!priceStr) {
+        ctx.addIssue({
+          code: z.ZodIssueCode.custom,
+          message: 'Giá sản phẩm không được để trống',
+          path: ['price'],
+        })
+      } else {
+        const num = Number(priceStr)
+        if (isNaN(num) || num < 0) {
+          ctx.addIssue({
+            code: z.ZodIssueCode.custom,
+            message: 'Giá phải lớn hơn hoặc bằng 0',
+            path: ['price'],
+          })
+        }
+      }
+
+      const stockVal = data.stockQuantity
+      const stockNum = typeof stockVal === 'string' ? (stockVal === '' ? 0 : Number(stockVal)) : stockVal
+      if (isNaN(stockNum) || stockNum < 0) {
+        ctx.addIssue({
+          code: z.ZodIssueCode.custom,
+          message: 'Số lượng tồn kho không được âm',
+          path: ['stockQuantity'],
+        })
+      }
+    } else {
+      // Variant mode: optionGroups and variants are required
+      if (!data.optionGroups || data.optionGroups.length === 0) {
+        ctx.addIssue({
+          code: z.ZodIssueCode.custom,
+          message: 'Vui lòng thêm ít nhất một nhóm phân loại',
+          path: ['optionGroups'],
+        })
+      } else {
+        data.optionGroups.forEach((group, gIdx) => {
+          if (!group.label || group.label.trim() === '') {
+            ctx.addIssue({
+              code: z.ZodIssueCode.custom,
+              message: 'Tên nhóm thuộc tính không được để trống',
+              path: ['optionGroups', gIdx, 'label'],
+            })
+          }
+
+          if (!group.values || group.values.length === 0) {
+            ctx.addIssue({
+              code: z.ZodIssueCode.custom,
+              message: 'Nhóm thuộc tính phải có ít nhất một giá trị',
+              path: ['optionGroups', gIdx, 'values'],
+            })
+          } else {
+            const seenValues = new Set<string>()
+            group.values.forEach((val, vIdx) => {
+              const trimmed = val.label.trim().toLowerCase()
+              if (!trimmed) {
+                ctx.addIssue({
+                  code: z.ZodIssueCode.custom,
+                  message: 'Giá trị phân loại không được để trống',
+                  path: ['optionGroups', gIdx, 'values', vIdx, 'label'],
+                })
+              } else if (seenValues.has(trimmed)) {
+                ctx.addIssue({
+                  code: z.ZodIssueCode.custom,
+                  message: 'Giá trị phân loại không được trùng lặp',
+                  path: ['optionGroups', gIdx, 'values', vIdx, 'label'],
+                })
+              } else {
+                seenValues.add(trimmed)
+              }
+            })
+          }
+        })
+      }
+
+      if (!data.variants || data.variants.length === 0) {
+        ctx.addIssue({
+          code: z.ZodIssueCode.custom,
+          message: 'Danh sách biến thể không được để trống',
+          path: ['variants'],
+        })
+      } else {
+        data.variants.forEach((v, vIdx) => {
+          const rawPrice = (v.priceVND ?? '').trim()
+          if (!rawPrice) {
+            ctx.addIssue({
+              code: z.ZodIssueCode.custom,
+              message: 'Giá biến thể không được để trống',
+              path: ['variants', vIdx, 'priceVND'],
+            })
+          } else {
+            const pNum = Number(rawPrice)
+            if (isNaN(pNum) || pNum < 0) {
+              ctx.addIssue({
+                code: z.ZodIssueCode.custom,
+                message: 'Giá biến thể phải là số không âm',
+                path: ['variants', vIdx, 'priceVND'],
+              })
+            }
+          }
+
+          const sVal = v.stockQuantity
+          const sNum = typeof sVal === 'string' ? (sVal === '' ? 0 : Number(sVal)) : sVal
+          if (isNaN(sNum) || sNum < 0) {
+            ctx.addIssue({
+              code: z.ZodIssueCode.custom,
+              message: 'Số lượng tồn kho không được âm',
+              path: ['variants', vIdx, 'stockQuantity'],
+            })
+          }
+        })
+      }
+    }
+  })
+
+export type ProductFormInput = z.input<typeof productFormSchema>
+export type ProductFormValues = z.output<typeof productFormSchema>

+import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach } from 'vitest'
+import { render, screen, fireEvent, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react'
+import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes } from 'react-router-dom'
+import { LoginPage } from './LoginPage'
+import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
+
+vi.mock('@/context/AuthContext', () => ({
+  useAuth: vi.fn(),
+}))
+
+describe('LoginPage', () => {
+  const mockLogin = vi.fn()
+
+  beforeEach(() => {
+    vi.clearAllMocks()
+    vi.mocked(useAuth).mockReturnValue({
+      user: null,
+      role: null,
+      isAuthenticated: false,
+      isLoading: false,
+      login: mockLogin,
+      logout: vi.fn(),
+    })
+  })
+
+  it('renders login form elements properly', () => {
+    render(
+      <MemoryRouter>
+        <LoginPage />
+      </MemoryRouter>
+    )
+
+    expect(screen.getByText('AuraPhone Admin')).toBeInTheDocument()
+    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/Email/i)).toBeInTheDocument()
+    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/Mật khẩu/i)).toBeInTheDocument()
+    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Đăng nhập/i })).toBeInTheDocument()
+  })
+
+  it('validates empty inputs before calling login', async () => {
+    render(
+      <MemoryRouter>
+        <LoginPage />
+      </MemoryRouter>
+    )
+
+    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/Email/i), { target: { value: '' } })
+    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/Mật khẩu/i), { target: { value: '' } })
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Đăng nhập/i }))
+
+    expect(mockLogin).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
+    expect(screen.getByText(/Vui lòng nhập đầy đủ email và mật khẩu/i)).toBeInTheDocument()
+  })
+
+  it('calls login with email and password upon submission', async () => {
+    mockLogin.mockResolvedValueOnce(undefined)
+
+    render(
+      <MemoryRouter>
+        <LoginPage />
+      </MemoryRouter>
+    )
+
+    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/Email/i), {
+      target: { value: 'admin@auraphone.vn' },
+    })
+    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/Mật khẩu/i), {
+      target: { value: 'AdminSecret123!' },
+    })
+
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Đăng nhập/i }))
+
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(mockLogin).toHaveBeenCalledWith('admin@auraphone.vn', 'AdminSecret123!')
+    })
+  })
+
+  it('displays Vietnamese error message when login fails', async () => {
+    mockLogin.mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error('Tài khoản không có quyền truy cập trang quản trị'))
+
+    render(
+      <MemoryRouter>
+        <LoginPage />
+      </MemoryRouter>
+    )
+
+    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/Email/i), {
+      target: { value: 'customer@auraphone.vn' },
+    })
+    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/Mật khẩu/i), {
+      target: { value: 'Customer123!' },
+    })
+
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Đăng nhập/i }))
+
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(screen.getByText('Tài khoản không có quyền truy cập trang quản trị')).toBeInTheDocument()
+    })
+  })
+
+  it('redirects to dashboard if already authenticated', () => {
+    vi.mocked(useAuth).mockReturnValue({
+      user: { id: 'admin-1', email: 'admin@auraphone.vn', role: 'admin' },
+      role: 'admin',
+      isAuthenticated: true,
+      isLoading: false,
+      login: mockLogin,
+      logout: vi.fn(),
+    })
+
+    render(
+      <MemoryRouter initialEntries={['/login']}>
+        <Routes>
+          <Route path="/login" element={<LoginPage />} />
+          <Route path="/" element={<div>Trang Chủ Quản Trị</div>} />
+        </Routes>
+      </MemoryRouter>
+    )
+
+    expect(screen.getByText('Trang Chủ Quản Trị')).toBeInTheDocument()
+  })
+})

+import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
+import { Navigate, useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
+import { Sparkles, Lock, Mail, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react'
+import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
+import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
+import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
+import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card'
+import { ApiError } from '@/api/types'
+
+export function LoginPage() {
+  const { isAuthenticated, login } = useAuth()
+  const navigate = useNavigate()
+  const location = useLocation()
+
+  const [email, setEmail] = useState('admin@example.com')
+  const [password, setPassword] = useState('Admin123456@')
+  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null)
+  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false)
+
+  // If already authenticated as admin, redirect to destination or home
+  if (isAuthenticated) {
+    const from = (location.state as { from?: { pathname: string } })?.from?.pathname || '/'
+    return <Navigate to={from} replace />
+  }
+
+  const handleSubmit = async (e: FormEvent) => {
+    e.preventDefault()
+    setErrorMessage(null)
+
+    const trimmedEmail = email.trim()
+    if (!trimmedEmail || !password) {
+      setErrorMessage('Vui lòng nhập đầy đủ email và mật khẩu.')
+      return
+    }
+
+    setIsSubmitting(true)
+    try {
+      await login(trimmedEmail, password)
+      const from = (location.state as { from?: { pathname: string } })?.from?.pathname || '/'
+      navigate(from, { replace: true })
+    } catch (err) {
+      if (err instanceof ApiError) {
+        setErrorMessage(err.message || 'Đăng nhập thất bại. Vui lòng kiểm tra lại thông tin.')
+      } else if (err instanceof Error) {
+        setErrorMessage(err.message)
+      } else {
+        setErrorMessage('Đã xảy ra lỗi không xác định. Vui lòng thử lại.')
+      }
+    } finally {
+      setIsSubmitting(false)
+    }
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-slate-50 p-4 sm:p-6 lg:p-8">
+      <div className="w-full max-w-md space-y-6">
+        <div className="flex flex-col items-center text-center space-y-2">
+          <div className="h-12 w-12 rounded-2xl bg-blue-600 flex items-center justify-center text-white shadow-md shadow-blue-500/25">
+            <Sparkles className="h-6 w-6" />
+          </div>
+          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900">
+            AuraPhone Admin
+          </h1>
+          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">
+            Hệ thống quản trị thương mại điện tử AuraPhone
+          </p>
+        </div>
+
+        <Card className="border-slate-200/80 shadow-lg shadow-slate-200/50">
+          <CardHeader className="space-y-1 pb-4">
+            <CardTitle className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">
+              Đăng nhập quản trị viên
+            </CardTitle>
+            <CardDescription className="text-xs text-slate-500">
+              Nhập email và mật khẩu của tài khoản quản trị để tiếp tục
+            </CardDescription>
+          </CardHeader>
+          <CardContent className="space-y-4">
+            <div className="rounded-xl border border-blue-100 bg-blue-50/70 p-3 text-xs text-blue-900 space-y-1">
+              <div className="font-semibold flex items-center justify-between">
+                <span>Tài khoản Admin mặc định:</span>
+                <button
+                  type="button"
+                  onClick={() => {
+                    setEmail('admin@example.com')
+                    setPassword('Admin123456@')
+                  }}
+                  className="text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 font-medium underline"
+                >
+                  Điền lại
+                </button>
+              </div>
+              <div className="text-slate-600 font-mono text-[11px]">
+                Email: <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">admin@example.com</span> | Pass: <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">Admin123456@</span>
+              </div>
+            </div>
+
+            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
+              {errorMessage && (
+                <div
+                  role="alert"
+                  className="flex items-start gap-2.5 rounded-xl border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 p-3.5 text-xs text-rose-800"
+                >
+                  <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-rose-600 mt-0.5" />
+                  <div className="flex-1 font-medium leading-relaxed">{errorMessage}</div>
+                </div>
+              )}
+
+              <div className="space-y-1.5">
+                <label
+                  htmlFor="email"
+                  className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 flex items-center gap-1.5"
+                >
+                  <Mail className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-slate-400" />
+                  Email quản trị
+                </label>
+                <Input
+                  id="email"
+                  type="email"
+                  placeholder="admin@example.com"
+                  value={email}
+                  onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
+                  disabled={isSubmitting}
+                  autoComplete="email"
+                  autoFocus
+                />
+              </div>
+
+              <div className="space-y-1.5">
+                <label
+                  htmlFor="password"
+                  className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 flex items-center gap-1.5"
+                >
+                  <Lock className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-slate-400" />
+                  Mật khẩu
+                </label>
+                <Input
+                  id="password"
+                  type="password"
+                  placeholder="••••••••"
+                  value={password}
+                  onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
+                  disabled={isSubmitting}
+                  autoComplete="current-password"
+                />
+              </div>
+
+              <Button
+                type="submit"
+                variant="primary"
+                className="w-full h-10 font-semibold"
+                isLoading={isSubmitting}
+                disabled={isSubmitting}
+              >
+                {isSubmitting ? 'Đang đăng nhập...' : 'Đăng nhập'}
+              </Button>
+            </form>
+          </CardContent>
+        </Card>
+
+        <p className="text-center text-xs text-slate-400">
+          Chỉ nhân sự có thẩm quyền mới được phép truy cập cổng quản trị.
+        </p>
+      </div>
+    </div>
+  )
+}

+function renderComponent(initialRoute = '/products/new') {
+  const queryClient = new QueryClient({
+    defaultOptions: {
+      queries: { retry: false },
+      mutations: { retry: false },
+    },
+  })
+
+  return render(
+    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
+      <MemoryRouter initialEntries={[initialRoute]}>
+        <Routes>
+          <Route path="/products" element={<div data-testid="products-list-page">Danh sách sản phẩm</div>} />
+          <Route path="/products/new" element={<ProductFormPage mode="create" />} />
+          <Route path="/products/:id" element={<ProductFormPage mode="edit" />} />
+          <Route path="/products/:id/edit" element={<ProductFormPage mode="edit" />} />
+        </Routes>
+      </MemoryRouter>
+    </QueryClientProvider>
+  )
+}

+describe('ProductFormPage', () => {

+

+      renderComponent('/products/new')
+
+      await waitFor(() => {
+        expect(screen.getByRole('option', { name: 'Điện thoại' })).toBeInTheDocument()
+      })
+
+      // Toggle to variant mode
+      fireEvent.click(screen.getByText(/sản phẩm có biến thể/i))
+
+      // Add group and value
+      fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /thêm nhóm phân loại/i }))
+      fireEvent.change(screen.getByPlaceholderText(/Ví dụ: Màu sắc, Dung lượng/i), { target: { value: 'Màu sắc' } })
+      const valInput = screen.getByPlaceholderText(/Nhập giá trị/i)
+      fireEvent.change(valInput, { target: { value: 'Đen' } })
+      fireEvent.keyDown(valInput, { key: 'Enter', code: 'Enter' })
+
+      fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/tên sản phẩm/i), { target: { value: 'iPhone 15' } })
+      fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/thương hiệu/i), { target: { value: 'Apple' } })
+      fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/danh mục/i), { target: { value: 'cat-phones' } })
+
+      // Apply bulk price
+      fireEvent.change(screen.getByPlaceholderText(/giá chung \(vnd\)/i), { target: { value: '25000000' } })
+      fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /áp dụng cho tất cả phân loại/i }))
+
+      const submitButton = screen.getByRole('button', { name: /^tạo sản phẩm$/i })
+      fireEvent.click(submitButton)
+
+      expect(await screen.findByText('Tên nhóm thuộc tính không hợp lệ')).toBeInTheDocument()
+      expect(await screen.findByText('Giá biến thể không hợp lệ')).toBeInTheDocument()
+    })
+  })
+})

+import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
+import { Link, useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom'
+import { useForm, type FieldErrors } from 'react-hook-form'
+import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod'
+import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query'
+import { ArrowLeft, AlertCircle, Save, Layers, Package } from 'lucide-react'
+import { getCategories } from '@/api/categories'
+import { createAdminProduct, getAdminProductById, updateAdminProduct } from '@/api/products'
+import { ApiError, type SaveProductRequest, type OptionGroupInput, type VariantInput } from '@/api/types'
+import { productFormSchema, type ProductFormValues, type ProductFormInput } from '@/lib/validations/product'
+import {
+  type OptionGroupItem,
+  type VariantMatrixRow,
+  generateVariantMatrix,
+  buildCombinationKey,
+  buildCombinationLabel,
+} from '@/lib/cartesian'
+import { OptionGroupBuilder } from '@/components/products/OptionGroupBuilder'
+import { VariantMatrixTable } from '@/components/products/VariantMatrixTable'
+import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
+import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
+import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label'
+import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea'
+import { Select } from '@/components/ui/select'
+import { Switch } from '@/components/ui/switch'
+import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle, CardDescription } from '@/components/ui/card'
+import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'
+
+export interface ProductFormPageProps {
+  mode?: 'create' | 'edit'
+}
