+function buildFlattenedErrors(
+  rhfErrors: FieldErrors<ProductFormInput>,
+  serverErrors: Record<string, string>
+): Record<string, string> {
+  const result: Record<string, string> = { ...serverErrors }
+
+  const ogErrors = rhfErrors.optionGroups as Record<string, unknown> | undefined
+  if (ogErrors) {
+    if (Array.isArray(ogErrors)) {
+      ogErrors.forEach((gErr: Record<string, unknown> | undefined, gIdx: number) => {
+        if (gErr) {
+          if (typeof (gErr.label as { message?: string } | undefined)?.message === 'string') {
+            const msg = (gErr.label as { message: string }).message
+            result[`optionGroups.${gIdx}.label`] = msg
+            result[`optionGroups[${gIdx}].label`] = msg
+          }
+          const vList = gErr.values
+          if (Array.isArray(vList)) {
+            vList.forEach((vErr: Record<string, unknown> | undefined, vIdx: number) => {
+              if (vErr && typeof (vErr.label as { message?: string } | undefined)?.message === 'string') {
+                const msg = (vErr.label as { message: string }).message
+                result[`optionGroups.${gIdx}.values.${vIdx}.label`] = msg
+                result[`optionGroups[${gIdx}].values[${vIdx}].label`] = msg
+              }
+            })
+          }
+          if (typeof (gErr.values as { message?: string } | undefined)?.message === 'string') {
+            const msg = (gErr.values as { message: string }).message
+            result[`optionGroups.${gIdx}.values`] = msg
+            result[`optionGroups[${gIdx}].values`] = msg
+          }
+        }
+      })
+    }
+    if (typeof (ogErrors as { message?: string }).message === 'string') {
+      result['optionGroups'] = (ogErrors as { message: string }).message
+    } else if (typeof (ogErrors as { root?: { message?: string } }).root?.message === 'string') {
+      result['optionGroups'] = (ogErrors as { root: { message: string } }).root.message
+    }
+  }
+
+  const varErrors = rhfErrors.variants as Record<string, unknown> | undefined
+  if (varErrors) {
+    if (Array.isArray(varErrors)) {
+      varErrors.forEach((vErr: Record<string, unknown> | undefined, vIdx: number) => {
+        if (vErr) {
+          if (typeof (vErr.priceVND as { message?: string } | undefined)?.message === 'string') {
+            const msg = (vErr.priceVND as { message: string }).message
+            result[`variants.${vIdx}.priceVND`] = msg
+            result[`variants[${vIdx}].priceVND`] = msg
+            result[`variants.${vIdx}.priceVnd`] = msg
+            result[`variants[${vIdx}].priceVnd`] = msg
+          }
+          if (typeof (vErr.stockQuantity as { message?: string } | undefined)?.message === 'string') {
+            const msg = (vErr.stockQuantity as { message: string }).message
+            result[`variants.${vIdx}.stockQuantity`] = msg
+            result[`variants[${vIdx}].stockQuantity`] = msg
+          }
+        }
+      })
+    }
+    if (typeof (varErrors as { message?: string }).message === 'string') {
+      result['variants'] = (varErrors as { message: string }).message
+    } else if (typeof (varErrors as { root?: { message?: string } }).root?.message === 'string') {
+      result['variants'] = (varErrors as { root: { message: string } }).root.message
+    }
+  }
+
+  return result
+}
+
+export function ProductFormPage({ mode }: ProductFormPageProps) {
+  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>()
+  const navigate = useNavigate()
+  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
+  const [serverErrors, setServerErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({})
+  const [hasVariants, setHasVariants] = useState(false)
+  const [optionGroups, setOptionGroups] = useState<OptionGroupItem[]>([])
+  const [variants, setVariants] = useState<VariantMatrixRow[]>([])
+
+  const isEdit = mode === 'edit' || (Boolean(id) && id !== 'new')
+
+  const {
+    data: categories = [],
+    isLoading: isCategoriesLoading,
+  } = useQuery({
+    queryKey: ['categories'],
+    queryFn: getCategories,
+  })
+
+  const {
+    data: product,
+    isLoading: isProductLoading,
+    isError: isProductError,
+    error: productError,
+  } = useQuery({
+    queryKey: ['admin', 'product', id],
+    queryFn: () => getAdminProductById(id!),
+    enabled: isEdit && Boolean(id),
+  })
+
+  const {
+    register,
+    handleSubmit,
+    reset,
+    setError,
+    setValue,
+    formState: { errors, isSubmitting },
+  } = useForm<ProductFormInput, unknown, ProductFormValues>({
+    resolver: zodResolver(productFormSchema),
+    defaultValues: {
+      name: '',
+      model: '',
+      brand: '',
+      categoryId: '',
+      price: '',
+      stockQuantity: 0,
+      description: '',
+      featured: false,
+      imageUrls: '',
+      hasVariants: false,
+      optionGroups: [],
+      variants: [],
+    },
+  })
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (isEdit && product) {
+      const hasExistingVariants = Boolean(
+        product.optionGroups &&
+        product.optionGroups.length > 0 &&
+        product.variants &&
+        product.variants.length > 0
+      )
+
+      const rawOptionGroups: OptionGroupItem[] = (product.optionGroups || []).map((g) => ({
+        id: g.id,
+        label: g.label,
+        position: g.position,
+        values: (g.values || []).map((v) => ({
+          id: v.id,
+          label: v.label,
+        })),
+      }))
+
+      const rawVariants: VariantMatrixRow[] = (product.variants || []).map((v) => {
+        const priceStr =
+          v.priceVND !== undefined
+            ? String(v.priceVND)
+            : v.priceVnd !== undefined
+              ? String(v.priceVnd)
+              : ''
+        return {
+          id: v.id,
+          optionValueIds: v.optionValueIds || {},
+          combinationKey: buildCombinationKey(v.optionValueIds || {}),
+          combinationLabel: buildCombinationLabel(v.optionValueIds || {}, rawOptionGroups),
+          priceVND: priceStr,
+          stockQuantity: typeof v.stockQuantity === 'number' ? v.stockQuantity : 0,
+          active: typeof v.active === 'boolean' ? v.active : true,
+        }
+      })
+
+      setHasVariants(hasExistingVariants)
+      setOptionGroups(rawOptionGroups)
+      setVariants(rawVariants)
+
+      reset({
+        name: product.name || '',
+        model: product.model || '',
+        brand: product.brand || '',
+        categoryId: product.category?.id || '',
+        price: product.price || '',
+        stockQuantity: product.stockQuantity ?? 0,
+        description: product.description || '',
+        featured: product.featured ?? false,
+        imageUrls: (product.imageUrls || []).join('\n'),
+        hasVariants: hasExistingVariants,
+        optionGroups: rawOptionGroups,
+        variants: rawVariants,
+      })
+    }
+  }, [isEdit, product, reset])
+
+  const handleOptionGroupsChange = (newGroups: OptionGroupItem[]) => {
+    setOptionGroups(newGroups)
+    setValue('optionGroups', newGroups, { shouldValidate: true })
+
+    const newVariants = generateVariantMatrix(newGroups, variants)
+    setVariants(newVariants)
+    setValue('variants', newVariants, { shouldValidate: true })
+
+    setServerErrors({})
+  }
+
+  const handleVariantsChange = (newVariants: VariantMatrixRow[]) => {
+    setVariants(newVariants)
+    setValue('variants', newVariants, { shouldValidate: true })
+    setServerErrors({})
+  }
+
+  const handleModeChange = (variantMode: boolean) => {
+    setHasVariants(variantMode)
+    setValue('hasVariants', variantMode, { shouldValidate: true })
+    setServerErrors({})
+  }
+
+  const onSubmit = async (values: ProductFormValues) => {
+    setServerErrors({})
+    const rawImages = typeof values.imageUrls === 'string' ? values.imageUrls : ''
+    const imageUrls = rawImages
+      .split(/[\n,]+/)
+      .map((u) => u.trim())
+      .filter(Boolean)
+
+    let basePrice = (values.price || '').trim()
+    let totalStock = Number(values.stockQuantity) || 0
+    let optionGroupsPayload: OptionGroupInput[] = []
+    let variantsPayload: VariantInput[] = []
+
+    if (hasVariants) {
+      const activeVariants = variants.filter((v) => v.active)
+      const validPrices = activeVariants
+        .map((v) => Number(v.priceVND))
+        .filter((p) => !isNaN(p) && p >= 0)
+
+      if (validPrices.length > 0) {
+        basePrice = String(Math.min(...validPrices))
+      } else {
+        const allPrices = variants
+          .map((v) => Number(v.priceVND))
+          .filter((p) => !isNaN(p) && p >= 0)
+        basePrice = allPrices.length > 0 ? String(Math.min(...allPrices)) : '0'
+      }
+
+      totalStock = activeVariants.reduce((sum, v) => sum + (Number(v.stockQuantity) || 0), 0)
+
+      optionGroupsPayload = optionGroups.map((g, gIdx) => ({
+        id: g.id,
+        label: g.label.trim(),
+        position: g.position !== undefined ? g.position : gIdx,
+        values: g.values.map((v, vIdx) => ({
+          id: v.id,
+          label: v.label.trim(),
+          position: v.position !== undefined ? v.position : vIdx,
+        })),
+      }))
+
+      variantsPayload = variants.map((v) => ({
+        id: v.id,
+        optionValueIds: v.optionValueIds,
+        priceVND: (v.priceVND ?? '').trim(),
+        stockQuantity: Number(v.stockQuantity) || 0,
+        active: Boolean(v.active),
+      }))
+    }
+
+    const payload: SaveProductRequest = {
+      name: values.name.trim(),
+      model: (values.model || '').trim(),
+      brand: values.brand.trim(),
+      categoryId: values.categoryId.trim(),
+      price: basePrice,
+      currency: 'VND',
+      stockQuantity: totalStock,
+      description: (values.description || '').trim(),
+      featured: Boolean(values.featured),
+      imageUrls,
+      hasVariants,
+      optionGroups: optionGroupsPayload,
+      variants: variantsPayload,
+    }
+
+    try {
+      if (isEdit && id) {
+        await updateAdminProduct(id, payload)
+      } else {
+        await createAdminProduct(payload)
+      }
+      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['admin', 'products'] })
+      navigate('/products')
+    } catch (err) {
+      if (err instanceof ApiError && err.isValidationError() && err.fields) {
+        const normalizedFields: Record<string, string> = {}
+        for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(err.fields)) {
+          normalizedFields[k] = v
+          const dotKey = k.replace(/\[(\d+)\]/g, '.$1')
+          normalizedFields[dotKey] = v
+          const bracketKey = k.replace(/\.(\d+)\./g, '[$1].')
+          normalizedFields[bracketKey] = v
+        }
+        setServerErrors(normalizedFields)
+
+        for (const [field, message] of Object.entries(err.fields)) {
+          if (
+            field === 'name' ||
+            field === 'model' ||
+            field === 'brand' ||
+            field === 'categoryId' ||
+            field === 'price' ||
+            field === 'stockQuantity' ||
+            field === 'description' ||
+            field === 'imageUrls'
+          ) {
+            setError(field as keyof ProductFormInput, {
+              type: 'server',
+              message,
+            })
+          }
+        }
+      } else if (err instanceof ApiError) {
+        setError('root', {
+          type: 'server',
+          message: err.message,
+        })
+      } else {
+        setError('root', {
+          type: 'server',
+          message: 'Đã có lỗi xảy ra khi lưu thông tin sản phẩm. Vui lòng thử lại.',
+        })
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  const flattenedErrors = buildFlattenedErrors(errors, serverErrors)
+
+  if (isEdit && isProductLoading) {
+    return (
+      <div className="space-y-6 max-w-4xl mx-auto">
+        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
+          <div className="h-9 w-9 rounded-xl bg-slate-200 animate-pulse" />
+          <div className="space-y-2">
+            <div className="h-6 w-48 bg-slate-200 rounded animate-pulse" />
+            <div className="h-4 w-72 bg-slate-100 rounded animate-pulse" />
+          </div>
+        </div>
+        <Card className="animate-pulse">
+          <CardContent className="p-6 space-y-4">
+            <div className="h-5 w-40 bg-slate-200 rounded" />
+            <div className="h-10 bg-slate-100 rounded-xl" />
+            <div className="h-10 bg-slate-100 rounded-xl" />
+            <div className="h-24 bg-slate-100 rounded-xl" />
+          </CardContent>
+        </Card>
+      </div>
+    )
+  }
+
+  if (isEdit && isProductError) {
+    return (
+      <div className="space-y-6 max-w-4xl mx-auto">
+        <Card className="border-rose-200 bg-rose-50/50">
+          <CardContent className="p-8 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center">
+            <div className="h-12 w-12 rounded-full bg-rose-100 flex items-center justify-center text-rose-600 mb-3">
+              <AlertCircle className="h-6 w-6" />
+            </div>
+            <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-rose-900">
+              Không tìm thấy sản phẩm hoặc đã xảy ra lỗi
+            </h3>
+            <p className="text-sm text-rose-600 mt-1 max-w-md">
+              {productError instanceof Error
+                ? productError.message
+                : 'Sản phẩm không tồn tại hoặc đã bị xóa khỏi hệ thống.'}
+            </p>
+            <Link to="/products" className="mt-4">
+              <Button variant="outline" className="border-rose-300 text-rose-700 hover:bg-rose-100">
+                <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
+                Quay lại danh sách sản phẩm
+              </Button>
+            </Link>
+          </CardContent>
+        </Card>
+      </div>
+    )
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <div className="space-y-6 max-w-4xl mx-auto pb-12">
+      {/* Header & Breadcrumb */}
+      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
+        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
+          <Link
+            to="/products"
+            className="p-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50 hover:text-slate-900 transition-colors shadow-sm"
+            title="Quay lại danh sách"
+          >
+            <ArrowLeft className="h-5 w-5" />
+          </Link>
+          <div>
+            <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">
+              {isEdit ? 'Chỉnh sửa sản phẩm' : 'Thêm sản phẩm mới'}
+            </h1>
+            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
+              {isEdit
+                ? 'Cập nhật thông tin chi tiết của sản phẩm.'
+                : 'Nhập thông tin sản phẩm để thêm vào danh mục bán lẻ.'}
+            </p>
+          </div>
+        </div>
+      </div>
+
+      {/* Root Server Error Alert */}
+      {errors.root && (
+        <div className="p-4 rounded-xl border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 text-rose-900 flex items-start gap-3">
+          <AlertCircle className="h-5 w-5 text-rose-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
+          <div>
+            <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold text-rose-800">Không thể lưu thông tin</h4>
+            <p className="text-sm text-rose-700 mt-0.5">{errors.root.message}</p>
+          </div>
+        </div>
+      )}
+
+      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} noValidate className="space-y-6">
+        {/* Basic Information Card */}
+        <Card>
+          <CardHeader>
+            <CardTitle>Thông tin cơ bản</CardTitle>
+            <CardDescription>
+              Tên, thương hiệu, danh mục và mã định danh của sản phẩm.
+            </CardDescription>
+          </CardHeader>
+          <CardContent className="space-y-4">
+            {/* Product Name */}
+            <div>
+              <Label htmlFor="product-name" required>
+                Tên sản phẩm
+              </Label>
+              <div className="mt-1.5">
+                <Input
+                  id="product-name"
+                  type="text"
+                  placeholder="Ví dụ: iPhone 15 Pro Max 256GB"
+                  error={Boolean(errors.name)}
+                  {...register('name')}
+                />
+              </div>
+              {errors.name && (
+                <p className="text-xs text-rose-600 font-medium mt-1.5" role="alert">
+                  {errors.name.message}
+                </p>
+              )}
+            </div>
+
+            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
+              {/* Model */}
+              <div>
+                <Label htmlFor="product-model">Model / Mã kiểu máy</Label>
+                <div className="mt-1.5">
+                  <Input
+                    id="product-model"
+                    type="text"
+                    placeholder="Ví dụ: A3106, SM-S928B..."
+                    error={Boolean(errors.model)}
+                    {...register('model')}
+                  />
+                </div>
+                {errors.model && (
+                  <p className="text-xs text-rose-600 font-medium mt-1.5" role="alert">
+                    {errors.model.message}
+                  </p>
+                )}
+              </div>
+
+              {/* Free-text Brand */}
+              <div>
+                <Label htmlFor="product-brand" required>
+                  Thương hiệu
+                </Label>
+                <div className="mt-1.5">
+                  <Input
+                    id="product-brand"
+                    type="text"
+                    placeholder="Ví dụ: Apple, Samsung, Xiaomi..."
+                    error={Boolean(errors.brand)}
+                    {...register('brand')}
+                  />
+                </div>
+                {errors.brand && (
+                  <p className="text-xs text-rose-600 font-medium mt-1.5" role="alert">
+                    {errors.brand.message}
+                  </p>
+                )}
+              </div>
+            </div>
+
+            {/* Category Select */}
+            <div>
+              <Label htmlFor="product-category" required>
+                Danh mục
+              </Label>
+              <div className="mt-1.5">
+                <Select
+                  id="product-category"
+                  error={Boolean(errors.categoryId)}
+                  disabled={isCategoriesLoading}
+                  {...register('categoryId')}
+                >
+                  <option value="">-- Chọn danh mục sản phẩm --</option>
+                  {categories.map((cat) => (
+                    <option key={cat.id} value={cat.id}>
+                      {cat.name}
+                    </option>
+                  ))}
+                </Select>
+              </div>
+              {errors.categoryId && (
+                <p className="text-xs text-rose-600 font-medium mt-1.5" role="alert">
+                  {errors.categoryId.message}
+                </p>
+              )}
+            </div>
+          </CardContent>
+        </Card>
+
+        {/* Pricing & Sales Configuration Card */}
+        <Card>
+          <CardHeader>
+            <CardTitle>Cấu hình bán hàng</CardTitle>
+            <CardDescription>
+              Chọn mô hình sản phẩm: sản phẩm đơn giản (giá và kho chung) hoặc sản phẩm có phân loại biến thể.
+            </CardDescription>
+          </CardHeader>
+          <CardContent className="space-y-6">
+            {/* Mode Switcher Buttons */}
+            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
+              <button
+                type="button"
+                role="radio"
+                aria-checked={!hasVariants}
+                onClick={() => handleModeChange(false)}
+                className={cn(
+                  'p-4 rounded-xl border text-left transition-all cursor-pointer flex items-start gap-3.5',
+                  !hasVariants
+                    ? 'border-blue-600 bg-blue-50/40 ring-2 ring-blue-600/20 shadow-xs'
+                    : 'border-slate-200 bg-white hover:border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50/60'
+                )}
+              >
+                <div
+                  className={cn(
+                    'w-4 h-4 rounded-full border flex items-center justify-center mt-0.5 shrink-0 transition-colors',
+                    !hasVariants ? 'border-blue-600 bg-blue-600' : 'border-slate-300 bg-white'
+                  )}
+                >
+                  {!hasVariants && <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-white" />}
+                </div>
+                <div>
+                  <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-1.5">
+                    <Package className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-600" />
+                    Sản phẩm đơn giản
+                  </div>
+                  <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">
+                    Một mức giá và số lượng tồn kho duy nhất cho toàn bộ sản phẩm.
+                  </div>
+                </div>
+              </button>
+
+              <button
+                type="button"
+                role="radio"
+                aria-checked={hasVariants}
+                onClick={() => handleModeChange(true)}
+                className={cn(
+                  'p-4 rounded-xl border text-left transition-all cursor-pointer flex items-start gap-3.5',
+                  hasVariants
+                    ? 'border-blue-600 bg-blue-50/40 ring-2 ring-blue-600/20 shadow-xs'
+                    : 'border-slate-200 bg-white hover:border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50/60'
+                )}
+              >
+                <div
+                  className={cn(
+                    'w-4 h-4 rounded-full border flex items-center justify-center mt-0.5 shrink-0 transition-colors',
+                    hasVariants ? 'border-blue-600 bg-blue-600' : 'border-slate-300 bg-white'
+                  )}
+                >
+                  {hasVariants && <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-white" />}
+                </div>
+                <div>
+                  <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-1.5">
+                    <Layers className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-600" />
+                    Sản phẩm có biến thể
+                  </div>
+                  <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">
+                    Nhiều phân loại hàng (màu sắc, kích cỡ, dung lượng...) với giá và kho riêng.
+                  </div>
+                </div>
+              </button>
+            </div>
+
+            {/* Simple Mode: Direct Price and Stock */}
+            {!hasVariants && (
+              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 pt-2 border-t border-slate-100">
+                {/* Price */}
+                <div>
+                  <Label htmlFor="product-price" required>
+                    Giá bán (VND)
+                  </Label>
+                  <div className="mt-1.5">
+                    <Input
+                      id="product-price"
+                      type="number"
+                      min="0"
+                      step="1000"
+                      placeholder="29990000"
+                      error={Boolean(errors.price)}
+                      {...register('price')}
+                    />
+                  </div>
+                  {errors.price && (
+                    <p className="text-xs text-rose-600 font-medium mt-1.5" role="alert">
+                      {errors.price.message}
+                    </p>
+                  )}
+                </div>
+
+                {/* Stock Quantity */}
+                <div>
+                  <Label htmlFor="product-stock">Số lượng tồn kho</Label>
+                  <div className="mt-1.5">
+                    <Input
+                      id="product-stock"
+                      type="number"
+                      min="0"
+                      placeholder="0"
+                      error={Boolean(errors.stockQuantity)}
+                      {...register('stockQuantity')}
+                    />
+                  </div>
+                  {errors.stockQuantity && (
+                    <p className="text-xs text-rose-600 font-medium mt-1.5" role="alert">
+                      {errors.stockQuantity.message}
+                    </p>
+                  )}
+                </div>
+              </div>
+            )}
+
+            {/* Variant Mode: Option Groups Builder and Variant Matrix Table */}
+            {hasVariants && (
+              <div className="space-y-6 pt-2 border-t border-slate-100">
+                {/* Option Groups Builder */}
+                <div className="space-y-2">
+                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
+                    <Label className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">
+                      Nhóm phân loại hàng <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
+                    </Label>
+                  </div>
+                  <OptionGroupBuilder
+                    groups={optionGroups}
+                    onChange={handleOptionGroupsChange}
+                    errors={flattenedErrors}
+                    disabled={isSubmitting}
+                  />
+                  {errors.optionGroups?.message && (
+                    <p className="text-xs text-rose-600 font-medium mt-1" role="alert">
+                      {errors.optionGroups.message}
+                    </p>
+                  )}
+                </div>
+
+                {/* Variant Matrix Table */}
+                <div className="space-y-2 pt-2">
+                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
+                    <Label className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">
+                      Danh sách biến thể & Bảng giá <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
+                    </Label>
+                  </div>
+                  <VariantMatrixTable
+                    variants={variants}
+                    onChange={handleVariantsChange}
+                    errors={flattenedErrors}
+                    disabled={isSubmitting}
+                  />
+                  {errors.variants?.message && (
+                    <p className="text-xs text-rose-600 font-medium mt-1" role="alert">
+                      {errors.variants.message}
+                    </p>
+                  )}
+                </div>
+              </div>
+            )}
+          </CardContent>
+        </Card>
+
+        {/* Description & Media Card */}
+        <Card>
+          <CardHeader>
+            <CardTitle>Chi tiết & Hình ảnh</CardTitle>
+            <CardDescription>
+              Mô tả sản phẩm, danh sách đường dẫn hình ảnh và tùy chọn hiển thị nổi bật.
+            </CardDescription>
+          </CardHeader>
+          <CardContent className="space-y-4">
+            {/* Description */}
+            <div>
+              <Label htmlFor="product-description">Mô tả sản phẩm</Label>
+              <div className="mt-1.5">
+                <Textarea
+                  id="product-description"
+                  rows={4}
+                  placeholder="Nhập thông số kỹ thuật hoặc mô tả chi tiết sản phẩm..."
+                  error={Boolean(errors.description)}
+                  {...register('description')}
+                />
+              </div>
+              {errors.description && (
+                <p className="text-xs text-rose-600 font-medium mt-1.5" role="alert">
+                  {errors.description.message}
+                </p>
+              )}
+            </div>
+
+            {/* Image URLs */}
+            <div>
+              <Label htmlFor="product-images">Danh sách URL hình ảnh</Label>
+              <div className="mt-1.5">
+                <Textarea
+                  id="product-images"
+                  rows={3}
+                  placeholder="https://example.com/image1.jpg&#10;https://example.com/image2.jpg"
+                  error={Boolean(errors.imageUrls)}
+                  {...register('imageUrls')}
+                />
+              </div>
+              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">
+                Nhập mỗi đường dẫn hình ảnh trên một dòng hoặc phân cách bởi dấu phẩy. URL đầu tiên sẽ được dùng làm ảnh đại diện chính.
+              </p>
+              {errors.imageUrls && (
+                <p className="text-xs text-rose-600 font-medium mt-1.5" role="alert">
+                  {errors.imageUrls.message}
+                </p>
+              )}
+            </div>
+
+            {/* Featured Switch */}
+            <div className="pt-2 border-t border-slate-100">
+              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
+                <Switch
+                  id="product-featured"
+                  label="Đặt làm sản phẩm nổi bật trên trang chủ"
+                  {...register('featured')}
+                />
+              </div>
+            </div>
+          </CardContent>
+        </Card>
+
+        {/* Action Buttons */}
+        <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-3 pt-2">
+          <Link to="/products">
+            <Button type="button" variant="outline" disabled={isSubmitting}>
+              Hủy
+            </Button>
+          </Link>
+          <Button type="submit" variant="primary" isLoading={isSubmitting} className="min-w-[140px]">
+            <Save className="h-4 w-4 mr-1.5" />
+            {isEdit ? 'Cập nhật sản phẩm' : 'Tạo sản phẩm'}
+          </Button>
+        </div>
+      </form>
+    </div>
+  )
+}

+import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach } from 'vitest'
+import { render, screen, fireEvent, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react'
+import { MemoryRouter } from 'react-router-dom'
+import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query'
+import { ProductsPage } from './ProductsPage'
+import * as productsApi from '@/api/products'
+import type { ProductSummary } from '@/api/types'
+
+vi.mock('@/api/products', () => ({
+  getAdminProducts: vi.fn(),
+  getAdminProductById: vi.fn(),
+  deleteAdminProduct: vi.fn(),
+}))

+const mockProducts: ProductSummary[] = [
+  {
+    id: 'prod-1',
+    name: 'iPhone 15 Pro Max',
+    model: 'A3106',
+    brand: 'Apple',
+    category: { id: 'cat-phones', name: 'Điện thoại' },

+    maxPriceVnd: '34990000',

+    maxPriceVnd: '24990000',

+    currency: 'VND',
+    primaryImageUrl: '',
+    inStock: false,

+function renderWithClient(ui: React.ReactElement, initialEntries = ['/products']) {
+  const testQueryClient = new QueryClient({
+    defaultOptions: {
+      queries: {
+        retry: false,
+      },
+    },
+  })
+  return render(
+    <QueryClientProvider client={testQueryClient}>
+      <MemoryRouter initialEntries={initialEntries}>
+        {ui}
+      </MemoryRouter>
+    </QueryClientProvider>
+  )
+}

+        pagination: {
+          page: 1,
+          limit: 10,
+          total: 1,
+          totalPages: 1,

+    // Dialog should be closed
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(screen.queryByRole('alertdialog')).not.toBeInTheDocument()
+    })
+
+    // Success message in Vietnamese
+    expect(
+      screen.getByText(/Đã xóa sản phẩm thành công/i)
+    ).toBeInTheDocument()
+  })
+
+  it('displays Vietnamese error message when delete API call fails', async () => {
+    vi.mocked(productsApi.getAdminProducts).mockResolvedValueOnce({
+      data: mockProducts,
+      meta: {
+        timestamp: '2026-08-16T08:00:00Z',
+        pagination: {
+          page: 1,
+          limit: 10,
+          total: 2,
+          totalPages: 1,
+          hasNext: false,
+          hasPrev: false,
+        },
+      },
+      message: '',
+    })
+
+    vi.mocked(productsApi.deleteAdminProduct).mockRejectedValueOnce(
+      new Error('Không thể xóa sản phẩm do lỗi hệ thống')
+    )
+
+    renderWithClient(<ProductsPage />)
+
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(screen.getByText('iPhone 15 Pro Max')).toBeInTheDocument()
+    })
+
+    const deleteButtons = screen.getAllByRole('button', { name: /Xóa sản phẩm/i })
+    fireEvent.click(deleteButtons[0])
+
+    const confirmBtn = screen.getByRole('button', { name: /^Xóa$|^Xác nhận/i })
+    fireEvent.click(confirmBtn)
+
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(productsApi.deleteAdminProduct).toHaveBeenCalledWith('prod-1')
+    })
+
+    // Shows error message
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(
+        screen.getByText(/Không thể xóa sản phẩm/i)
+      ).toBeInTheDocument()
+    })
+  })
+})

+import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
+import { Link, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom'
+import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query'
+import {
+  Plus,
+  Search,
+  RefreshCw,
+  Package,
+  AlertCircle,
+  CheckCircle2,
+  ChevronLeft,
+  ChevronRight,
+  ImageIcon,
+  Pencil,
+  Trash2,
+  X,
+} from 'lucide-react'
+import { getAdminProducts, deleteAdminProduct } from '@/api/products'
+import { ApiError, type ProductSummary } from '@/api/types'
+import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
+import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
+import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
+import { Card, CardContent } from '@/components/ui/card'
+import {
+  Table,
+  TableBody,
+  TableCell,
+  TableHead,
+  TableHeader,
+  TableRow,
+} from '@/components/ui/table'
+import {
+  AlertDialog,
+  AlertDialogAction,
+  AlertDialogCancel,
+  AlertDialogContent,
+  AlertDialogDescription,
+  AlertDialogFooter,
+  AlertDialogHeader,
+  AlertDialogTitle,
+} from '@/components/ui/alert-dialog'
+import { formatVND } from '@/lib/utils'
+
+export function ProductsPage() {
+  const [searchParams, setSearchParams] = useSearchParams()
+  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
+
+  const queryParam = searchParams.get('q') || ''
+  const pageParam = parseInt(searchParams.get('page') || '1', 10)
+  const currentPage = isNaN(pageParam) || pageParam < 1 ? 1 : pageParam
+  const limit = 10
+
+  const [searchInput, setSearchInput] = useState(queryParam)
+  const [productToDelete, setProductToDelete] = useState<ProductSummary | null>(null)
+  const [isDeleting, setIsDeleting] = useState(false)
+  const [deleteError, setDeleteError] = useState<string | null>(null)
+  const [successMessage, setSuccessMessage] = useState<string | null>(null)
+
+  const { data, isLoading, isError, error, refetch, isFetching } = useQuery({
+    queryKey: ['admin', 'products', { q: queryParam, page: currentPage, limit }],
+    queryFn: () => getAdminProducts({ q: queryParam, page: currentPage, limit }),
+  })
+
+  const products = data?.data || []
+  const pagination = data?.meta.pagination || {
+    page: currentPage,
+    limit,
+    total: 0,
+    totalPages: 1,
+    hasNext: false,
+    hasPrev: false,
+  }
+
+  const handleConfirmDelete = async () => {
+    if (!productToDelete) return
+
+    try {
+      setIsDeleting(true)
+      setDeleteError(null)
+      await deleteAdminProduct(productToDelete.id)
+      setProductToDelete(null)
+      setSuccessMessage('Đã xóa sản phẩm thành công')
+      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['admin', 'products'] })
+    } catch (err) {
+      if (err instanceof ApiError) {
+        setDeleteError(err.message)
+      } else if (err instanceof Error) {
+        setDeleteError(err.message)
+      } else {
+        setDeleteError('Đã có lỗi xảy ra khi xóa sản phẩm. Vui lòng thử lại.')
+      }
+    } finally {
+      setIsDeleting(false)
+    }
+  }
+
+  const handleSearchSubmit = (e: FormEvent) => {
+    e.preventDefault()
+    const nextParams = new URLSearchParams(searchParams)
+    const trimmed = searchInput.trim()
+    if (trimmed) {
+      nextParams.set('q', trimmed)
+    } else {
+      nextParams.delete('q')
+    }
+    nextParams.set('page', '1')
+    setSearchParams(nextParams)
+  }
+
+  const handleClearSearch = () => {
+    setSearchInput('')
+    const nextParams = new URLSearchParams(searchParams)
+    nextParams.delete('q')
+    nextParams.set('page', '1')
+    setSearchParams(nextParams)
+  }
+
+  const handlePageChange = (newPage: number) => {
+    const nextParams = new URLSearchParams(searchParams)
+    nextParams.set('page', String(newPage))
+    setSearchParams(nextParams)
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <div className="space-y-6">
+      {/* Top Header */}
+      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
+        <div>
+          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">Quản lý sản phẩm</h1>
+          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">
+            Danh sách tất cả sản phẩm điện thoại và thiết bị công nghệ trong danh mục.
+          </p>
+        </div>
+        <Link
+          to="/products/new"
+          className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-xl text-sm font-medium transition-all duration-200 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-blue-600 focus-visible:ring-offset-2 disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 active:scale-[0.98] cursor-pointer select-none bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-sm shadow-blue-600/20 hover:bg-blue-700 hover:shadow-md hover:shadow-blue-600/30 active:bg-blue-800 h-10 px-4 py-2"
+        >
+          <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
+          Thêm sản phẩm
+        </Link>
+      </div>
+
+      {/* Success Notification Banner */}
+      {successMessage && (
+        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50/80 p-4 text-sm text-emerald-800 flex items-center justify-between shadow-xs animate-in fade-in">
+          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
+            <CheckCircle2 className="h-5 w-5 text-emerald-600 shrink-0" />
+            <span className="font-medium">{successMessage}</span>
+          </div>
+          <button
+            type="button"
+            onClick={() => setSuccessMessage(null)}
+            className="text-emerald-600 hover:text-emerald-800 p-1 rounded-lg transition-colors cursor-pointer"
+            aria-label="Đóng thông báo"
+          >
+            <X className="h-4 w-4" />
+          </button>
+        </div>
+      )}
+
+      {/* Filter and Search Bar */}
+      <Card>
+        <CardContent className="p-4">
+          <form onSubmit={handleSearchSubmit} className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3">
+            <div className="relative flex-1">
+              <Search className="absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-4 w-4 text-slate-400 pointer-events-none" />
+              <Input
+                type="text"
+                placeholder="Tìm kiếm sản phẩm theo tên..."
+                value={searchInput}
+                onChange={(e) => setSearchInput(e.target.value)}
+                className="pl-10 pr-10"
+              />
+              {searchInput && (
+                <button
+                  type="button"
+                  onClick={handleClearSearch}
+                  aria-label="Xóa từ khóa tìm kiếm"
+                  className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600"
+                >
+                  <X className="h-4 w-4" />
+                </button>
+              )}
+            </div>
+            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
+              <Button type="submit" variant="primary" className="shrink-0">
+                <Search className="h-4 w-4 mr-1.5" />
+                Tìm kiếm
+              </Button>
+              <Button
+                type="button"
+                variant="outline"
+                onClick={() => refetch()}
+                disabled={isFetching}
+                title="Làm mới dữ liệu"
+                className="shrink-0"
+              >
+                <RefreshCw className={`h-4 w-4 ${isFetching ? 'animate-spin text-blue-600' : ''}`} />
+              </Button>
+            </div>
+          </form>
+        </CardContent>
+      </Card>
+
+      {/* Main Content Area */}
+      {isError ? (
+        <Card className="border-rose-200 bg-rose-50/50">
+          <CardContent className="p-8 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center">
+            <div className="h-12 w-12 rounded-full bg-rose-100 flex items-center justify-center text-rose-600 mb-3">
+              <AlertCircle className="h-6 w-6" />
+            </div>
+            <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-rose-900">
+              Đã có lỗi xảy ra khi tải danh sách sản phẩm
+            </h3>
+            <p className="text-sm text-rose-600 mt-1 max-w-md">
+              {error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Không thể kết nối đến máy chủ. Vui lòng kiểm tra lại.'}
+            </p>
+            <Button
+              variant="outline"
+              onClick={() => refetch()}
+              className="mt-4 border-rose-300 text-rose-700 hover:bg-rose-100"
+            >
+              <RefreshCw className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
+              Thử lại
+            </Button>
+          </CardContent>
+        </Card>
+      ) : (
+        <Card>
+          <div className="rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border-0">
+            <Table>
+              <TableHeader>
+                <TableRow>
+                  <TableHead className="w-16 text-center">Hình ảnh</TableHead>
+                  <TableHead>Tên sản phẩm</TableHead>
+                  <TableHead className="w-36">Thương hiệu</TableHead>
+                  <TableHead className="w-36">Danh mục</TableHead>
+                  <TableHead className="w-36">Giá</TableHead>
+                  <TableHead className="w-32">Trạng thái</TableHead>
+                  <TableHead className="w-28 text-right">Thao tác</TableHead>
+                </TableRow>
+              </TableHeader>
+              <TableBody>
+                {isLoading ? (
+                  Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, idx) => (
+                    <TableRow key={`skeleton-${idx}`} className="animate-pulse">
+                      <TableCell className="text-center">
+                        <div className="h-12 w-12 rounded-xl bg-slate-200 mx-auto" />
+                      </TableCell>
+                      <TableCell>
+                        <div className="h-4 w-48 bg-slate-200 rounded mb-1.5" />
+                        <div className="h-3 w-24 bg-slate-100 rounded" />
+                      </TableCell>
+                      <TableCell>
+                        <div className="h-4 w-20 bg-slate-200 rounded" />
+                      </TableCell>
+                      <TableCell>
+                        <div className="h-6 w-24 bg-slate-200 rounded-full" />
+                      </TableCell>
+                      <TableCell>
+                        <div className="h-4 w-24 bg-slate-200 rounded" />
+                      </TableCell>
+                      <TableCell>
+                        <div className="h-6 w-20 bg-slate-200 rounded-full" />
+                      </TableCell>
+                      <TableCell className="text-right">
+                        <div className="h-8 w-16 bg-slate-200 rounded ml-auto" />
+                      </TableCell>
+                    </TableRow>
+                  ))
+                ) : products.length === 0 ? (
+                  <TableRow>
+                    <TableCell colSpan={7} className="h-64 text-center">
+                      <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-slate-500 py-6">
+                        <div className="h-12 w-12 rounded-2xl bg-slate-100 flex items-center justify-center text-slate-400 mb-3">
+                          <Package className="h-6 w-6" />
+                        </div>
+                        <p className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-800">
+                          Không tìm thấy sản phẩm nào
+                        </p>
+                        <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1 max-w-sm">
+                          {queryParam
+                            ? `Không có kết quả nào phù hợp với từ khóa "${queryParam}". Thử tìm kiếm với từ khóa khác.`
+                            : 'Chưa có sản phẩm nào trong danh mục. Hãy bắt đầu bằng cách thêm sản phẩm mới.'}
+                        </p>
+                        {queryParam && (
+                          <Button
+                            variant="secondary"
+                            size="sm"
+                            onClick={handleClearSearch}
+                            className="mt-3"
+                          >
+                            Xóa bộ lọc
+                          </Button>
+                        )}
+                      </div>
+                    </TableCell>
+                  </TableRow>
+                ) : (
+                  products.map((product) => (
+                    <TableRow key={product.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/70">
+                      {/* Product Image */}
+                      <TableCell className="text-center">
+                        <div className="h-12 w-12 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 overflow-hidden flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 mx-auto">
+                          {product.primaryImageUrl ? (
+                            <img
+                              src={product.primaryImageUrl}
+                              alt={product.name}
+                              className="h-full w-full object-cover"
+                              onError={(e) => {
+                                (e.target as HTMLElement).style.display = 'none'
+                              }}
+                            />
+                          ) : (
+                            <ImageIcon className="h-5 w-5 text-slate-400" />
+                          )}
+                        </div>
+                      </TableCell>
+
+                      {/* Product Name & Model */}
+                      <TableCell>
+                        <div className="font-medium text-slate-900 line-clamp-1">
+                          {product.name}
+                        </div>
+                        {product.model && (
+                          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-mono mt-0.5">
+                            {product.model}
+                          </div>
+                        )}
+                      </TableCell>
+
+                      {/* Plain text Brand column */}
+                      <TableCell className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
+                        {product.brand || '—'}
+                      </TableCell>
+
+                      {/* Category */}
+                      <TableCell>
+                        <Badge variant="secondary" className="font-normal text-slate-700 bg-slate-100">
+                          {product.category?.name || 'Chưa phân loại'}
+                        </Badge>
+                      </TableCell>
+
+                      {/* Formatted Price */}
+                      <TableCell className="font-semibold text-slate-900 whitespace-nowrap">
+                        {formatVND(product.price)}
+                      </TableCell>
+
+                      {/* Stock Status */}
+                      <TableCell>
+                        {product.inStock ? (
+                          <Badge variant="success">Còn hàng</Badge>
+                        ) : (
+                          <Badge variant="destructive">Hết hàng</Badge>
+                        )}
+                      </TableCell>
+
+                      {/* Actions */}
+                      <TableCell className="text-right">
+                        <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1">
+                          <Link
+                            to={`/products/${product.id}`}
+                            className="p-1.5 text-slate-500 hover:text-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-50 rounded-lg transition-colors"
+                            title="Chỉnh sửa sản phẩm"
+                          >
+                            <Pencil className="h-4 w-4" />
+                          </Link>
+                          <button
+                            type="button"
+                            onClick={() => {
+                              setProductToDelete(product)
+                              setDeleteError(null)
+                            }}
+                            aria-label={`Xóa sản phẩm ${product.name}`}
+                            className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-50 rounded-lg transition-colors cursor-pointer"
+                            title="Xóa sản phẩm"
+                          >
+                            <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
+                          </button>
+                        </div>
+                      </TableCell>
+                    </TableRow>
+                  ))
+                )}
+              </TableBody>
+            </Table>
+          </div>
+
+          {/* Pagination Controls */}
+          {pagination.total > 0 && (
+            <div className="px-6 py-4 border-t border-slate-100 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4">
+              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
+                Hiển thị{' '}
+                <span className="font-medium text-slate-700">
+                  {Math.min((currentPage - 1) * limit + 1, pagination.total)}
+                </span>{' '}
+                -{' '}
+                <span className="font-medium text-slate-700">
+                  {Math.min(currentPage * limit, pagination.total)}
+                </span>{' '}
+                trên tổng số{' '}
+                <span className="font-medium text-slate-700">{pagination.total}</span> sản phẩm
+              </div>
+
+              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
+                <Button
+                  variant="outline"
+                  size="sm"
+                  onClick={() => handlePageChange(currentPage - 1)}
+                  disabled={!pagination.hasPrev || currentPage <= 1}
+                  className="gap-1 text-xs"
+                >
+                  <ChevronLeft className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
+                  Trước
+                </Button>
+
+                <div className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 px-2">
+                  Trang {pagination.page} / {Math.max(pagination.totalPages, 1)}
+                </div>
+
+                <Button
+                  variant="outline"
+                  size="sm"
+                  onClick={() => handlePageChange(currentPage + 1)}
+                  disabled={!pagination.hasNext || currentPage >= pagination.totalPages}
+                  className="gap-1 text-xs"
+                >
+                  Sau
+                  <ChevronRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
+                </Button>
+              </div>
+            </div>
+          )}
+        </Card>
+      )}
+
+      {/* Confirmation Dialog for Product Deletion */}
+      <AlertDialog
+        open={Boolean(productToDelete)}
+        onOpenChange={(open) => {
+          if (!open) {
+            setProductToDelete(null)
+            setDeleteError(null)
+          }
+        }}
+      >
+        <AlertDialogContent>
+          <AlertDialogHeader>
+            <AlertDialogTitle>Xác nhận xóa sản phẩm</AlertDialogTitle>
+            <AlertDialogDescription>
+              Bạn có chắc chắn muốn xóa sản phẩm{' '}
+              <span className="font-semibold text-slate-900">
+                "{productToDelete?.name}"
+              </span>{' '}
+              không? Hành động này sẽ ẩn sản phẩm khỏi hệ thống danh mục.
+            </AlertDialogDescription>
+          </AlertDialogHeader>
+
+          {deleteError && (
+            <div className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 p-3 text-xs text-rose-700 flex items-center gap-2">
+              <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4 text-rose-600 shrink-0" />
+              <span>{deleteError}</span>
+            </div>
+          )}
+
+          <AlertDialogFooter>
+            <AlertDialogCancel disabled={isDeleting}>Hủy</AlertDialogCancel>
+            <AlertDialogAction
+              variant="destructive"
+              onClick={handleConfirmDelete}
+              isLoading={isDeleting}
+            >
+              Xóa
+            </AlertDialogAction>
+          </AlertDialogFooter>
+        </AlertDialogContent>
+      </AlertDialog>
+    </div>
+  )
+}

+import { render, screen, fireEvent, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react'
+import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach } from 'vitest'
+import App from '../App'
+import { authApi } from '@/api/auth'
+import { tokenStore } from '@/api/tokenStore'
+
+vi.mock('@/api/auth', () => ({
+  authApi: {
+    login: vi.fn(),
+    refresh: vi.fn(),
+    logout: vi.fn(),
+    getMe: vi.fn(),
+  },
+}))
+
+describe('App Shell', () => {
+  const adminUser = { id: 'admin-1', email: 'admin@auraphone.vn', role: 'admin' }
+
+  beforeEach(() => {
+    vi.clearAllMocks()
+    tokenStore.clearToken()
+    window.history.pushState({}, '', '/')
+    vi.mocked(authApi.refresh).mockResolvedValue({
+      user: adminUser,
+      accessToken: 'token-admin-123',
+    })
+  })
+
+  it('renders the admin dashboard shell with header and brand title', async () => {
+    render(<App />)
+
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(screen.getByText('AuraPhone Admin')).toBeInTheDocument()
+      expect(screen.getByText('Bảng điều khiển quản trị hệ thống AuraPhone')).toBeInTheDocument()
+    })
+  })
+
+  it('renders sidebar navigation links', async () => {
+    render(<App />)
+
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(screen.getByRole('link', { name: /Tổng quan/i })).toBeInTheDocument()
+      expect(screen.getByRole('link', { name: /Sản phẩm/i })).toBeInTheDocument()
+      expect(screen.getByText(/Người dùng/i)).toBeInTheDocument()
+    })
+  })
+
+  it('renders shadcn/ui components (Cards, Badges, Buttons)', async () => {
+    render(<App />)
+
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(screen.getByText('Thống kê nhanh')).toBeInTheDocument()
+      expect(screen.getByText('Hệ thống')).toBeInTheDocument()
+      expect(screen.getByText('Hoạt động')).toBeInTheDocument()
+    })
+
+    const actionButton = screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Làm mới dữ liệu/i })
+    expect(actionButton).toBeInTheDocument()
+  })
+
+  it('handles user interaction on the action button', async () => {
+    render(<App />)
+
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Làm mới dữ liệu/i })).toBeInTheDocument()
+    })
+
+    const actionButton = screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Làm mới dữ liệu/i })
+    fireEvent.click(actionButton)
+    expect(screen.getByText(/Đã làm mới/i)).toBeInTheDocument()
+  })
+
+  it('renders product form page when navigating to /products/new', async () => {

diff --git a/frontend-admin/src/test/AuthFlow.test.tsx b/frontend-admin/src/test/AuthFlow.test.tsx
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..733c2a7
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend-admin/src/test/AuthFlow.test.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,109 @@
+import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach } from 'vitest'
+import { render, screen, fireEvent, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react'
+import App from '../App'
+import { authApi } from '@/api/auth'
+import { tokenStore } from '@/api/tokenStore'
+
+vi.mock('@/api/auth', () => ({
+  authApi: {
+    login: vi.fn(),
+    refresh: vi.fn(),
+    logout: vi.fn(),
+    getMe: vi.fn(),
+  },
+}))
+
+describe('Full Authentication Flow', () => {
+  beforeEach(() => {
+    vi.clearAllMocks()
+    tokenStore.clearToken()
+    window.history.pushState({}, '', '/')
+  })
+
+  it('redirects unauthenticated user from / to /login', async () => {
+    vi.mocked(authApi.refresh).mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error('Unauthorized'))
+
+    render(<App />)
+
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Đăng nhập/i })).toBeInTheDocument()
+    })
+    expect(screen.getByText('AuraPhone Admin')).toBeInTheDocument()
+  })
+
+  it('allows admin login, redirects to dashboard, shows admin badge and email, and supports logout', async () => {
+    vi.mocked(authApi.refresh).mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error('Unauthorized'))
+    const adminUser = { id: 'admin-1', email: 'admin@auraphone.vn', role: 'admin' }
+
+    vi.mocked(authApi.login).mockResolvedValueOnce({
+      user: adminUser,
+      accessToken: 'jwt-admin-token-123',
+    })
+    vi.mocked(authApi.logout).mockResolvedValueOnce()
+
+    render(<App />)
+
+    // Wait for login page
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Đăng nhập/i })).toBeInTheDocument()
+    })
+
+    // Fill form
+    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/Email/i), {
+      target: { value: 'admin@auraphone.vn' },
+    })
+    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/Mật khẩu/i), {
+      target: { value: 'Secret123!' },
+    })
+
+    // Submit
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Đăng nhập/i }))
+
+    // Expect dashboard shell to render with admin email and logout button
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(screen.getByText('Bảng điều khiển quản trị hệ thống AuraPhone')).toBeInTheDocument()
+    })
+    expect(screen.getAllByText('admin@auraphone.vn').length).toBeGreaterThan(0)
+    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Đăng xuất/i })).toBeInTheDocument()
+
+    // Test logout button
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Đăng xuất/i }))
+
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Đăng nhập/i })).toBeInTheDocument()
+    })
+  })
+
+  it('denies access to non-admin user with Vietnamese error message', async () => {
+    vi.mocked(authApi.refresh).mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error('Unauthorized'))
+    const customerUser = { id: 'customer-1', email: 'customer@auraphone.vn', role: 'customer' }
+
+    vi.mocked(authApi.login).mockResolvedValueOnce({
+      user: customerUser,
+      accessToken: 'jwt-customer-token',
+    })
+
+    render(<App />)
+
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Đăng nhập/i })).toBeInTheDocument()
+    })
+
+    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/Email/i), {
+      target: { value: 'customer@auraphone.vn' },
+    })
+    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/Mật khẩu/i), {
+      target: { value: 'CustomerPass123' },
+    })
+
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Đăng nhập/i }))
+
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(screen.getByText(/Tài khoản không có quyền truy cập trang quản trị/i)).toBeInTheDocument()
+    })
+
+    // Confirm we are still on the login page and not dashboard
+    expect(screen.queryByText('Bảng điều khiển quản trị hệ thống AuraPhone')).not.toBeInTheDocument()
+    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Đăng nhập/i })).toBeInTheDocument()
+  })
+})

new file mode 100644
index 0000000..e68034f

+import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest'
+import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react'
+import { MemoryRouter } from 'react-router-dom'
+import { ProductCard } from './ProductCard'
+import type { ProductSummary } from '@/api/types'
+
+describe('ProductCard', () => {
+  const dummyProduct: ProductSummary = {
+    id: 'prod-apple-1',
+    name: 'iPhone 16 Pro Max',
+    model: 'A3297',
+    brand: 'Apple',
+    category: { id: 'cat-phone', name: 'Điện thoại' },
+    minPriceVnd: '34990000',
+    maxPriceVnd: '34990000',
+    price: '34990000',
+    currency: 'VND',
+    primaryImageUrl: 'https://example.com/iphone-16.jpg',
+    inStock: true,
+  }
+
+  it('renders brand text badge and product details', () => {
+    render(
+      <MemoryRouter>
+        <ProductCard product={dummyProduct} />
+      </MemoryRouter>
+    )
+
+    expect(screen.getByText('Apple')).toBeInTheDocument()
+    expect(screen.getByText('iPhone 16 Pro Max')).toBeInTheDocument()
+    expect(screen.getByText('A3297')).toBeInTheDocument()
+    const img = screen.getByRole('img')
+    expect(img).toHaveAttribute('alt', 'Apple iPhone 16 Pro Max A3297')
+  })
+
+  it('falls back to default label when brand is empty', () => {
+    const productWithoutBrand: ProductSummary = {
+      ...dummyProduct,
+      brand: '',
+    }
+
+    render(
+      <MemoryRouter>
+        <ProductCard product={productWithoutBrand} />
+      </MemoryRouter>
+    )
+
+    expect(screen.getByText('Smartphone')).toBeInTheDocument()
+  })
+
+  it('renders "Còn hàng" badge when inStock is true', () => {
+    render(
+      <MemoryRouter>
+        <ProductCard product={{ ...dummyProduct, inStock: true }} />
+      </MemoryRouter>
+    )
+
+    expect(screen.getByText('Còn hàng')).toBeInTheDocument()
+  })
+
+  it('renders "Hết hàng" badge when inStock is false', () => {
+    render(
+      <MemoryRouter>
+        <ProductCard product={{ ...dummyProduct, inStock: false }} />
+      </MemoryRouter>
+    )
+
+    expect(screen.getByText('Hết hàng')).toBeInTheDocument()
+  })
+
+  it('renders price range when minPriceVnd and maxPriceVnd differ', () => {
+    const multiVariantProduct: ProductSummary = {
+      ...dummyProduct,
+      minPriceVnd: '25990000',
+      maxPriceVnd: '32990000',
+      price: '25990000',
+    }
+
+    render(
+      <MemoryRouter>
+        <ProductCard product={multiVariantProduct} />
+      </MemoryRouter>
+    )
+
+    expect(screen.getByText(/25\.990\.000\s*₫\s*-\s*32\.990\.000\s*₫/)).toBeInTheDocument()
+  })
+})
